### Steps
Put the steps of a flow in DialogSteps: a new step comes in from the trailing edge over the one before, which moves a third of the way out under it, as a push on iOS. direction="back" reverses it for a return to a step before. The new step starts at its top, the steps reach the panel's edges, and focus in the step that leaves moves to the one that comes in. With reduced motion the step changes at once. A step's Back is a DialogHeaderAction side="leading": on a phone it takes the place of Close in the Sheet's bar, as the back button of a screen pushed inside an iOS sheet. Each step is laid out as Apple Account asks for an address: its text, a soft capsule field and the action under it.
```vue
<Dialog v-model:open="open" @update:open="(next) => { if (!next) { step = 'email'; direction = 'forward' } }">
  <DialogTrigger>
    <Button color="secondary" variant="outline">Sign in</Button>
  </DialogTrigger>
  <DialogContent mobile-variant="sheet" :width="420">
    <DialogHeader>
      <DialogHeaderAction v-if="step === 'code'" side="leading">
        <NavigationButton action="back" variant="ghost" @click="direction = 'back'; step = 'email'" />
      </DialogHeaderAction>
      <DialogTitle>Sign in</DialogTitle>
    </DialogHeader>
    <DialogBody>
      <DialogSteps :step="step" :direction="direction">
        <div v-if="step === 'email'" class="flex flex-col gap-4 text-sm text-secondary max-md:gap-5 max-md:text-[length:var(--dialog-mobile-type-size)] max-md:leading-[var(--dialog-mobile-type-leading)]">
          <p>We send a code to this address.</p>
          <Input v-model:value="email" aria-label="Email" type="email" autocomplete="email" placeholder="Email address" variant="soft" size="3xl" pill />
        </div>
        <div v-else class="flex flex-col gap-4 text-sm text-secondary max-md:gap-5 max-md:text-[length:var(--dialog-mobile-type-size)] max-md:leading-[var(--dialog-mobile-type-leading)]">
          <p>Enter the code we sent to {{ email || 'your email' }}.</p>
          <Input aria-label="Code" inputmode="numeric" autocomplete="one-time-code" placeholder="6-digit code" variant="soft" size="3xl" pill />
        </div>
      </DialogSteps>
    </DialogBody>
    <DialogFooter>
      <Button v-if="step === 'email'" color="primary" @click="direction = 'forward'; step = 'code'">Continue</Button>
      <Button v-else color="primary" @click="open = false">Verify</Button>
    </DialogFooter>
  </DialogContent>
</Dialog>
```

### Keep it open
Escape, a press or a focus outside and, on a phone, a Sheet pulled down ask @escape-key-down and @interact-outside first: event.preventDefault() keeps the dialog open, such as for a form with unsaved changes, or for a press on a developer panel over the page. A Sheet kept open returns to its place, as an iOS sheet under isModalInPresentation. The Close button and DialogClose still close it. On a phone the form is laid out as Photos' New Album: a grouped Sheet, the name a field alone in its list with the note under it, Close to discard and the bar's checkmark (a DialogHeaderAction) to save, in place of the footer.
```vue
<Dialog v-model:open="open" @update:open="(next) => { if (!next) { profileName = 'Mia'; held = false } }">
  <DialogTrigger>
    <Button color="secondary" variant="outline">Edit profile</Button>
  </DialogTrigger>
  <DialogContent mobile-variant="sheet" surface="grouped" :width="420" @escape-key-down="(event) => { if (profileName !== 'Mia') { event.preventDefault(); held = true } }" @interact-outside="(event) => { if (profileName !== 'Mia') { event.preventDefault(); held = true } }">
    <DialogHeader>
      <DialogTitle>Edit profile</DialogTitle>
      <DialogDescription>{{ held ? 'Save or discard your changes to close.' : 'Escape or a press outside closes it until you change the name.' }}</DialogDescription>
    </DialogHeader>
    <DialogBody>
      <FieldGroup>
        <Field row label="Name">
          <Input v-model:value="profileName" placeholder="Name" @update:value="held = false" />
        </Field>
      </FieldGroup>
    </DialogBody>
    <DialogFooter>
      <DialogClose>
        <Button color="primary" variant="soft">Discard</Button>
      </DialogClose>
      <Button color="primary" @click="open = false">Save</Button>
    </DialogFooter>
  </DialogContent>
</Dialog>
```

## Empty Message
Empty state for telling users there's nothing to show, with error, warning, no-results, and onboarding compositions.
Import: import { EmptyMessage, EmptyMessageActionRow, EmptyMessageDescription, EmptyMessageIcon, EmptyMessageTitle } from "@plexui/vue/components/EmptyMessage"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/empty-message
Props:
  - fill ("static" | "absolute" | "none") default: "static" — How the container fills its parent: "static" takes full width and height, "absolute" covers a positioned parent (inset 0), "none" applies no sizing
  - size ("sm" | "md") default: "md" — sm is the compact message for a panel, a card section or an empty list
  - align ("center" | "start") default: "center" — start left-aligns the message, for an empty state under a section heading
  EmptyMessageIcon:
    - color ("secondary" | "danger" | "warning") default: "secondary" — Semantic color
    - size ("sm" | "md") default: "md" — Icon badge size
  EmptyMessageTitle:
    - color ("secondary" | "danger" | "warning") default: "secondary" — Semantic color
  EmptyMessageActionRow:
    - class (string) — Additional classes for the action row container

### Overview
An empty message is made of parts, each optional: an icon, a title, a description and an action row. It needs a title or a description, so this preview offers description with a title; the description takes text or nodes, such as a search term in font-semibold. color on EmptyMessageIcon and on EmptyMessageTitle shows the intent of the message, such as danger on both for an error (a missing permission) or warning on the icon for a notice (a feature in beta); the description keeps its own color. fill sizes the message to its parent: static takes the full width and height, absolute covers a positioned parent, and none applies no sizing, for a container that sets the height (a list with no results). Use size="sm" for an empty panel, card section or list: a smaller title, tighter spacing, no minimum width and a small icon badge; in a message of the default size the icon takes its own size. align="start" left-aligns the message, for an empty state under a section heading: a single line such as "No tags added" reads with the heading above it. The action row takes buttons, or a TextLink with class="text-sm" on the row. On a phone a message of the default size takes the shape of the kit's empty state: the symbol in a 64px slot, a 22px bold title over a 17px description, and its buttons 24px under the text, one at its own width and two across the width one under the other. A message that fills a screen puts its buttons across the screen at its bottom, as iOS does, the main one on top, and centres the text in the space above them; a second action reads best as a plain button.
```vue
<div
  class="flex w-full flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center"
  :style="fill === 'absolute' ? { position: 'relative', minHeight: '200px' } : undefined"
>
  <EmptyMessage :fill="fill" :size="size" :align="align">
    <EmptyMessageIcon v-if="icon" :color="iconColor" :size="iconSize">
      <Explore />
    </EmptyMessageIcon>
    <EmptyMessageTitle v-if="title" :color="titleColor">No projects yet</EmptyMessageTitle>
    <EmptyMessageDescription v-if="description || !title">
      Create your first project to get started
    </EmptyMessageDescription>
    <EmptyMessageActionRow v-if="actions === 'link'" class="text-sm">
      <TextLink href="#">Learn more <ArrowUpRight /></TextLink>
    </EmptyMessageActionRow>
    <EmptyMessageActionRow v-else-if="actions !== 'none'">
      <Button color="primary" :size="size === 'sm' ? 'md' : 'xl'">
        <Plus class="mr-[-2px]" />
        New project
      </Button>
      <Button v-if="actions === 'two'" color="primary" variant="ghost" :size="size === 'sm' ? 'md' : 'xl'">Browse templates</Button>
    </EmptyMessageActionRow>
  </EmptyMessage>
</div>
```

## Field
Form field wrapper with a label, helper text, an error message, and automatic accessibility linking to its control.
Import: import { Field } from "@plexui/vue/components/Field"
Import: import { FieldGroup } from "@plexui/vue/components/FieldGroup"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/field
Props:
  - label (string) — Label text for the field
  - hide-label (boolean) default: false — Hides the label from view; it still names the control. A description moves under the control. On a row the value or placeholder starts at the row's edge, so the row is vertical unless orientation says otherwise
  - label slot (VNode[]) — Rich label content; takes precedence over the label prop
  - description (string) — Helper text below the label; linked via aria-describedby (or the description slot)
  - error-message (string) — Error message below the control; sets aria-invalid on the child (or the errorMessage slot)
  - label-action (string) — Content at the end of the label row, such as a link that acts on the field (or the labelAction slot)
  - size ("3xs" | "2xs" | "xs" | "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl" | "2xl" | "3xl") default: child's size, else "md" — Label font size; defaults to the child control's size
  - required (boolean) default: false — Shows a required asterisk after the label (visual only)
  - orientation ("vertical" | "horizontal") default: "horizontal" on a row with a visible label, else "vertical" — Layout direction of label and control
  - row (boolean) default: false — One line of a FieldGroup. Clicking its non-interactive area focuses the text editor or activates the choice or popup control. Other attributes reach the root only on a row
  - fill (boolean) default: false — On a row, the control takes the rest of the line after the label
  - #action (slot) — On a fill row, a control at the end of the row, such as a button that adds to a list. It stays on the list's first line, and on a phone it stands beside the label with the list under it
  - optically-align ("start" | "end") — Applies optical alignment to the child control via negative margin using its gutter
  - id (string) — Override the generated id used for the label's for and aria linking. A child's own id wins: it stays, and the label's for follows it
  - class (string) — CSS classes applied to the root element
  - default slot ((fieldProps) => VNode[]) — Form control; its single root element receives id, aria-describedby and aria-invalid (plus aria-labelledby unless it has its own name) unless the slot reads fieldProps, which include aria-labelledby
  FieldGroup:
    - title (string) — Heading over the block (or the title slot); it names the group
    - header-prominence ("standard" | "increased") default: "standard" — How much the title stands out: a quiet heading over the rows, or a title that leads its content (on a phone the iOS prominent list header, 20pt Semibold)
    - description (string) — Line under the title (or the description slot)
    - columns (boolean) default: false — Every value starts at one column, --field-row-column-width into the row, on the web and on a phone. Off, values end at the row's edge
    - bodyClass (string) — Applied to the block that holds the rows
    - default slot (VNode) — The rows
    - action slot (VNode) — Content at the end of the header. On a phone a ghost text button reads as the list header's action: tinted text on the title's last line
    - footer slot (VNode) — Content under the block, such as a note; its paragraphs stand apart

### Overview
Field wraps a form control with a label, a description and an error message, and links them: the label's for, aria-describedby and aria-invalid reach the control. required adds an asterisk after the label; it is visual only, so give the control its own required for form validation. The layout control puts the label in its four places: above: the default, orientation="vertical". columns: orientation="horizontal", every value starting at one column. end: row, one line of a FieldGroup with the value at its end. A framed field is a pill there and takes --field-row-control-width, as its width cannot follow what is typed; :pill="false" on the control keeps the rounded box. hidden: hide-label. The label still names the control for screen readers; the placeholder shows what to type, and a description moves under the field. On Phone a field is always a cell of a FieldGroup on the grouped background, as in an iOS form, in the same four places: the label above its value (row orientation="vertical"), values in one column (FieldGroup columns), the value at the end of the row, or the placeholder alone (row hide-label), its value starting at the row's edge. The row is the field's frame, so a framed or soft field loses its frame. The error stands inside the cell, under the value. The words that explain a field go in the group's footer slot, and a required field says so in its placeholder, as iOS account forms do. A disabled field dims its label with its value. A one-time code is the exception: OTPInput keeps its own digit cells on the screen, outside a group, as in Verify email.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<Field label="Name" required>
  <Input placeholder="Jane Appleseed" />
</Field>
<Field
  label="Email"
  required
  description="We'll never share your email with anyone else."
  error-message="Enter a valid email address."
>
  <Input type="email" default-value="jane@example" />
</Field>

<!-- Phone: a cell of a FieldGroup; the words that explain it go in the footer. -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Name" orientation="vertical">
    <Input placeholder="Required" />
  </Field>
  <Field row label="Email" orientation="vertical" error-message="Enter a valid email address.">
    <Input type="email" default-value="jane@example" />
  </Field>
  <template #footer>We'll never share your email with anyone else.</template>
</FieldGroup>

<!-- columns: <FieldGroup columns> with rows; end: rows; hidden: placeholder only -->
<Field row hide-label label="Name">
  <Input placeholder="Name" />
</Field>
```

### Group
FieldGroup holds the rows and draws the hairlines between them. Its title names the group, description stands under the title, the action slot at the end of the header, and the footer slot under the rows; paragraphs of a footer stand apart. columns starts every value at one column, --field-row-column-width into the row. header-prominence="increased" makes the title lead its content, as a section of a page. On Phone the standard title is the quiet heading of an iOS list, and the increased one is the list's prominent header, 20pt Semibold in the primary label. A ghost text button in the header is the list header's action: tinted text on the title's last line, with a 44pt target. The footer is 13/16 Footnote, its paragraphs one empty line apart, as in Settings.
```vue
<FieldGroup title="Billing address" :header-prominence="headerProminence" :columns="columns">
  <template v-if="action" #action>
    <Button color="primary" variant="ghost" size="sm" @click="street = ''; city = ''; postcode = ''">Clear</Button>
  </template>
  <Field row label="Street">
    <Input placeholder="Required" v-model:value="street" autocomplete="address-line1" />
  </Field>
  <Field row label="City">
    <Input placeholder="Required" v-model:value="city" autocomplete="address-level2" />
  </Field>
  <Field row label="Postcode">
    <Input placeholder="Required" v-model:value="postcode" autocomplete="postal-code" />
  </Field>
  <template v-if="footer" #footer>
    <p>Your bank checks this address against your card. <TextLink href="#group">Learn more</TextLink></p>
  </template>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Other controls
Field works with any form control. A native one, such as a Textarea, is named by the label's for. A Select or DatePicker trigger is not a native form control, so Field points its aria-labelledby at the label, and the trigger adds its value: a screen reader hears "Role, Editor". A child with a name of its own (aria-label, aria-labelledby or label) keeps it. On Phone a Select or a date is the value at the end of its row, and a long text stands under its label. A group of checkboxes, radios or switches is a row per choice: see Checkbox, Radio Group and Switch.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="flex w-full max-w-[320px] flex-col gap-4">
  <Field label="Role" description="What this person can change.">
    <Select v-model:value="role" :options="roles" />
  </Field>
  <Field label="Starts">
    <DatePicker v-model:value="start" placeholder="Select date" />
  </Field>
  <Field label="Notes">
    <Textarea placeholder="Anything the team should know" :rows="3" />
  </Field>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Role">
    <Select v-model:value="role" :options="roles" />
  </Field>
  <Field row label="Starts">
    <DatePicker v-model:value="start" trigger-date-format="MMM d, yyyy" :trigger-show-icon="false" placeholder="Select date" />
  </Field>
  <Field row label="Notes" orientation="vertical">
    <Textarea placeholder="Anything the team should know" :rows="3" auto-resize />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Label action
Use the labelAction slot (or the label-action string prop) for a link or a small control that acts on the field, at the end of the label row: a password reset, a template switch, a "Replace" link. It takes the label's font size. On Phone a row has no label line to hold it: the link stands under the group in the rows' text, as under the Apple Account sign-in. A group's footer is a footnote, for words to read.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Field label="Password">
    <template #labelAction>
      <TextLink href="#label-action">Forgot password?</TextLink>
    </template>
    <Input type="password" placeholder="Enter password" autocomplete="current-password" />
  </Field>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<div class="flex flex-col">
  <FieldGroup>
    <Field row label="Password">
      <Input type="password" placeholder="Required" autocomplete="current-password" />
    </Field>
  </FieldGroup>
  <TextLink primary href="#label-action" class="mt-[15px] self-start text-[length:var(--field-row-label-font-size)] leading-[var(--field-row-label-line-height)]">
    Forgot password?
  </TextLink>
</div>
```

### Several controls
For more than one control, or a control that needs its props placed by hand, read the default slot's props (v-slot="fieldProps"): {'{ id, "aria-labelledby", "aria-describedby", "aria-invalid" }'}. A slot that reads them renders its content as it is, so you bind them where they belong: put the id and aria-labelledby on the first control, so the label names it, and give the others an aria-label of their own. On a phone a birthday is one row and one value, whose wheel sets the day, the month and the year, as in Contacts. PlexUI has no such wheel, so this example has no Phone view.
```vue
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Field label="Birthday" v-slot="fieldProps">
    <div class="flex gap-2">
      <div class="w-[130px]">
        <Select :id="fieldProps.id" :aria-labelledby="fieldProps['aria-labelledby']" v-model:value="month" :options="months" placeholder="Month" />
      </div>
      <div class="w-[84px]">
        <Select v-model:value="day" :options="days" placeholder="Day" aria-label="Day" />
      </div>
      <div class="w-[96px]">
        <Select v-model:value="year" :options="years" placeholder="Year" aria-label="Year" />
      </div>
    </div>
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Sizing
The label and description scale with the child control: Field reads the control's size, so {''} gets a lg label without repeating it. Pass size to Field to set the label size yourself, or when the slot reads the field props. On a phone a row sets the field's height, insets and type, so this example has no Phone view. Outside a row, with touch.css a text field is 44px from 3xs to 2xl and 50px at 3xl, with the 17px text of iOS.
```vue
<div class="w-full max-w-[320px]">
  <Field
    label="Email"
    description="We'll never share your email with anyone else."
    error-message="Enter a valid email address."
    required
  >
    <Input type="email" placeholder="name@example.com" :size="size" :pill="pill" />
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Optical alignment
Use optically-align="start" to apply a negative margin on the child control, aligning its text with the surrounding content. Works with pill inputs. The label, description, and error message stay at the container edge while the control extends past it. A phone row has no frame to align, so this example has no Phone view.
```vue
<div class="w-full min-w-0 max-w-[400px]">
  <div class="border border-dashed border-alpha/20 rounded-md py-6 px-8">
    <div class="mb-5 text-secondary text-sm">
      {{ opticallyAlign ? 'optically-align="start"' : 'Default' }}
    </div>
    <div class="flex flex-col gap-5">
      <Field label="Full name" :optically-align="opticallyAlign ? 'start' : undefined">
        <Input placeholder="Jane Doe" :pill="pill" />
      </Field>
      <Field
        label="Email"
        description="We'll never share your email."
        error-message="Enter a valid email address."
        required
        :optically-align="opticallyAlign ? 'start' : undefined"
      >
        <Input placeholder="jane@example.com" :pill="pill" />
      </Field>
    </div>
    <Button class="mt-6" color="primary" variant="soft" :pill="pill">Submit</Button>
  </div>
</div>
```

## File Upload
Mobile-first file upload with MIME-type and size validation, multi-file selection, error states, and read-only file cards.
Import: import { FileUpload, FileCard, FileCardGroup } from "@plexui/vue/components/FileUpload"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/file-upload
Props:
  - variant ("compact" | "area") default: "compact" — Visual variant: compact button or larger tappable area
  - label (string) — Button label text
  - description (string) — Helper text below the icon (area variant only)
  - accept (string) — Accepted file types (e.g. ".pdf,.png,image/*")
  - multiple (boolean) default: false — Allow selecting multiple files. Without it, a new pick replaces the current file
  - max-files (number) — Maximum number of files. In multiple mode, the button disables at the limit. Single-file mode keeps the button available for replacement.
  - max-size (number) — Maximum file size in bytes
  - value / v-model:value (File[]) — Controlled file list; `update:value` fires when files are added or removed
  - @file-reject ((file: File, reason: string) => void) — Fires when a file is rejected (wrong type, too large, over limit)
  - error-message (string) — Error message; sets the invalid visual state
  - invalid (boolean) default: false — Invalid state (auto-set when error-message is provided)
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable all interactions
  - id (string) — Id of the hidden file input
  - class (string) — Additional CSS class
  FileCard:
    - file (File) — The file to display
    - @remove (() => void) — Remove callback. When omitted, the delete button is hidden.
    - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable the remove button
    - class (string) — Additional CSS class
  FileCardGroup:
    - default slot (VNode[]) — FileCard elements
    - class (string) — Additional CSS class

### Overview
The example starts with three files: the list stands under the button, each file with a type icon, its name truncated in the middle, its size and a remove button. Toggle errorMessage and disabled while keeping the selected files. The error control demonstrates a supplied message; it does not validate the number of files. Accepted types and size limits still apply.
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref } from 'vue';
import { FileUpload } from '@plexui/vue/components/FileUpload';

const files = ref<File[]>(initialFiles);
const errorMessage = ref(false);
const disabled = ref(false);
</script>

<template>
  <FileUpload
    v-model:value="files"
    accept=".pdf,.png,.jpg,.jpeg"
    multiple
    :max-files="5"
    :max-size="10 * 1024 * 1024"
    :error-message="errorMessage ? 'Please upload at least 2 documents.' : undefined"
    :disabled="disabled"
  />
</template>
```

### File list
One file is a list of one. Turn off onRemove for read-only cards. After keyboard removal, focus moves to the next file, then the previous one, or the Files removed status when the list becomes empty, without scrolling. Pointer removal does not move focus if the remove button was not focused.
```vue
<script setup>
import { ref } from 'vue';

function removeFileWithFocus(root, index, remove) {
  const buttons = Array.from(root?.querySelectorAll('button:not(:disabled)') ?? []);
  const source = buttons[index];
  const target = buttons[index + 1] ?? buttons[index - 1] ?? root?.querySelector('[data-file-removal-status]');
  const doc = source?.ownerDocument;
  const restore = source !== undefined && doc?.activeElement === source;
  remove();
  if (!restore || !source || !doc || !(target instanceof HTMLElement)) return;
  queueMicrotask(() => {
    if (!source.isConnected && target.isConnected && (doc.activeElement === doc.body || doc.activeElement === source)) {
      target.focus({ preventScroll: true });
    }
  });
}

const files = ref(initialFiles);
const removable = ref(true);
</script>

<template>
<div ref="fileCards" class="w-[360px]">
  <FileCardGroup v-if="files.length > 0">
    <FileCard
      v-for="(file, index) in files"
      :key="file.name + '-' + file.size"
      :file="file"
      :on-remove="removable ? () => removeFileWithFocus($refs.fileCards, index, () => { files = files.filter((_, i) => i !== index) }) : undefined"
    />
  </FileCardGroup>
  <div role="status" tabindex="-1" data-file-removal-status :class="files.length > 0 ? 'sr-only' : 'text-center text-sm text-tertiary'">
    {{ files.length > 0 ? '' : 'Files removed' }}
  </div>
</div>
</template>
```

## Float Input
Text input whose label floats above the value, with error and helper text, a clear button, a password reveal toggle, and a multi-line variant.
Import: import { FloatingLabelInput } from "@plexui/vue/components/FloatingLabelInput"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/floating-label-input
  FloatingLabelInput:
    - label (string) — Label text for the floating label
    - value / v-model:value (string) — Controlled value; `update:value` fires on input
    - default-value (string) — Initial value when uncontrolled
    - error-message (string) — Error message to display below the input
    - invalid (boolean) default: false (true if error-message) — Mark the input as invalid
    - description (string | slot) — Helper text below the input
    - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disables the input
    - read-only / readonly (boolean) default: false — Makes the input read-only (both spellings work)
    - type (string) default: "text" — Input type; password gets a reveal toggle
    - revealable (boolean) default: true for password without an end adornment — Built-in reveal toggle
    - multiline (boolean) default: false — Render an auto-growing textarea
    - rows (number) default: 2 — Visible lines when multiline
    - label-placement ("border" | "inside") default: "border" — Where the label rests once it floats. inside keeps it in the box, with the value under it, and always takes the rounded box
    - pill (boolean) default: true — Pill shape; false takes the rounded box of the other fields
    - allow-autofill-extensions (boolean) default: false — Let password-manager extensions decorate the input
    - #startAdornment / #endAdornment (slots) — Content before or after the input (camelCase slot names). A start adornment is always shown and keeps the label floated
    - @clear (() => void) — Shows the clear button and fires when it is clicked; focus returns to the input
    - @revealed-change ((revealed: boolean) => void) — Fires when the reveal toggle is clicked
    - @autofill (() => void) — Fires when the browser autofills the input
  FloatingLabelSelect:
    - label (string) — Label text for the floating label
    - selected (boolean) default: false — Whether a value is selected: floats the label and shows the default slot
    - open (boolean) default: false — Whether the attached list is open
    - error-message (string) — Error message to display below the field
    - invalid (boolean) default: false (true if error-message) — Mark the field as invalid
    - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disables the field
    - label-placement ("border" | "inside") default: "border" — Where the label rests once it floats. inside keeps it in the box, with the value under it, and always takes the rounded box
    - pill (boolean) default: true — Pill shape; false takes the rounded box of the other fields
    - @clear-click (() => void) — Shows a clear button when a value is selected
    - @interact (() => void) — Fires on pointer down and on the keys that open the field. Without it those keys click the field, so a wrapping trigger opens

### Overview
Two things set the type: where the label rests (label-placement: on the border, or inside the box with the value under it), and the shape of the field. An empty field shows the label as a placeholder, so the difference appears once it has a value or the focus. :pill="false" takes the rounded box of Input and Select with the label on the border; inside always takes the rounded box, so pill is offered only with the label on the border. Text fields, textareas and selects take both settings the same way. Both floating fields follow the same two tokens, so a form can set its radius and gutter in one place. Choose Empty or Filled to replace the value with a preset. The other controls preserve your text. Invalid and errorMessage demonstrate visual states, not email validation. An error message also marks the field invalid, so invalid is offered only without one; when both messages are enabled, the error appears before the description. Read-only text can still be selected and copied. onClear shows a built-in clear button: it appears when the field has a value and is not disabled or read-only, and focus returns to the field. Listen to @clear, or bind :on-clear as here to switch it on and off. On Phone the field is a cell named by its placeholder: the label does not float and the cell is the frame, so label-placement and pill are desktop settings. The description goes in the group's footer and the error stands inside the cell. A cell has no frame to color, so invalid alone is desktop only.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-full min-w-0 max-w-[360px]">
  <FloatingLabelInput
    v-model:value="value"
    label="Email address"
    :label-placement="labelPlacement"
    :pill="pill"
    :invalid="invalid || errorMessage"
    :error-message="errorMessage ? 'Email is not valid.' : undefined"
    :description="description ? `We'll never share your email with anyone else.` : undefined"
    :disabled="disabled"
    :read-only="readOnly"
    :on-clear="clearable ? () => (value = '') : undefined"
  />
</div>

<!-- Phone: a cell of a FieldGroup; the description goes in the footer, the error stands inside the cell -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row hide-label label="Email address" :error-message="errorMessage ? 'Email is not valid.' : undefined">
    <Input
      v-model:value="value"
      type="email"
      placeholder="Email address"
      :invalid="errorMessage"
      :disabled="disabled"
      :read-only="readOnly"
      :on-clear="clearable ? () => (value = '') : undefined"
    />
  </Field>
  <template v-if="description" #footer>
    <p>We'll never share your email with anyone else.</p>
  </template>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Textarea
Use multiline to render an auto-growing textarea with the same floating-label treatment. The textarea starts at 2 rows and grows automatically as content is added. The border radius adjusts to half the input height. On Phone the cell grows with a Textarea that starts at one line.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FloatingLabelInput
    :key="String(multiline)"
    v-model:value="value"
    label="Message"
    :multiline="multiline"
  />
</div>

<!-- Phone: the cell grows with the text -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row hide-label label="Message">
    <Textarea v-if="multiline" v-model:value="value" placeholder="Message" :rows="1" auto-resize />
    <Input v-else v-model:value="value" placeholder="Message" />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Password input
A type="password" field ships a built-in reveal toggle: click the eye to show or hide the value. Set revealable to false to remove it. On Phone the cell holds an Input with an InputAdornmentButton at its end, as on the Input page.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <FloatingLabelInput label="Password" type="password" />
</div>

<!-- Phone: the reveal button stands at the end of the cell -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row hide-label label="Password">
    <Input placeholder="Password" :type="visible ? 'text' : 'password'">
      <template #endAdornment>
        <InputAdornmentButton :aria-label="visible ? 'Hide' : 'Show'" :aria-pressed="visible" @click="visible = !visible">
          <EyeOff v-if="visible" />
          <Eye v-else />
        </InputAdornmentButton>
      </template>
    </Input>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Phone input
A country code selector paired with a FloatingLabelInput that uses the startAdornment slot to show the dial code as a non-editable prefix. The prefix is always shown, and it keeps the label floated. On Phone the country and the number are two linked rows of one group, as in the Sign up (phone) form, so this example has no Phone view.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px] flex flex-col gap-3">
  <Select
    v-model:value="country"
    :options="phoneCountryOptions"
    search-placeholder="Search countries..."
    :search-predicate="phoneCountrySearchPredicate"
    :list-min-width="360"
    block
    size="3xl"
    check-position="end"
    @change="focusLater($refs.phoneInput)"
  >
    <template #optionView="{ value, label }">
      <span :class="countryLabelStyles.Root"><component :is="FlagIcon" :code="value" /><span :class="countryLabelStyles.Text">{{ label }}</span></span>
    </template>
    <template #trigger="{ open, onToggle }">
      <component :is="PhoneCountryTrigger" :country="country" :open="open" @click="onToggle" />
    </template>
  </Select>
  <FloatingLabelInput
    ref="phoneInput"
    label="Phone number"
    :value="phone"
    @update:value="(raw) => (phone = formatPhoneNumber(raw.replace(/\D/g, ''), country))"
    type="tel"
    inputmode="tel"
  >
    <template #startAdornment>
      <span class="text-tertiary whitespace-nowrap select-none mr-2">{{ dialCodeOf(country) }}</span>
    </template>
  </FloatingLabelInput>
</div>
```

### Select
Use FloatingLabelSelect for plain text dropdowns. The label floats up when a value is selected, matching FloatingLabelInput styling. On Phone the cell holds a plain Select named by its placeholder.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <Select
    v-model:value="role"
    :options="roleOptions"
    search-placeholder="Search roles..."
    :list-min-width="360"
    block
    check-position="end"
  >
    <template #trigger="{ open }">
      <FloatingLabelSelect
        label="Role"
        :selected="!!role"
        :open="open"
        :on-clear-click="role ? () => (role = '') : undefined"
      >
        {{ roleOptions.find((option) => option.value === role)?.label }}
      </FloatingLabelSelect>
    </template>
  </Select>
</div>

<!-- Phone: a cell named by the placeholder -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row hide-label label="Role">
    <Select v-model:value="role" :options="roleOptions" placeholder="Role" block />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Multiple selection
Pair FloatingLabelSelect with a multiple Select to collect several values while keeping the floating-label treatment. The trigger shows the selected count and exposes a clear button that resets the whole list. On Phone this is the cell of the Select example with a multiple Select, so it has no Phone view.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <Select
    multiple
    clearable
    v-model:value="skills"
    :options="skillOptions"
    search-placeholder="Search skills..."
    :list-min-width="360"
    block
    check-position="end"
  >
    <template #trigger="{ open }">
      <FloatingLabelSelect
        label="Skills"
        :selected="skills.length > 0"
        :open="open"
        :on-clear-click="skills.length > 0 ? () => (skills = []) : undefined"
      >
        {{
          skills.length === 1
            ? skillOptions.find((option) => option.value === skills[0])?.label
            : skills.length > 1
              ? skills.length + ' skills'
              : ''
        }}
      </FloatingLabelSelect>
    </template>
  </Select>
</div>
```

### Country select
Use FloatingLabelSelect alongside FloatingLabelInput for consistent form styling. The select trigger shares the same floating-label visual shell. The label floats up when a value is selected. On Phone this is the cell of the Select example with a flag in its options, so it has no Phone view.
```vue
<div class="w-[360px]">
  <Select
    v-model:value="country"
    :options="countries"
    search-placeholder="Search countries..."
    :list-min-width="360"
    block
    check-position="end"
  >
    <template #optionView="{ value, label }">
      <span :class="countryLabelStyles.Root"><component :is="FlagIcon" :code="value" /><span :class="countryLabelStyles.Text">{{ label }}</span></span>
    </template>
    <template #trigger="{ open }">
      <FloatingLabelSelect
        label="Country"
        :selected="!!countryOf(country)"
        :open="open"
        :on-clear-click="countryOf(country) ? () => (country = '') : undefined"
      >
        <span
          v-if="countryOf(country)"
          :class="countryLabelStyles.Root"
        ><component :is="FlagIcon" :code="country" /><span :class="countryLabelStyles.Text">{{ countryOf(country).label }}</span></span>
      </FloatingLabelSelect>
    </template>
  </Select>
</div>
```

### Label inside
Use label-placement="inside" on FloatingLabelInput and FloatingLabelSelect for long forms such as an address: the label stays inside the box, with the value under it. It works with multiline, adornments, errors and the clear button, and always takes the rounded box. On Phone a cell names its field with the placeholder, so this example has no Phone view.
```vue
<div class="flex w-[360px] flex-col gap-3">
  <FloatingLabelInput label="Full name" label-placement="inside" default-value="Jane Doe" />
  <Select
    v-model:value="country"
    :options="countries"
    search-placeholder="Search countries..."
    :list-min-width="360"
    block
    check-position="end"
  >
    <template #trigger="{ open }">
      <FloatingLabelSelect
        label="Country or region"
        label-placement="inside"
        :selected="!!country"
        :open="open"
      >
        {{ countries.find((option) => option.value === country)?.label }}
      </FloatingLabelSelect>
    </template>
  </Select>
  <FloatingLabelInput label="Address line 1" label-placement="inside" />
  <FloatingLabelInput label="Delivery notes" label-placement="inside" multiline />
</div>
```

## Index Bar
"The section index of a phone list: a column of letters at the trailing edge that jumps to a section under the finger, as the iOS section index does. Desktop lists have no index, so an index bar never renders from 768px up."
Import: import { IndexBar } from "@plexui/vue/components/IndexBar"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/index-bar
Props:
  - items (string[]) — The titles of the sections, in order: usually letters, such as A to Z and #
  - id (string) — The id of the index bar
  - aria-label (string) default: "Section index" — Name of the index bar
  - aria-controls (string) — The id of the list the index scrolls

### Overview
Put the bar in a Page, or in any positioned container that holds the list: it runs down the trailing edge, centred between the bars of a Page (or the safe areas), as iOS centres its index, its letters semibold, 13.79px apart and centred about 7.4px from the edge, where iOS 27.2 centres its own, in the info color, as iOS 27.2 draws its index and its tint. A drag highlights nothing, as on iOS. The rows keep the margins of the list, and the letters sit in the trailing one, as in Contacts. A press shows the section of the letter under the finger, and a drag moves through the sections letter by letter; the select event receives the title and its index, and the list scrolls itself there. Where the letters do not fit, as in a short sheet, every other letter gives way to a dot, as UIKit&apos;s index does, and a drag over a dot still reaches the letters behind it.
```vue
<Page surface="grouped">
  <Toolbar>
    <ToolbarTitle>Countries</ToolbarTitle>
  </Toolbar>
  <PageContent ref="content">
    <FieldGroup v-for="section in sections" :key="section.letter" :title="section.letter" :data-section="section.letter">
      ...
    </FieldGroup>
  </PageContent>
  <IndexBar :items="sections.map((section) => section.letter)" @select="(letter) => scrollToSection(content, letter)" />
</Page>
```

## Indicators
Loading and progress indicators, from circular spinners and determinate progress rings to animated dots, with configurable color and stroke.
Import: import { LoadingIndicator, CircularProgress, LoadingDots } from "@plexui/vue/components/Indicator"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/indicators
  LoadingIndicator:
    - size (number | string) default: "1em" — Size in px or any CSS length. Defaults to 20px with the optional touch stylesheet, also inside a field. Also accepts --indicator-size.
    - stroke-width (number) default: 2 — Ring stroke or mobile spoke width in px. Mobile spokes default to the iOS 27 kit proportion: 2/14 of the size up to 16px, 4/30 to 27px, 5/35 from 28px.
  CircularProgress:
    - progress (number) — Static progress 0-100 (disables the simulated animation)
    - done (boolean) default: false — Fills the ring and ends the simulated progress
    - max-duration (number) default: 15000 — Duration of the simulated progress in ms
    - size (number | string) default: "28px" — Diameter in px or any CSS length
    - stroke-width (number) default: 2 — Stroke thickness in px, capped at 4
    - track-color (string) — Track color (CSS variable or value)
    - track-active-color (string) — Active segment color

### Overview
indicator picks one of the three. LoadingIndicator is a spinner for general loading states. It takes the surrounding font-size and text color by default; size and stroke-width set its size and stroke in pixels, a text class such as text-danger (or --indicator-color) colors it, and --indicator-rotate-duration sets the time of one turn. On a desktop it is a turning ring. With the optional touch stylesheet it is the iOS 27 spinner of eight rounded spokes: 20px by default, also inside a field (an Input adornment, an InputGroup addon or a SelectControl, through --control-indicator-size); up to 16px it takes the Small spokes and from 28px the Large ones. A stroke-width replaces the spokes' widths, so the Phone view leaves it unset. CircularProgress is a progress ring. Without progress it simulates progress over max-duration milliseconds, and done fills the ring and ends the simulation; this preview sets it every 4 seconds to start over. progress, from 0 to 100, shows a fixed value instead. size and stroke-width (at most 4) size the ring, and track-color and track-active-color color its track and its progress. LoadingDots shows typing progress with staggered pulsing dots in the current text color.
```vue
<div v-if="indicator === 'LoadingIndicator'" :style="{ '--indicator-rotate-duration': duration + 'ms' }">
  <LoadingIndicator :size="size" :stroke-width="strokeWidth" :class="color || undefined" />
</div>
<CircularProgress
  v-else-if="indicator === 'CircularProgress'"
  :size="size"
  :stroke-width="strokeWidth"
  :progress="simulated ? undefined : progress"
  :max-duration="maxDuration"
  :done="done"
  :track-color="trackColor || undefined"
  :track-active-color="trackActiveColor || undefined"
/>
<LoadingDots v-else :class="color || undefined" />
```

## Input
Text input with start and end adornments, a clear button, a password visibility toggle, and presets for SSN and birthday.
Import: import { Input, InputAdornmentButton } from "@plexui/vue/components/Input"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/input
Props:
  - value / v-model:value (string | number) — Controlled value; `update:value` fires on every input
  - default-value (string | number) — Initial value when uncontrolled
  - variant ("outline" | "soft" | "ghost") default: "outline" — Visual style of the input; ghost has no border and no fill
  - size ("3xs" | "2xs" | "xs" | "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl" | "2xl" | "3xl") default: "md" — Controls the size of the input
  - gutter-size (string) — Controls gutter on the edges; defaults to value from size
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disables the input visually and from interactions
  - read-only / readonly (boolean) default: false — Makes the input read-only and hides the clear button
  - invalid (boolean) default: false — Mark the input as invalid
  - allow-autofill-extensions (boolean) default: false (true with name or type="password") — Allow autofill extensions to appear in the input
  - auto-select (boolean) default: false — Select all contents of the input when mounted
  - startAdornment / endAdornment (slots) — Content rendered at the start or end of the input
  - @clear ((event: MouseEvent) => void) — When set, displays a clear button that appears when the input has a value
  - @autofill (() => void) — Fires when the browser autofills the input
  - pill (boolean) default: false — Fully rounded pill shape
  - optically-align ("start" | "end") — Applies a negative margin using the current gutter to optically align with surrounding content
  InputAdornmentButton:
    - color (SemanticColor) — Tint of the pressed (aria-pressed="true") state; the neutral primary-soft tint when unset
    - type (string) default: "button" — Button type

### Overview
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Input placeholder="Enter text..." />
</div>

<!-- Phone: the input is the value of a row. -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Email">
    <Input type="email" placeholder="name@example.com" />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Variants
Use variant to stylize the input. ghost has no border and no fill, for a field its container already frames, such as the search line at the top of a dialog; it still turns red when invalid. The state control shows each variant empty, with a value, invalid and disabled. You decide how the invalid state is removed, whether that's through @focus, @update:value, a submit handler, etc.; [data-invalid] is applied to the container for custom styling. disabled greys the input out and prevents user interactions. On Phone the row is the frame, and the value stands in one of three places: at the end of the row, at one column for the whole group with FieldGroup columns, or under the label with orientation="vertical". A row has no frame to color, so an error is the row's error-message under it.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2 w-[320px]">
  <Input
    v-for="variant in ['outline', 'soft', 'ghost']"
    :key="variant + state"
    type="email"
    :variant="variant"
    placeholder="name@example.com"
    :default-value="state === 'empty' ? undefined : state === 'invalid' ? 'jane@example' : 'jane@example.com'"
    :invalid="state === 'invalid'"
    :disabled="state === 'disabled'"
  />
</div>

<!-- Phone: the value at the end of the row, at one column, or under the label. -->
<FieldGroup
  v-for="place in [
    { title: 'Value at the end', columns: false, vertical: false },
    { title: 'Values in a column', columns: true, vertical: false },
    { title: 'Value under the label', columns: false, vertical: true },
  ]"
  :key="place.title"
  :title="place.title"
  :columns="place.columns"
>
  <Field row :orientation="place.vertical ? 'vertical' : undefined" label="Name">
    <Input
      :key="state"
      :variant="place.vertical ? 'ghost' : undefined"
      placeholder="Required"
      :default-value="state === 'empty' ? undefined : 'Jane Appleseed'"
      :disabled="state === 'disabled'"
    />
  </Field>
  <Field
    row
    :orientation="place.vertical ? 'vertical' : undefined"
    label="Email"
    :error-message="state === 'invalid' ? 'Enter a valid email address.' : undefined"
  >
    <Input
      :key="state"
      type="email"
      :variant="place.vertical ? 'ghost' : undefined"
      placeholder="name@example.com"
      :default-value="state === 'empty' ? undefined : state === 'invalid' ? 'jane@example' : 'jane@example.com'"
      :invalid="state === 'invalid'"
      :disabled="state === 'disabled'"
    />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Adornments
Add arbitrary content to the beginning of an input with the startAdornment slot, like a search icon, or to its end with the endAdornment slot, like a character counter or custom icon; position switches between the two. Ideally, the spacing will look pretty good by default. If you need to customize the spacing: gutter-size determines the amount of spacing on the edges of the container, which you can fine-tune as needed (if you need precise spacing not supported by gutter-size, use a negative margin on your adornment directly, e.g. -ml-2). The spacing between the adornment and the input is controlled with gap on the container, which you can customize as needed with Tailwind classes. On Phone an Input in a Toolbar takes the bar's search field, and one with type="search" at the top of the content is the same glass field under a large title; in a row a start adornment is a prefix of the value, such as a currency, and trailing text-secondary text takes the row's accessory color.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Input v-if="position === 'start'" placeholder="Search">
    <template #startAdornment>
      <Search class="fill-tertiary" />
    </template>
  </Input>
  <Input v-else v-model:value="username" placeholder="Username" maxlength="16">
    <template #endAdornment>
      <span class="mt-[1.5px] tabular-nums text-tertiary text-[.875em]">{{ username.length }}/16</span>
    </template>
  </Input>
</div>

<!-- Phone: a prefix of the value, which starts at the column, or trailing text. -->
<FieldGroup v-if="position === 'start'" columns>
  <Field row label="Amount">
    <Input placeholder="0.00" inputmode="decimal">
      <template #startAdornment>
        <span class="text-secondary">$</span>
      </template>
    </Input>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
<FieldGroup v-else>
  <Field row label="Username">
    <Input v-model:value="username" placeholder="Required" maxlength="16">
      <template #endAdornment>
        <span class="tabular-nums text-secondary">{{ username.length }}/16</span>
      </template>
    </Input>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Clear button
Listen to @clear to display a built-in clear button. The button appears when the input has a value and is not disabled or read-only. Clicking it keeps focus in the input. On Phone it is the filled circle of an iOS text field: in a row it shows only while the value is being edited, at the row's end, and a long value stops 6px before it.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Input
    v-model:value="value"
    placeholder="Enter text..."
    :pill="pill"
    :size="size"
    @clear="value = ''"
  />
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Name">
    <Input v-model:value="value" placeholder="Enter text..." @clear="value = ''" />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Password with visibility toggle
Put an InputAdornmentButton in the endAdornment slot to switch between type="password" and type="text". Works across all sizes, variants, and with pill. On Phone, the button stands at the end of the row.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Input
    placeholder="Password"
    :type="visible ? 'text' : 'password'"
    :size="size"
    :variant="variant"
    :pill="pill"
  >
    <template #endAdornment>
      <InputAdornmentButton
        :aria-label="visible ? 'Hide' : 'Show'"
        :aria-pressed="visible"
        @click="visible = !visible"
      >
        <EyeOff v-if="visible" />
        <Eye v-else />
      </InputAdornmentButton>
    </template>
  </Input>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Password">
    <Input placeholder="Required" :type="visible ? 'text' : 'password'">
      <template #endAdornment>
        <InputAdornmentButton :aria-label="visible ? 'Hide' : 'Show'" :aria-pressed="visible" @click="visible = !visible">
          <EyeOff v-if="visible" />
          <Eye v-else />
        </InputAdornmentButton>
      </template>
    </Input>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### SSN input
A masked SSN field that formats input as XXX-XX-XXXX, hides digits behind bullets when masked, and reveals the value via an InputAdornmentButton in the endAdornment slot. An inline mask suffix highlights the next expected segment. Adapts to all sizes automatically. formatSSN, nextSSNDigits, ssnMask and measureInput are plain helper functions of the example. On Phone the row is the input's frame, and the overlay needs a wrapper that the row cannot frame: the field formats as you type and hides as a secure field.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <div style="position: relative">
    <Input
      :value="formatSSN(digits)"
      :placeholder="focused && ssnMask(digits).suffix ? undefined : 'Social Security Number'"
      :size="size"
      :variant="variant"
      :pill="pill"
      maxlength="11"
      inputmode="numeric"
      :style="{
        fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono, ui-monospace, monospace)',
        ...(!visible && digits
          ? { color: 'transparent', caretColor: 'transparent' }
          : focused && ssnMask(digits).suffix
            ? { caretColor: 'transparent' }
            : {}),
      }"
      @update:value="(raw) => (digits = nextSSNDigits(raw, digits))"
      @focus="focused = true"
      @blur="focused = false"
      @vue:mounted="(vnode) => Object.assign(metrics, measureInput(vnode))"
      @vue:updated="(vnode) => Object.assign(metrics, measureInput(vnode))"
    >
      <template #endAdornment>
        <InputAdornmentButton
          :aria-label="visible ? 'Hide' : 'Show'"
          :aria-pressed="visible"
          @click="visible = !visible"
        >
          <EyeOff v-if="visible" />
          <Eye v-else />
        </InputAdornmentButton>
      </template>
    </Input>
    <div
      v-if="(!visible && digits) || (focused && ssnMask(digits).suffix)"
      aria-hidden="true"
      :style="{
        position: 'absolute',
        top: '0',
        left: '0',
        right: '0',
        bottom: '0',
        display: 'flex',
        alignItems: 'center',
        padding: '0 ' + metrics.padding,
        pointerEvents: 'none',
        fontSize: metrics.fontSize,
        lineHeight: metrics.lineHeight,
        zIndex: 2,
        fontFamily: 'var(--font-mono, ui-monospace, monospace)',
        overflow: 'hidden',
      }"
    >
      <span v-if="!visible && digits" style="white-space: pre">{{ formatSSN(digits).replace(/[0-9]/g, '•') }}</span>
      <span v-else style="visibility: hidden; white-space: pre">{{ formatSSN(digits) }}</span>
      <template v-if="focused && ssnMask(digits).suffix">
        <span v-if="ssnMask(digits).before" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ ssnMask(digits).before }}</span>
        <span
          v-if="ssnMask(digits).highlight"
          style="background-color: var(--color-background-primary-solid); color: white; white-space: pre; border-radius: 3px; padding: 1px 1px"
        >{{ ssnMask(digits).highlight }}</span>
        <span v-if="ssnMask(digits).after" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ ssnMask(digits).after }}</span>
      </template>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="SSN">
    <Input
      :value="formatSSN(digits)"
      placeholder="XXX-XX-XXXX"
      :type="visible ? 'text' : 'password'"
      maxlength="11"
      inputmode="numeric"
      autocomplete="off"
      :allow-autofill-extensions="false"
      @update:value="(raw) => (digits = nextSSNDigits(raw, digits))"
    >
      <template #endAdornment>
        <InputAdornmentButton :aria-label="visible ? 'Hide' : 'Show'" :aria-pressed="visible" @click="visible = !visible">
          <EyeOff v-if="visible" />
          <Eye v-else />
        </InputAdornmentButton>
      </template>
    </Input>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Birthday input
A date-of-birth input with inline formatting that inserts separators as you type (MM / DD / YYYY). An inline mask suffix highlights the next expected segment. Works across all sizes, variants, and with pill. nextBirthday, birthdayMask and measureInput are plain helper functions of the example. On Phone the field formats as you type and the pattern is its placeholder, as in the About you form.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <div style="position: relative">
    <Input
      :value="birthday"
      :placeholder="focused && birthdayMask(birthday).suffix ? undefined : 'Birthday (MM / DD / YYYY)'"
      :size="size"
      :variant="variant"
      :pill="pill"
      maxlength="14"
      inputmode="numeric"
      :style="focused && birthdayMask(birthday).suffix ? { caretColor: 'transparent' } : undefined"
      @update:value="(raw) => (birthday = nextBirthday(raw, birthday))"
      @focus="focused = true"
      @blur="focused = false"
      @vue:mounted="(vnode) => Object.assign(metrics, measureInput(vnode))"
      @vue:updated="(vnode) => Object.assign(metrics, measureInput(vnode))"
    />
    <div
      v-if="focused && birthdayMask(birthday).suffix"
      aria-hidden="true"
      :style="{
        position: 'absolute',
        top: '0',
        left: '0',
        right: '0',
        bottom: '0',
        display: 'flex',
        alignItems: 'center',
        padding: '0 ' + metrics.padding,
        pointerEvents: 'none',
        fontSize: metrics.fontSize,
        lineHeight: metrics.lineHeight,
        zIndex: 2,
        overflow: 'hidden',
      }"
    >
      <span style="visibility: hidden; white-space: pre">{{ birthday }}</span>
      <span v-if="birthdayMask(birthday).before" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ birthdayMask(birthday).before }}</span>
      <span
        v-if="birthdayMask(birthday).highlight"
        style="background-color: var(--color-background-primary-solid); color: white; white-space: pre; border-radius: 3px; padding: 1px 1px"
      >{{ birthdayMask(birthday).highlight }}</span>
      <span v-if="birthdayMask(birthday).after" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ birthdayMask(birthday).after }}</span>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Birthday">
    <Input
      :value="birthday"
      placeholder="MM / DD / YYYY"
      maxlength="14"
      inputmode="numeric"
      autocomplete="bday"
      @update:value="(raw) => (birthday = nextBirthday(raw, birthday))"
    />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Birthday with validation
Use invalid and FieldError to display a validation state when the entered date is not valid. On Phone, give the message to the row's Field as error-message: it stands under the row.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <div class="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
    <div style="position: relative">
      <Input
        :value="birthday"
        :placeholder="focused && birthdayMask(birthday).suffix ? undefined : 'Birthday (MM / DD / YYYY)'"
        :size="size"
        :variant="variant"
        :pill="pill"
        maxlength="14"
        inputmode="numeric"
        :invalid="!!birthday && !isValidBirthday(birthday)"
        :style="focused && birthdayMask(birthday).suffix ? { caretColor: 'transparent' } : undefined"
        @update:value="(raw) => (birthday = nextBirthday(raw, birthday))"
        @focus="focused = true"
        @blur="focused = false"
        @vue:mounted="(vnode) => Object.assign(metrics, measureInput(vnode))"
        @vue:updated="(vnode) => Object.assign(metrics, measureInput(vnode))"
      />
      <div
        v-if="focused && birthdayMask(birthday).suffix"
        aria-hidden="true"
        :style="{
          position: 'absolute',
          top: '0',
          left: '0',
          right: '0',
          bottom: '0',
          display: 'flex',
          alignItems: 'center',
          padding: '0 ' + metrics.padding,
          pointerEvents: 'none',
          fontSize: metrics.fontSize,
          lineHeight: metrics.lineHeight,
          zIndex: 2,
          overflow: 'hidden',
        }"
      >
        <span style="visibility: hidden; white-space: pre">{{ birthday }}</span>
        <span v-if="birthdayMask(birthday).before" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ birthdayMask(birthday).before }}</span>
        <span
          v-if="birthdayMask(birthday).highlight"
          style="background-color: var(--color-background-primary-solid); color: white; white-space: pre; border-radius: 3px; padding: 1px 1px"
        >{{ birthdayMask(birthday).highlight }}</span>
        <span v-if="birthdayMask(birthday).after" style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary); white-space: pre">{{ birthdayMask(birthday).after }}</span>
      </div>
    </div>
    <FieldError v-if="birthday && !isValidBirthday(birthday)" id="input-birthday-validation-demo-error">
      Please enter a valid date.
    </FieldError>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Birthday" :error-message="birthday && !isValidBirthday(birthday) ? 'Please enter a valid date.' : undefined">
    <Input
      :value="birthday"
      placeholder="MM / DD / YYYY"
      maxlength="14"
      inputmode="numeric"
      autocomplete="bday"
      :invalid="!!birthday && !isValidBirthday(birthday)"
      @update:value="(raw) => (birthday = nextBirthday(raw, birthday))"
    />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### With button
You can pair Input and Button components together by using the same size value. On Phone, the button is the 50px main action under the group, size="3xl".
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="flex w-[320px] gap-2">
  <Input placeholder="jane.doe@gmail.com" :size="size" :variant="variant" />
  <Button color="primary" :size="size" :variant="variant">Subscribe</Button>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Email">
    <Input type="email" placeholder="jane.doe@gmail.com" />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
<Button color="primary" size="3xl" pill block>Subscribe</Button>
```

### Sizing & roundness
Sizing of the input is controlled with the size and gutter-size props. size sets the input to a specific control height, and gutter-size sets the horizontal padding. Each size provides default values for gutter-size, as well as font-size. Each of these values can be overridden independently. On Phone, a row sets the input's height, insets and type, and with touch.css the 3xs to xl sizes are all 44px, so this example has no Phone switch.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5 justify-start items-start w-[320px]">
  <Input
    placeholder="Enter text..."
    :size="size"
    :gutter-size="gutterSize === 'auto' ? undefined : gutterSize"
    :pill="pill"
  />
  <Input
    placeholder="Search..."
    :size="size"
    :gutter-size="gutterSize === 'auto' ? undefined : gutterSize"
    :pill="pill"
  >
    <template #startAdornment>
      <Search class="fill-tertiary" />
    </template>
  </Input>
  <Input
    v-model:value="username"
    placeholder="Username"
    maxlength="16"
    :size="size"
    :gutter-size="gutterSize === 'auto' ? undefined : gutterSize"
    :pill="pill"
  >
    <template #endAdornment>
      <span class="mt-[1.5px] tabular-nums text-tertiary text-[.875em]">{{ username.length }}/16</span>
    </template>
  </Input>
</div>

<!-- Phone: the row sets the height, insets and type. -->
<FieldGroup columns>
  <Field row label="Name">
    <Input placeholder="Enter text..." />
  </Field>
  <Field row label="Username">
    <Input v-model:value="username" placeholder="Required" maxlength="16">
      <template #endAdornment>
        <span class="tabular-nums text-secondary">{{ username.length }}/16</span>
      </template>
    </Input>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Behavior
Pass auto-select to select all text in the input, useful when pairing with other copy actions. This prop behaves like an imperative action when toggled and allows selection to change. By default, autofill extensions are prevented by Input. For the occasions where you want browser extensions to be present, pass allow-autofill-extensions to remove the disabling. When name is passed, or type is set to "password", this value will default to true. Both behave the same in a phone row, so this example has no Phone switch.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2 w-[320px]">
  <Input placeholder="Add text..." :auto-select="autoSelect" default-value="Toggle to auto select" />
  <Input
    :key="String(allowAutofillExtensions)"
    name="email"
    :allow-autofill-extensions="allowAutofillExtensions"
    :placeholder="allowAutofillExtensions ? 'Allowed' : 'Not allowed'"
  />
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Name">
    <Input placeholder="Add text..." :auto-select="autoSelect" default-value="Toggle to auto select" />
  </Field>
  <Field row label="Email">
    <Input
      :key="String(allowAutofillExtensions)"
      name="email"
      :allow-autofill-extensions="allowAutofillExtensions"
      :placeholder="allowAutofillExtensions ? 'Allowed' : 'Not allowed'"
    />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Optical alignment
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-full min-w-0 max-w-[400px]">
  <div class="border border-dashed border-alpha/20 rounded-md py-6 px-8">
    <div class="mb-5 text-secondary text-sm">
      {{ opticallyAlign ? 'optically-align="start"' : 'Default' }}
    </div>
    <div class="flex flex-col gap-5">
      <Field label="Full name" :optically-align="opticallyAlign ? 'start' : undefined">
        <Input placeholder="Jane Doe" :pill="pill" />
      </Field>
      <Field
        label="Email"
        description="We'll never share your email."
        error-message="Please enter a valid email address."
        required
        :optically-align="opticallyAlign ? 'start' : undefined"
      >
        <Input placeholder="jane@example.com" :pill="pill" />
      </Field>
    </div>
    <Button class="mt-6" color="primary" variant="soft" :pill="pill">Submit</Button>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- Phone: the help is the group's footer. -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Full name">
    <Input placeholder="Jane Doe" />
  </Field>
  <Field row label="Email" error-message="Please enter a valid email address.">
    <Input placeholder="jane@example.com" />
  </Field>
  <template #footer>We'll never share your email.</template>
</FieldGroup>
<Button color="primary" size="3xl" pill block>Submit</Button>
```

## Input Group
Input or textarea with icons, text, buttons and menus attached inside one frame, inline or as header and footer rows, in every input size and style.
Import: import { InputGroup, InputGroupAddon, InputGroupButton, InputGroupInput, InputGroupText, InputGroupTextarea } from "@plexui/vue/components/InputGroup"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/input-group
Props:
  - size ("3xs" | "2xs" | "xs" | "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl" | "2xl" | "3xl") default: "md" — Control height, on the same scale as Input (22px to 48px)
  - variant ("outline" | "soft") default: "outline" — Visual style of the frame
  - pill (boolean) default: false — Fully rounded frame, with pill buttons inside
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disables the control and the buttons inside
  - invalid (boolean) default: false — Invalid border; sets aria-invalid on the control
  - class (string) — Applied to the root <div>
  InputGroupAddon:
    - align ("inline-start" | "inline-end" | "block-start" | "block-end") default: "inline-start" — Before or after the control on its line, or a row above or below it
    - class (string) — Applied to the addon <div>

### Overview
Put an InputGroupInput and one or more InputGroupAddons inside an InputGroup. The group draws the frame and passes size, pill, disabled and invalid down to the control and the buttons.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5 w-[320px]">
  <InputGroup :size="size" :variant="variant" :pill="pill" :disabled="disabled" :invalid="invalid">
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="Search..." />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <Search />
    </InputGroupAddon>
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <InputGroupText>12 results</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup :size="size" :variant="variant" :pill="pill" :disabled="disabled" :invalid="invalid">
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="example" />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <InputGroupText>https://</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <InputGroupText>.com</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup :size="size" :variant="variant" :pill="pill" :disabled="disabled" :invalid="invalid">
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="Ask anything..." />
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <InputGroupButton variant="solid" color="primary" uniform aria-label="Send">
        <ArrowUpSm />
      </InputGroupButton>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
</div>

<!-- Phone: the group is the value of a row; with a prefix the rows take columns. -->
<FieldGroup columns>
  <Field row label="Website" :error-message="invalid ? 'Enter a valid address.' : undefined">
    <template #default="control">
      <InputGroup :disabled="disabled" :invalid="invalid">
        <InputGroupInput v-bind="control" placeholder="acme.com" inputmode="url" autocapitalize="none" spellcheck="false" />
        <InputGroupAddon>
          <InputGroupText>https://</InputGroupText>
        </InputGroupAddon>
      </InputGroup>
    </template>
  </Field>
  <Field row label="Amount">
    <template #default="control">
      <InputGroup :disabled="disabled">
        <InputGroupInput v-bind="control" placeholder="0.00" inputmode="decimal" />
        <InputGroupAddon>
          <InputGroupText>$</InputGroupText>
        </InputGroupAddon>
      </InputGroup>
    </template>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Variants
Use variant to stylize the group, like Input. On Phone the row is the frame, so a group in a row keeps the default outline, which the row draws without a border, and the row places the value: at its end, or, with columns, at one column shared by the group's rows.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5 w-[320px]">
  <InputGroup variant="outline">
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="Outline" />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <Search />
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup variant="soft">
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="Soft" />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <Search />
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup title="Value at the end">
  <Field row label="Distance">
    <template #default="control">
      <InputGroup>
        <InputGroupInput v-bind="control" default-value="5" inputmode="decimal" />
        <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
          <InputGroupText>km</InputGroupText>
        </InputGroupAddon>
      </InputGroup>
    </template>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
<FieldGroup title="Values in a column" columns>
  <Field row label="Handle">
    <template #default="control">
      <InputGroup>
        <InputGroupInput v-bind="control" placeholder="username" autocapitalize="none" spellcheck="false" />
        <InputGroupAddon>
          <InputGroupText>@</InputGroupText>
        </InputGroupAddon>
      </InputGroup>
    </template>
  </Field>
  <Field row label="Price">
    <template #default="control">
      <InputGroup>
        <InputGroupInput v-bind="control" placeholder="0.00" inputmode="decimal" />
        <InputGroupAddon>
          <InputGroupText>€</InputGroupText>
        </InputGroupAddon>
      </InputGroup>
    </template>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Align
Use align on InputGroupAddon to place it before (inline-start, the default) or after (inline-end) the control, or as a row above (block-start) or below (block-end) it. Place addons after the control in the markup so the tab order starts with the field; align handles the visual position. On Phone the inline addons stay in the row (see Variants), a block-start text becomes the row's label and a block-end text the group's footer.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5 w-[320px]">
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="inline-start" />
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-start">
      <Search />
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="inline-end" />
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <InfoCircle />
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="Ada Lovelace" />
    <InputGroupAddon align="block-start">
      <InputGroupText>Full name</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput placeholder="0.00" />
    <InputGroupAddon align="block-end">
      <InputGroupText>Amount in USD</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Full name">
    <template #default="control">
      <InputGroup>
        <InputGroupInput v-bind="control" placeholder="Ada Lovelace" />
      </InputGroup>
    </template>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
<FieldGroup>
  <template #footer>Amount in USD</template>
  <Field row label="Amount">
    <template #default="control">
      <InputGroup>
        <InputGroupInput v-bind="control" placeholder="0.00" inputmode="decimal" />
      </InputGroup>
    </template>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Text
Use InputGroupText for prefixes, suffixes, units and counters. Clicking text or icons in an addon focuses the field.
```vue

      <InputGroupText>https://</InputGroupText>

      <InputGroupText>.com</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>

    <InputGroupInput placeholder="username" />

<Button color="primary" size="3xl" pill block>Pay</Button>
```

### Dropdown
Wrap an InputGroupButton in a Menu to pick a unit of measurement next to the value.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref } from "vue"
import { ChevronDownMd } from "@plexui/vue/components/Icon"
import {
  InputGroup,
  InputGroupAddon,
  InputGroupButton,
  InputGroupInput,
  InputGroupText,
} from "@plexui/vue/components/InputGroup"
import { Menu, MenuCheckboxItem, MenuContent, MenuTrigger } from "@plexui/vue/components/Menu"

const UNITS = [
  { value: "kg", label: "Kilograms" },
  { value: "lb", label: "Pounds" },
  { value: "st", label: "Stones" },
]
const unit = ref("kg")
</script>

<template>
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput default-value="72" inputmode="decimal" aria-label="Weight" />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <InputGroupText>Weight</InputGroupText>
    </InputGroupAddon>
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <Menu>
        <MenuTrigger>
          <InputGroupButton aria-label="Weight unit">
            {{ unit }}<ChevronDownMd />
          </InputGroupButton>
        </MenuTrigger>
        <MenuContent align="end" :min-width="160">
          <MenuCheckboxItem
            v-for="item in UNITS"
            :key="item.value"
            :checked="unit === item.value"
            indicator-variant="ghost"
            @update:checked="unit = item.value"
          >
            {{ item.label }} ({{ item.value }})
          </MenuCheckboxItem>
        </MenuContent>
      </Menu>
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
</template>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Weight">
    <template #default="control">
      <InputGroup>
        <InputGroupInput v-bind="control" default-value="72" inputmode="decimal" />
        <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
          <Menu>
            <MenuTrigger>
              <InputGroupButton :aria-label="'Unit: ' + weightUnit" class="text-secondary font-normal">
                {{ weightUnit }}<span class="ms-1"><DropdownIcon icon-type="dropdown" /></span>
              </InputGroupButton>
            </MenuTrigger>
            <MenuContent align="end" min-width="auto">
              <MenuCheckboxItem
                v-for="unit in weightUnits"
                :key="unit.value"
                :checked="weightUnit === unit.value"
                indicator-position="start"
                indicator-variant="ghost"
                @update:checked="weightUnit = unit.value"
              >
                {{ unit.label }}
              </MenuCheckboxItem>
            </MenuContent>
          </Menu>
        </InputGroupAddon>
      </InputGroup>
    </template>
  </Field>
  <Field row label="Height">
    <template #default="control">
      <InputGroup>
        <InputGroupInput v-bind="control" default-value="180" inputmode="decimal" />
        <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
          <Menu>
            <MenuTrigger>
              <InputGroupButton :aria-label="'Unit: ' + lengthUnit" class="text-secondary font-normal">
                {{ lengthUnit }}<span class="ms-1"><DropdownIcon icon-type="dropdown" /></span>
              </InputGroupButton>
            </MenuTrigger>
            <MenuContent align="end" min-width="auto">
              <MenuCheckboxItem
                v-for="unit in lengthUnits"
                :key="unit.value"
                :checked="lengthUnit === unit.value"
                indicator-position="start"
                indicator-variant="ghost"
                @update:checked="lengthUnit = unit.value"
              >
                {{ unit.label }}
              </MenuCheckboxItem>
            </MenuContent>
          </Menu>
        </InputGroupAddon>
      </InputGroup>
    </template>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Spinner
Show a LoadingIndicator in an addon while a value is searched, checked or saved. On a phone with touch.css an indicator in a field is 20px, the iOS 27 Regular spinner, as a standalone one is.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2.5 w-[320px]">
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput default-value="Searching..." readonly />
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <LoadingIndicator />
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput default-value="Processing..." readonly />
    <InputGroupAddon>
      <LoadingIndicator />
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
  <InputGroup>
    <InputGroupInput default-value="Saving changes" readonly />
    <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
      <InputGroupText>Saving...</InputGroupText>
      <LoadingIndicator />
    </InputGroupAddon>
  </InputGroup>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <template #footer>Checking availability...</template>
  <Field row label="Username">
    <template #default="control">
      <InputGroup>
        <InputGroupInput v-bind="control" default-value="ada" autocapitalize="none" spellcheck="false" />
        <InputGroupAddon align="inline-end">
          <LoadingIndicator />
        </InputGroupAddon>
      </InputGroup>
    </template>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Textarea
