```vue
<!-- Phone: each option is a row; the selected row ends with a checkmark. -->
<RadioGroup direction="col" class="w-full" v-model:value="frequency" :disabled="disabled" aria-label="Notification frequency">
  <FieldGroup title="Notification frequency">
    <Field row label="Daily" :description="description ? 'A summary every morning.' : undefined">
      <RadioGroupItem value="daily" />
    </Field>
    <Field row label="Weekly" :description="description ? 'A digest every Monday.' : undefined">
      <RadioGroupItem value="weekly" />
    </Field>
    <Field row label="Monthly" :description="description ? 'A recap on the first of the month.' : undefined">
      <RadioGroupItem value="monthly" :disabled="itemDisabled" />
    </Field>
    <Field row label="Never" :description="description ? 'Only security alerts.' : undefined">
      <RadioGroupItem value="never" />
    </Field>
  </FieldGroup>
</RadioGroup>
```

### With Field
Wrap a RadioGroup in a Field for a shared label and description. Bind the field props from the slot onto the group so it is described by the Field's description (aria-describedby). On a phone the FieldGroup title and description above the rows take this place.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="max-w-sm">
  <Field label="Notification method" description="Choose how you want to receive notifications." v-slot="fieldProps">
    <RadioGroup direction="col" v-model:value="value" aria-label="Notification method" v-bind="fieldProps">
      <RadioGroupItem value="email">Email</RadioGroupItem>
      <RadioGroupItem value="sms">SMS</RadioGroupItem>
      <RadioGroupItem value="push">Push notification</RadioGroupItem>
    </RadioGroup>
  </Field>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<RadioGroup direction="col" class="w-full" v-model:value="value" aria-label="Notification method">
  <FieldGroup title="Notification method" description="Choose how you want to receive notifications.">
    <Field row label="Email"><RadioGroupItem value="email" /></Field>
    <Field row label="SMS"><RadioGroupItem value="sms" /></Field>
    <Field row label="Push notification"><RadioGroupItem value="push" /></Field>
  </FieldGroup>
</RadioGroup>
```

### With Field error
When a Field has an error-message, it sets aria-invalid on the child control for screen readers. The error message is displayed below the group. On a phone a FieldError in the group's footer shows it under the rows; link it to the group with aria-describedby.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="max-w-sm">
  <Field
    label="Subscription plan"
    description="Select a plan to continue."
    :error-message="showError ? 'Please select a subscription plan.' : undefined"
    v-slot="fieldProps"
  >
    <RadioGroup direction="col" v-model:value="value" aria-label="Subscription plan" v-bind="fieldProps">
      <RadioGroupItem value="monthly">Monthly ($9.99/month)</RadioGroupItem>
      <RadioGroupItem value="yearly">Yearly ($99.99/year)</RadioGroupItem>
      <RadioGroupItem value="lifetime">Lifetime ($299.99)</RadioGroupItem>
    </RadioGroup>
  </Field>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<RadioGroup
  direction="col"
  class="w-full"
  v-model:value="value"
  aria-label="Subscription plan"
  :aria-invalid="showError || undefined"
  :aria-describedby="showError ? 'subscription-plan-error' : undefined"
>
  <FieldGroup title="Subscription plan" description="Select a plan to continue.">
    <Field row label="Monthly ($9.99/month)"><RadioGroupItem value="monthly" /></Field>
    <Field row label="Yearly ($99.99/year)"><RadioGroupItem value="yearly" /></Field>
    <Field row label="Lifetime ($299.99)"><RadioGroupItem value="lifetime" /></Field>
    <template v-if="showError" #footer>
      <FieldError id="subscription-plan-error">Please select a subscription plan.</FieldError>
    </template>
  </FieldGroup>
</RadioGroup>
```

### Grouped sections
Use dividers to separate radio groups into logical sections. Each section can be wrapped in a Field with its own label and description.
```vue
<Page surface="grouped" class="h-dvh" data-form-screen style="--page-content-padding-top:10px; --page-content-padding-bottom:16px; --page-footer-gutter:24px; --page-footer-padding-bottom:max(24px, env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px))">
  <Toolbar>
    <ToolbarLeading><DemoBackButton /></ToolbarLeading>
    <ToolbarTitle>Preferences</ToolbarTitle>
  </Toolbar>
  <PageContent data-form-content>
    <form ref="screenForm" class="flex flex-col gap-6" @submit.prevent="" >
      <RadioGroup direction="col" class="w-full" v-model:value="visibility" aria-label="Visibility">
        <FieldGroup title="Visibility" description="Control who can see project resources.">
          <Field row label="Hidden"><RadioGroupItem value="hidden" /></Field>
          <Field row label="Visible to organization owners"><RadioGroupItem value="owners" /></Field>
          <Field row label="Visible to everyone"><RadioGroupItem value="everyone" /></Field>
        </FieldGroup>
      </RadioGroup>
      <RadioGroup direction="col" class="w-full" v-model:value="view" aria-label="Default view">
        <FieldGroup title="Default view" description="What opens first in a project.">
          <Field row label="Board"><RadioGroupItem value="board" /></Field>
          <Field row label="List"><RadioGroupItem value="list" /></Field>
          <Field row label="Timeline"><RadioGroupItem value="timeline" /></Field>
        </FieldGroup>
      </RadioGroup>
    </form>
  </PageContent>

</Page>
```

### With Checkbox
Place RadioGroup alongside Checkbox controls to verify that indicator sizes, label baselines, and spacing align consistently in both horizontal and vertical layouts. This check is desktop only: on a phone both live in rows, where the radio is a checkmark and the checkbox a circle at the end of the row.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-6 w-full max-w-md">
  <Field label="Notification channel" description="Choose how you'd like to be notified." v-slot="{ id }">
    <RadioGroup :direction="direction" default-value="email" aria-label="Notification channel">
      <RadioGroupItem
        v-for="channel in [
          { value: 'email', label: 'Email', description: 'Messages delivered to your inbox.' },
          { value: 'sms', label: 'SMS', description: 'Text messages to your phone number.' },
          { value: 'push', label: 'Push', description: 'Real-time alerts on your device.' },
        ]"
        :key="channel.value"
        :value="channel.value"
      >
        <div v-if="description === 'true'" class="flex flex-col">
          <span class="font-medium">{{ channel.label }}</span>
          <span class="text-xs text-secondary mt-0.5">{{ channel.description }}</span>
        </div>
        <template v-else>{{ channel.label }}</template>
      </RadioGroupItem>
    </RadioGroup>
  </Field>
  <Field label="Topics" description="Select the topics you want to follow." v-slot="{ id }">
    <div :class="direction === 'row' ? 'flex flex-row gap-5' : 'flex flex-col gap-2.5'">
      <Checkbox
        v-for="topic in [
          { label: 'Product updates', description: 'New features and improvements.', checked: true },
          { label: 'Security alerts', description: 'Account activity and login notifications.', checked: true },
          { label: 'Community', description: 'Events, meetups, and discussions.', checked: false },
        ]"
        :key="topic.label"
        :default-checked="topic.checked"
      >
        <template #label>
          <div v-if="description === 'true'" class="flex flex-col">
            <span class="font-medium">{{ topic.label }}</span>
            <span class="text-xs text-secondary mt-0.5">{{ topic.description }}</span>
          </div>
          <template v-else>{{ topic.label }}</template>
        </template>
      </Checkbox>
    </div>
  </Field>
</div>
```

### Descriptions
Combine Field-level label and description with item-level descriptions for options that need additional context. Use a plain label without descriptions when the options are self-explanatory. On a phone the group title, its description and row descriptions do this, as the Overview and With Field show, so this example is desktop only.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col max-w-sm w-full divide-y divide-alpha/15">
  <div class="pb-5">
    <Field label="Notification channel" description="Choose how you'd like to be notified." v-slot="{ id }">
      <RadioGroup direction="col" v-model:value="channel" aria-label="Notification channel">
        <RadioGroupItem value="email">
          <div class="flex flex-col">
            <span class="font-medium">Email</span>
            <span class="text-xs text-secondary mt-0.5">Messages delivered to your inbox.</span>
          </div>
        </RadioGroupItem>
        <RadioGroupItem value="sms">
          <div class="flex flex-col">
            <span class="font-medium">SMS</span>
            <span class="text-xs text-secondary mt-0.5">Text messages to your phone number.</span>
          </div>
        </RadioGroupItem>
        <RadioGroupItem value="push">
          <div class="flex flex-col">
            <span class="font-medium">Push</span>
            <span class="text-xs text-secondary mt-0.5">Real-time alerts on your device.</span>
          </div>
        </RadioGroupItem>
      </RadioGroup>
    </Field>
  </div>
  <div class="pt-5">
    <Field label="Notification sound" v-slot="{ id }">
      <RadioGroup direction="col" v-model:value="sound" aria-label="Notification sound">
        <RadioGroupItem value="default">Default</RadioGroupItem>
        <RadioGroupItem value="chime">Chime</RadioGroupItem>
        <RadioGroupItem value="none">None</RadioGroupItem>
      </RadioGroup>
    </Field>
  </div>
</div>
```

### Choice card
Style each radio option as a bordered card with visual selection state. The selected card gets a highlighted border using design tokens. Use orientation="right" to place the indicator after the content. Wrap each RadioGroupItem in a card container with border and borderRadius on the same element. On a phone a choice with a description is a row, as in the Overview, so this example is desktop only.
```vue
<RadioGroup class="flex-col w-[320px] gap-3" v-model:value="value" aria-label="Workspace type">
  <div
    v-for="card in [
      { id: 'personal', title: 'Personal', description: 'For hobby projects and experimentation.' },
      { id: 'team', title: 'Team', description: 'Collaborate with up to 10 members.' },
      { id: 'business', title: 'Business', description: 'SSO, audit logs, and priority support.' },
    ]"
    :key="card.id"
    :style="{
      border: '1px solid',
      borderColor: value === card.id ? 'var(--color-background-primary-solid)' : 'var(--color-border-primary-outline)',
      borderRadius: 'var(--radius-lg)',
      backgroundColor: value === card.id ? 'var(--color-background-primary-soft-alpha)' : undefined,
      transition: 'border-color 150ms ease, background-color 150ms ease',
    }"
  >
    <RadioGroupItem :value="card.id" block :orientation="orientation" class="gap-3 p-3">
      <div class="flex flex-col flex-1">
        <span class="font-semibold">{{ card.title }}</span>
        <span class="text-xs text-secondary mt-0.5">{{ card.description }}</span>
      </div>
    </RadioGroupItem>
  </div>
</RadioGroup>
```

### Card selection
Leverage component composition and class overrides to build card-style radio selections with rich content per option. Desktop only, like Choice card.
```vue
<RadioGroup class="flex-col w-full min-w-0 max-w-[390px] gap-4" aria-label="Sample options">
  <RadioGroupItem class="gap-2.5" value="basic">
    <div>
      <h4 class="font-semibold mb-1">Basic Plan</h4>
      <p class="text-secondary text-sm">5 GB storage • Email support • Free</p>
    </div>
  </RadioGroupItem>
  <hr class="border-default" />
  <RadioGroupItem class="gap-2.5" value="standard">
    <div>
      <h4 class="font-semibold mb-1">Standard Plan</h4>
      <p class="text-secondary text-sm">100 GB storage • Priority email support • $9.99/m</p>
    </div>
  </RadioGroupItem>
  <hr class="border-default" />
  <Tooltip :max-width="258" interactive>
    <template #content>
      Contact our <TextLink href="#">support team</TextLink> for more information on enterprise plans.
    </template>
    <TooltipTrigger>
      <RadioGroupItem class="gap-2.5" value="enterprise" disabled>
        <div>
          <h4 class="font-semibold mb-1">Enterprise Plan</h4>
          <p class="text-secondary text-sm">Unlimited storage • 24/7 phone support • Custom pricing</p>
        </div>
      </RadioGroupItem>
    </TooltipTrigger>
  </Tooltip>
</RadioGroup>
```

## Segmented Control
Switch between grouped views with one control, a segmented track or underline tabs, with icon-only segments and badge counts on each option.
Import: import { SegmentedControl, SegmentedControlOption } from "@plexui/vue/components/SegmentedControl"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/segmented-control
Props:
  - value (string) — Selected tab value. Bind with v-model:value; leave unset for an uncontrolled group
  - variant ("segmented" | "underline") default: "segmented" — Visual style: segmented (background highlight) or underline (line indicator)
  - orientation ("horizontal" | "vertical") default: "horizontal" — Layout direction of the tab group
  - size ("3xs" | "2xs" | "xs" | "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl" | "2xl" | "3xl") default: "md" — Control height (3xs through 3xl). On a phone sm and smaller are the small 32px iOS control and md through 3xl the large 48px one (49px when flat); in a phone Toolbar it is the large one
  - gutter-size ("2xs" | "xs" | "sm" | "md" | "lg" | "xl") — Horizontal padding of tabs
  - pill (boolean) default: true — Fully rounded control (segmented variant only)
  - flush (boolean) default: false — Text-width indicator, no border, gap spacing (underline variant only)
  - block (boolean) default: false — Full-width control with equal-width tabs
  - mobileMaterial ("glass" | "flat") default: "glass" at md and larger, "flat" at sm and smaller — Phone surface of horizontal tabs, as for Button: flat makes the large control plain (49px), glass puts the small one under Liquid Glass
  - mobileSegmentWidth ("equal" | "content") default: "equal" — Phone widths of horizontal segments, as UISegmentedControl sets them: equal gives every segment the widest one's width, content gives each its label and the same air around it (apportionsSegmentWidthsByContent). Equal segments that do not fit take their labels' widths; where those still do not fit, the air beside each label narrows, to 4px, and what still does not fit scrolls; a block control fills its row
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable all tabs
  - aria-label (string) — Accessible name (required)
  - overflow ("scroll" | "menu") default: "scroll" — Options that do not fit: scroll sideways, or move into a menu at the end (horizontal, not block)
  - overflow-label (string) default: "More" — Text of the menu button with overflow="menu"
  - class (string) — Extra classes on the group element
  - default slot (VNode[]) — SegmentedControlOption children
  - @update:value ((value: string) => void) — Emitted when a tab is selected; powers v-model:value
  - @change ((value: string) => void) — Emitted with update:value, named after the React onChange prop
  - @click ((event: MouseEvent) => void) — Emitted on any click in the group, even when the selection does not change
  SegmentedControlOption:
    - value (string) — Tab value (required)
    - disabled (boolean) — Disable this tab
    - icon (Component | VNode | string) — Icon before the label (sized automatically); the icon slot takes precedence
    - badge (number | string | VNode | { content; color?; variant?; pill?; loading? }) — Badge/count or custom badge config
    - aria-label (string) — Accessible name (required for icon-only tabs)
    - default slot (VNode[]) — Tab label
    - icon slot (VNode[]) — Icon before the label
    - badge slot (VNode[]) — Badge content; styling still comes from the badge object

### Overview
SegmentedControl provides two visual variants: segmented (default) with a sliding background highlight, and underline with an animated line indicator under the active tab and no background container. Bind the selected tab with v-model:value; the examples keep it in a tab variable. flush makes the underline indicator match the text width exactly, with gap between tabs instead of padding, and block stretches the tabs evenly across the container. orientation="vertical" stacks the tabs: the thumb slides up and down, and the underline becomes a line on the left side, for sidebar-style navigation. Both variants take the full 9-step size scale of Button, Input, Select and SelectControl, which sets the height, font size, icon size and spacing of the tabs. Put an icon in a tab's icon slot, or pass a component to its icon prop, to show it before the label, sized to the size; leave out the label for icon-only tabs of a horizontal control and give each an aria-label. Vertical tabs keep their labels, so the example offers no icon only there: they are a list, a sidebar or on a phone an inset grouped list, and a list row always has a title. badge takes a number or an object with content, color, variant and pill, shown after the label inside the control; a count names what it counts, so it sits on a labelled tab and icon-only tabs carry none. disabled affects the whole group, which on a phone shows at half opacity when horizontal, as a disabled iOS segmented control does; monthDisabled disables only Month, whose label in a horizontal control on a phone takes the disabled label of iOS, 20% of the label color, while its row in a vertical one shows at half opacity. Arrow-key navigation skips disabled tabs without resetting the selected value.On a phone horizontal tabs of either variant are the iOS 27 segmented control, filling the row of a phone screen, in its two sizes. The default md and larger are the large control of iOS 27 apps, as Calendar and Reminders draw it and SwiftUI draws a segmented Picker in a toolbar: a 48px glass capsule, a 40px gray selection 4px inside it, 8px between segments, every label 15px Medium and primary. sm and smaller are the small one, the plain control of SwiftUI's Picker in content and of Safari's bookmarks: a 32px capsule of the tertiary fill, a white selection 2px inside it, labels 13px, Semibold when selected. mobileMaterial switches the look, as for Button: flat makes the large one plain, 49px as SwiftUI's .controlSize(.large), and glass puts the small one under the Liquid Glass of .glassEffect(). Inside a phone Toolbar every control is the large glass one. The phone example puts the control in a top bar between Close and Confirm, as a sheet's toolbar holds a segmented Picker in iOS 27.2 and Calendar its new event, in a bottom bar, or above the content, as Safari's bookmarks do, over a day that scrolls under the glass of the bars; placement picks one. Vertical tabs of either variant are an inset grouped list, as iOS lists a choice: 52px rows with the 17px label and a checkmark on the selected row instead of the thumb. A segment with an icon and a label shows the icon alone, at the 20px symbol size, as UISegmentedControl draws an action with a title and an image; the label stays the segment&apos;s accessible name, since iOS segments carry a title or a symbol, not both. They carry no badges either: on a phone a badge is not drawn and its count stays in the segment&apos;s accessible name, so write a count into the label to show it. mobileSegmentWidth sets the segments&apos; widths, as UISegmentedControl does: equal, the default, gives every segment the widest one&apos;s width, and content gives each its label and the same air around it, a compact control. At the control&apos;s own width the widest label stands 4px inside its segment in the large control and 9px in the small one, as iOS 27.2 sets them. Underline tabs draw the same segmented control on a phone and every control there is a capsule filling its row, so the phone view has no variant, flush, block, pill and badge controls, and its size offers sm and md.
```vue
<SegmentedControl
  v-model:value="tab"
  :variant="variant"
  :orientation="orientation"
  :size="size"
  :pill="pill"
  :flush="flush"
  :block="block"
  :disabled="disabled"
  :mobile-material="mobileMaterial"
  :mobile-segment-width="mobileSegmentWidth"
  aria-label="Calendar view"
>
  <SegmentedControlOption value="day">Day</SegmentedControlOption>
  <SegmentedControlOption value="week">Week</SegmentedControlOption>
  <SegmentedControlOption value="month" :disabled="monthDisabled">Month</SegmentedControlOption>
  <SegmentedControlOption value="year">Year</SegmentedControlOption>
</SegmentedControl>
```

### Icons
An icon before each label, sized to the control's size.
```vue
<SegmentedControl
  v-model:value="tab"
  :variant="variant"
  :orientation="orientation"
  :size="size"
  :pill="pill"
  :flush="flush"
  :block="block"
  :disabled="disabled"
  :mobile-material="mobileMaterial"
  :mobile-segment-width="mobileSegmentWidth"
  aria-label="Calendar view"
>
  <SegmentedControlOption value="day"><template #icon><CalendarToday /></template>Day</SegmentedControlOption>
  <SegmentedControlOption value="week"><template #icon><List /></template>Week</SegmentedControlOption>
  <SegmentedControlOption value="month" :disabled="monthDisabled"><template #icon><Calendar /></template>Month</SegmentedControlOption>
  <SegmentedControlOption value="year"><template #icon><Grid /></template>Year</SegmentedControlOption>
</SegmentedControl>
```

### Icon only
Icons alone, each tab named by its aria-label.
```vue
<SegmentedControl
  v-model:value="tab"
  :variant="variant"
  :orientation="orientation"
  :size="size"
  :pill="pill"
  :flush="flush"
  :block="block"
  :disabled="disabled"
  :mobile-material="mobileMaterial"
  :mobile-segment-width="mobileSegmentWidth"
  aria-label="Calendar view"
>
  <SegmentedControlOption value="day" aria-label="Day"><template #icon><CalendarToday /></template></SegmentedControlOption>
  <SegmentedControlOption value="week" aria-label="Week"><template #icon><List /></template></SegmentedControlOption>
  <SegmentedControlOption value="month" :disabled="monthDisabled" aria-label="Month"><template #icon><Calendar /></template></SegmentedControlOption>
  <SegmentedControlOption value="year" aria-label="Year"><template #icon><Grid /></template></SegmentedControlOption>
</SegmentedControl>
```

### Badges
A count after each label: a number, or an object with content, color, variant and pill.
```vue
<SegmentedControl
  v-model:value="tab"
  :variant="variant"
  :orientation="orientation"
  :size="size"
  :pill="pill"
  :flush="flush"
  :block="block"
  :disabled="disabled"
  :mobile-material="mobileMaterial"
  :mobile-segment-width="mobileSegmentWidth"
  aria-label="Calendar view"
>
  <SegmentedControlOption value="day" :badge="{ content: 3, color: 'danger', pill: true }">Day</SegmentedControlOption>
  <SegmentedControlOption value="week" :badge="{ content: 12, color: 'info', pill: true }">Week</SegmentedControlOption>
  <SegmentedControlOption value="month" :disabled="monthDisabled" :badge="{ content: 48, pill: true }">Month</SegmentedControlOption>
  <SegmentedControlOption value="year">Year</SegmentedControlOption>
</SegmentedControl>
```

### Overflow
When tabs overflow their container they scroll sideways, the default overflow="scroll", and the active tab scrolls into view smoothly: constrain the container width and nothing else is needed. On a phone the segments share one width, the widest label's, as iOS 27.2 draws a segmented control at its own width; where that does not fit, each segment takes its label with the same air around it, as iOS does for segments sized by their content, and tabs that still do not fit scroll inside the capsule, as Safari's tab groups do. A phone draws no badges, as iOS segments carry none. With overflow="menu" the tabs that do not fit stay out of the row and move into a menu at its end. When the active tab is a hidden one, the menu button shows its name and carries the selection. A tab's badge moves into the menu with it. overflow-label sets the button's text (default "More"). The menu works for horizontal tabs without block.
```vue
<div class="flex min-w-0 max-w-full" :style="{ width: width ? width + 'px' : undefined }">
  <SegmentedControl v-model:value="tab" :overflow="overflow" :variant="variant" aria-label="Repository">
    <SegmentedControlOption value="code">Code</SegmentedControlOption>
    <SegmentedControlOption value="issues" :badge="12">Issues</SegmentedControlOption>
    <SegmentedControlOption value="pulls" :badge="3">Pull requests</SegmentedControlOption>
    <SegmentedControlOption value="actions">Actions</SegmentedControlOption>
    <SegmentedControlOption value="projects">Projects</SegmentedControlOption>
    <SegmentedControlOption value="insights">Insights</SegmentedControlOption>
    <SegmentedControlOption value="settings">Settings</SegmentedControlOption>
  </SegmentedControl>
</div>
```

## Select
Select dropdown with grouped options, per-item descriptions, multiple selection and action items; on a phone, an iOS pop-up menu.
Import: import { Select } from "@plexui/vue/components/Select"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/select
Props:
  - options (Options<T>) — Array of options or grouped options
  - v-model:value (string | string[]) — Selected value (single) or values (multiple)
  - @change ((option | options) => void) — Called when selection changes, with the selected option (or options with multiple)
  - multiple (boolean) default: false — Allow multiple selection. value, @change and @update:value are typed by it
  - placeholder (string) default: "Select..." — Trigger placeholder
  - variant ("soft" | "outline" | "ghost") default: "outline" — Trigger variant
  - size (ControlSize) default: "md" — Trigger size (3xs to 3xl)
  - pill (boolean) default: false — Rounded trigger
  - clearable (boolean) default: false — Show clear button. On a phone, with the default trigger, a None row first in the list instead
  - none-label (string) default: "None" — Label of the row that unsets a clearable select on a phone
  - block (boolean) default: variant !== "ghost" — Full width trigger
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable select. A disabled select is not submitted or validated with its form
  - loading (boolean) default: false — Loading state; shows loading-placeholder and keeps the menu closed
  - loading-placeholder (string) default: "Loading..." — Trigger text while loading
  - all-selected-label (string) default: "All selected" — Text of a multiple trigger when every option is selected
  - name (string) — Name of the hidden form input. With multiple, every selected value is submitted
  - required (boolean) — Required for native form validation
  - id (string) — Id of the hidden form input
  - actions (array) — Actions below options list
  - check-position ("start" | "end" | "none") default: "start" — Where the check indicator sits in the list. "none" removes the indicator column for lists that never carry a selection. On a phone the checkmark leads the row, as in iOS menus, so "end" stands at the start there
  - #optionView / OptionView (slot (option) | Component) — Custom option render
  - #triggerView / TriggerView (slot | Component) — Custom trigger render: the selected option, or { values, selectedAll } with multiple
  - TriggerStartIcon (Component) — Icon at the start of the trigger
  - #trigger / trigger (slot ({ open, onToggle }) | function) — Custom trigger element, wrapped as the popover trigger
  - dropdown-icon-type ("chevronDown" | "dropdown" | "none") default: "dropdown" — Indicator at the end of the trigger
  - side ("top" | "bottom") default: "bottom" — Preferred side of the list. Without one, on a phone the list opens over its trigger toward the larger part of the screen
  - align ("start" | "center" | "end") default: "end" at the end of a Field row ("start" in a FieldGroup with columns), "center" when block, otherwise "start" — Alignment of the list against the trigger
  - list-width (number | "auto") default: trigger width (fits the options when not block or at the end of a Field row; 250px on a phone) — Width of the list in px
  - list-min-width (number | "auto") default: trigger width (at least 250px on a phone, unless the list has its own width or a smaller maximum) — Minimum width of the list in px
  - list-max-width (number | "auto") default: "auto" (300 when it fits the options) — Maximum width of the list in px
  - search-placeholder (string) — Search input placeholder
  - search-aria-label (string) default: "Search options" — Accessible name of the search input, independent of the trigger label
  - #searchEmptyMessage / search-empty-message (slot | string) default: "No results found." — Shown when the search matches nothing
  - search-size (ControlSize) default: "md" — Size of the search input in the menu. A phone list has no search: it is an iOS menu, and a long one scrolls
  - search-predicate ((option, term) => boolean) — Filters options while searching
  - aria-label / aria-labelledby (string) — Accessible name of the trigger and the list

### Overview
Bind the selection with v-model:value: a string, or an array of strings with multiple. multiple also changes what @change and the #triggerView slot carry: @change carries the selected option, or every selected option, like React's onChange, and the slot receives {"{ values, selectedAll }"}. The trigger is a SelectControl: variant, size (the 9-step scale of Button, Input, SelectControl and SegmentedControl), pill and dropdown-icon-type style it. block stretches it across its container, here the 290px field, and the list takes its width. Without block the trigger fits its value and the list is at least list-min-width, here 240px, wider than the trigger, so align places it against the trigger. side is the side the list prefers. check-position puts the checkmark at the start or the end of a row; "none" leaves the column out. clearable adds a clear action. loading shows loading-placeholder and keeps the list closed. disabled disables the select, and a disabled select is not submitted with its form. optically-align offsets the trigger by its gutter: "start" puts the text of a ghost trigger on the label's edge, and "end" puts the end of a block one, its indicator, on the field's end. A framed trigger keeps its gutter, so the example offers the setting for ghost only, and "end" only with block: a narrower trigger does not reach the field's end.On Phone the select is the value of a row in a FieldGroup, with :block="false" and align="end". The row sets its height, insets, type and colors, and the list opens over the value with the checkmark leading. So the Phone preview has no variant, size, pill, block, dropdown-icon-type, side, align and optically-align controls, and its check-position is "start" or "none": the checkmark leads every row, and "none" leaves it out. A clearable select leads its list with a None row instead of a clear button.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[290px]">
  <Field :label="multiple ? 'Fruits' : 'Fruit'">
    <Select
      v-if="multiple"
      key="multi"
      v-model:value="fruits"
      :options="fruitsOptions"
      name="fruits"
      multiple
      placeholder="Select fruits..."
      :variant="variant"
      :size="size"
      :pill="pill"
      :block="block"
      :dropdown-icon-type="dropdownIconType"
      :check-position="checkPosition"
      :side="side"
      :align="block ? undefined : align"
      :list-min-width="block ? undefined : 240"
      :optically-align="variant === 'ghost' && opticallyAlign !== 'none' ? opticallyAlign : undefined"
      :clearable="clearable"
      :loading="loading"
      :disabled="disabled"
    >
      <template #triggerView="{ values, selectedAll }">{{ selectedAll ? "All fruits" : values.length === 1 ? values[0].label : values.length + " fruits" }}</template>
    </Select>
    <Select
      v-else
      key="single"
      v-model:value="fruit"
      :options="fruitsOptions"
      name="fruits"
      :multiple="false"
      placeholder="Select a fruit..."
      :variant="variant"
      :size="size"
      :pill="pill"
      :block="block"
      :dropdown-icon-type="dropdownIconType"
      :check-position="checkPosition"
      :side="side"
      :align="block ? undefined : align"
      :list-min-width="block ? undefined : 240"
      :optically-align="variant === 'ghost' && opticallyAlign !== 'none' ? opticallyAlign : undefined"
      :clearable="clearable"
      :loading="loading"
      :disabled="disabled"
    />
  </Field>
</div>

<!-- Phone: the select is the value of a row; a clearable one leads its list with None. -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row :label="multiple ? 'Fruits' : 'Fruit'">
    <Select
      v-if="multiple"
      key="multi"
      v-model:value="fruits"
      :options="fruitsOptions"
      name="fruits"
      multiple
      placeholder="None"
      :block="false"
      align="end"
      :check-position="checkPosition"
      :clearable="clearable"
      :loading="loading"
      :disabled="disabled"
    >
      <template #triggerView="{ values, selectedAll }">{{ selectedAll ? "All fruits" : values.length === 1 ? values[0].label : values.length + " fruits" }}</template>
    </Select>
    <Select
      v-else
      key="single"
      v-model:value="fruit"
      :options="fruitsOptions"
      name="fruits"
      :multiple="false"
      placeholder="None"
      :block="false"
      align="end"
      :check-position="checkPosition"
      :clearable="clearable"
      :loading="loading"
      :disabled="disabled"
    />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### With descriptions
Options carrying a description field render a secondary line under each label. A description is a string, a VNode or a render function; here each role's is a render function returning {''}. The title stays semibold in every state (idle, highlighted, selected): the component handles that automatically, no option-class-name needed. Pass a component to TriggerStartIcon to reflect the selected option's identity in the trigger. In a phone row the value stands alone at the row's end, so the row has no trigger icon.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div style="width: 220px">
  <Select
    v-model:value="role"
    :options="roles"
    placeholder="Select role..."
    align="start"
    :list-min-width="260"
    :variant="variant"
    :size="size"
    :check-position="checkPosition"
    :TriggerStartIcon="triggerIcon ? (role === 'owner' ? UserLock : User) : undefined"
  />
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Role">
    <Select
      v-model:value="role"
      :options="phoneRoles"
      :block="false"
      align="end"
    />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Actions
Custom, arbitrary actions can be displayed below the options list via the actions prop. Each action has an id, a label, an optional Icon component and className, and an onSelect callback.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div style="width: 150px">
  <Select
    variant="soft"
    v-model:value="value"
    block
    :options="items"
    placeholder="Select..."
    align="start"
    :list-min-width="240"
    :actions="[
      { id: 'create', label: 'Create project', Icon: Plus, onSelect: () => {} },
      { id: 'overview', label: 'Organization overview', Icon: Workspace, onSelect: () => {} },
    ]"
  />
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Project">
    <Select
      v-model:value="value"
      :block="false"
      :options="items"
      placeholder="None"
      align="end"
      :actions="[
        { id: 'create', label: 'Create project', Icon: Plus, onSelect: () => {} },
        { id: 'overview', label: 'Organization overview', Icon: Workspace, onSelect: () => {} },
      ]"
    />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Grouped options
Select supports option groups, similar to the native &lt;optgroup&gt; element, and per-option tooltips. Each group is announced as a group named by its label. All selects are searchable by default, but will only present a searchbox when it makes sense. Currently, this means when there are more than 15 options. On a phone the list is an iOS menu, which has no search: a long list scrolls.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div style="width: 200px">
  <Select
    variant="outline"
    v-model:value="value"
    side="bottom"
    size="lg"
    :options="groupedItems"
    :list-min-width="300"
    search-placeholder="Select an option..."
    clearable
  />
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Plan">
    <Select
      v-model:value="value"
      :block="false"
      align="end"
      :options="groupedItems"
      search-placeholder="Select an option..."
    />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

## Select Control
The button of a Select on its own, for a list of your own, with variants, sizes, start and dropdown icons, clearable, loading and invalid states.
Import: import { SelectControl } from "@plexui/vue/components/SelectControl"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/select-control
Props:
  - selected (boolean) default: false — Whether a value is selected (affects placeholder styling)
  - variant ("soft" | "outline" | "ghost") default: "outline" — Visual variant
  - size (ControlSize) default: "md" — Control size (3xs to 3xl)
  - pill (boolean) default: false — Fully rounded trigger
  - StartIcon (Component) — Icon at start
  - @clear-click (() => void) — Enables the clear button when set (a function prop, onClearClick)
  - interact-on-enter (boolean) default: false — Enter interacts too, as on a combobox button. Off by default, as Enter may be an attempt to submit a form
  - @interact (() => void) — Called on pointer down, ArrowDown, ArrowUp and Space (a function prop, onInteract). Without it, a wrapping trigger opens on pointer down
  - dropdown-icon-type ("chevronDown" | "dropdown" | "none") default: "dropdown" — Dropdown indicator style
  - invalid (boolean) default: false — Invalid state
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable control
  - block (boolean) default: false — Full width
  - loading (boolean) default: false — Loading state (aria-busy); hides the indicators and ignores interaction
  - optically-align ("start" | "end") — Optical alignment
  - class (string) — Applied to the control

### Overview
Use variant to stylize the control: its styles are like those of {""}, with the small differences of {""}, and ghost has no frame. size sets the control's height, font size, gutter and the placement of its icon and indicator, on the 9-step scale of Button, Input, Select and SegmentedControl; pill rounds it fully and widens its gutter, and its clear action is a pill too. Pass a component to StartIcon to put an icon before the value, sized, spaced and colored by the control, and dropdown-icon-type picks the indicator. selected says the control holds a value and turns off the placeholder styling. It does nothing to the content, which is up to you, so here it follows the value: pick a period, or clear it. Listen to @clear-click (or pass on-clear-click) to make the control clearable: the clear action shows while it is selected. During loading the dropdown icon and clear action are hidden, the control is marked aria-busy and it ignores pointer and keyboard interaction; add disabled to dim it as well. invalid marks the control invalid; pair it with the field's error-message. disabled disables it both visually and accessibly. block stretches it across its container, here the 290px field. optically-align offsets the control by its gutter: "start" puts the start of a ghost control, its icon or its text, on the label's edge, and "end" puts the end of a block one, its indicator, on the field's end. A framed control keeps its gutter, so the example offers the setting for ghost only, and "end" only with block: a narrower control does not reach the field's end. The control's own aria-labelledby names it by the label and the value, as Select's trigger is named.On Phone the control is the value of a row in a FieldGroup: the value and its up-down chevrons sit at the row's end, as an iOS pop-up button. The row sets its height, insets, type and colors, so the Phone preview has no variant, size, pill, StartIcon, dropdownIconType, block and opticallyAlign controls. A selected value takes the secondary label color (the primary one in a group with columns), a placeholder the tertiary one. A clearable control unsets its value with the menu's None row, as iOS pop-ups do, rather than a clear button. A row has no frame to color, so the error is the row's error-message line.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[290px]">
  <Field label="Period" :error-message="invalid ? 'No data for this period.' : undefined">
    <template #default="control">
      <Menu>
        <MenuTrigger>
          <SelectControl
            v-bind="control"
            :aria-labelledby="control['aria-labelledby'] + ' ' + control.id + '-value'"
            :class="block ? undefined : 'w-[200px]'"
            :variant="variant"
            :size="size"
            :pill="pill"
            :block="block"
            :optically-align="variant === 'ghost' && opticallyAlign !== 'none' ? opticallyAlign : undefined"
            :StartIcon="startIcon ? CalendarAlt : undefined"
            :dropdown-icon-type="dropdownIconType"
            :selected="!!value"
            :on-clear-click="clearable ? () => (value = '') : undefined"
            :loading="loading"
            :invalid="invalid"
            :disabled="disabled"
          ><span :id="control.id + '-value'">{{ value || "Select date..." }}</span></SelectControl>
        </MenuTrigger>
        <MenuContent :min-width="200" :width="block ? 290 : 200">
          <MenuRadioGroup v-model:value="value">
            <MenuRadioItem value="Today">Today</MenuRadioItem>
            <MenuRadioItem value="Last week">Last week</MenuRadioItem>
            <MenuRadioItem value="Last month">Last month</MenuRadioItem>
            <MenuRadioItem value="Last 3 months">Last 3 months</MenuRadioItem>
          </MenuRadioGroup>
        </MenuContent>
      </Menu>
    </template>
  </Field>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Period" :error-message="invalid ? 'No data for this period.' : undefined">
    <template #default="control">
      <Menu>
        <MenuTrigger>
          <SelectControl
            v-bind="control"
            :aria-labelledby="control['aria-labelledby'] + ' ' + control.id + '-value'"
            :selected="!!value"
            :loading="loading"
            :invalid="invalid"
            :disabled="disabled"
          ><span :id="control.id + '-value'">{{ value || "None" }}</span></SelectControl>
        </MenuTrigger>
        <MenuContent :min-width="200" align="end">
          <MenuRadioGroup v-model:value="value">
            <MenuRadioItem v-if="clearable" value="">None</MenuRadioItem>
            <MenuRadioItem value="Today">Today</MenuRadioItem>
            <MenuRadioItem value="Last week">Last week</MenuRadioItem>
            <MenuRadioItem value="Last month">Last month</MenuRadioItem>
            <MenuRadioItem value="Last 3 months">Last 3 months</MenuRadioItem>
          </MenuRadioGroup>
        </MenuContent>
      </Menu>
    </template>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Country selector
A plain country selector built with Select. The #triggerView and #optionView slots show flag icons with country names, no dial codes. Useful for profile forms, shipping addresses, and other country-only fields. countries holds {"{ value, label }"} options, and FlagIcon renders the flag image for a country code. The search box appears on its own because the list has more than 15 options; there is no prop for it.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Select
    v-model:value="country"
    :options="countries"
    placeholder="Select country..."
    :size="size"
    :variant="variant"
    :pill="pill"
    block
    search-placeholder="Search countries..."
    :list-min-width="300"
    :option-class-name="countryOptionClass"
  >
    <template #triggerView="{ value, label }">
      <span style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; height: 100%; overflow: hidden; white-space: nowrap"><component :is="FlagIcon" :code="value" trigger />{{ label }}</span>
    </template>
    <template #optionView="{ value, label }">
      <span :class="countryLabelStyles.Root"><component :is="FlagIcon" :code="value" /><span :class="countryLabelStyles.Text">{{ label }}</span></span>
    </template>
  </Select>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Country">
    <Select
      v-model:value="country"
      :options="countries"
      :block="false"
      align="end"
      search-placeholder="Search countries..."
      :list-min-width="300"
      :option-class-name="countryOptionClass"
    >
      <template #optionView="{ value, label }">
        <span :class="countryLabelStyles.Root"><component :is="FlagIcon" :code="value" /><span :class="countryLabelStyles.Text">{{ label }}</span></span>
      </template>
    </Select>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Country code selector
A searchable country selector built with Select. The #triggerView and #optionView slots render flag icons and dial codes. A search-predicate matches both country name and dial code.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="w-[320px]">
  <Select
    v-model:value="country"
    :options="countryOptions"
    placeholder="Select country..."
    :size="size"
    :variant="variant"
    :pill="pill"
    block
    search-placeholder="Search countries..."
    :search-predicate="countrySearchPredicate"
    :list-min-width="300"
    :option-class-name="countryOptionClass"
  >
    <template #triggerView="{ value, label }">
      <span v-if="countryOf(value)" style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; height: 100%; overflow: hidden; white-space: nowrap"><component :is="FlagIcon" :code="value" trigger />{{ countryOf(value).label }} ({{ countryOf(value).dialCode }})</span>
      <template v-else>{{ label }}</template>
    </template>
    <template #optionView="{ value, label }">
      <span :class="countryLabelStyles.Root"><component :is="FlagIcon" :code="value" /><span :class="countryLabelStyles.Text">{{ label }}</span></span>
    </template>
  </Select>
</div>

<!-- Phone -->
<FieldGroup>
  <Field row label="Country">
    <Select
      v-model:value="country"
      :options="countryOptions"
      :block="false"
      align="end"
      search-placeholder="Search countries..."
      :search-predicate="countrySearchPredicate"
      :list-min-width="300"
      :option-class-name="countryOptionClass"
    >
      <template #optionView="{ value, label }">
        <span :class="countryLabelStyles.Root"><component :is="FlagIcon" :code="value" /><span :class="countryLabelStyles.Text">{{ label }}</span></span>
      </template>
    </Select>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Phone form
Country selector with flag icons and phone input composed into a form. Selecting a country updates the dial code prefix, clears the phone field and focuses it. Phone numbers are formatted as you type using per-country patterns (e.g. 123 456 7890 for US, 1234 567 890 for UK). Search works by both country name and dial code. Validation runs on submit: the phone field shows an error state with FieldError when the number is too short or too long.
```vue
<Page surface="grouped" class="h-dvh" data-form-screen style="--page-content-padding-top:10px; --page-content-padding-bottom:16px; --page-footer-gutter:24px; --page-footer-padding-bottom:max(24px, env(safe-area-inset-bottom, 0px))">
  <Toolbar>
    <ToolbarLeading><DemoBackButton /></ToolbarLeading>
    <ToolbarTitle>Phone number</ToolbarTitle>
  </Toolbar>
  <PageContent data-form-content>
    <form ref="screenForm" class="flex flex-col gap-6" @submit.prevent="submitted = true" novalidate>
      <FieldGroup title="Phone number" description="Choose your country and enter the number you want to use." columns>
  <Field row label="Country">
    <Select v-model:value="country" :options="countryOptions" search-placeholder="Search countries..."
      :search-predicate="countrySearchPredicate" :list-min-width="300" :option-class-name="countryOptionClass"
      @change="() => { phone = ''; submitted = false; focusLater($refs.phoneInput) }">
      <template #optionView="{ value, label }"><span class="inline-flex items-center gap-2"><component :is="FlagIcon" :code="value" />{{ label }}</span></template>
    </Select>
  </Field>
  <Field row label="Phone" :error-message="submitted && !isPhoneValid(phone) ? 'Phone number is not valid.' : undefined">
    <Input ref="phoneInput" :value="phone" @update:value="(raw) => { phone = formatPhoneNumber(raw, country); submitted = false }"
      placeholder="Phone number" type="tel" inputmode="tel" autocomplete="tel-national">
      <template #startAdornment><span class="text-tertiary whitespace-nowrap select-none mr-2">{{ countryOf(country)?.dialCode ?? '+1' }}</span></template>
    </Input>
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
    </form>
  </PageContent>
  <PageFooter data-form-footer><Button color="primary" size="3xl" pill block @click="$refs.screenForm.requestSubmit()">Continue</Button></PageFooter>
  <Toolbar placement="bottom"><ToolbarContent fill><Button color="primary" pill block @click="$refs.screenForm.requestSubmit()">Continue</Button></ToolbarContent></Toolbar>
</Page>
```

## Separator
A thin divider between content, horizontal or vertical, decorative by default or exposed to assistive technology when it marks a real grouping.
Import: import { Separator } from "@plexui/vue/components/Separator"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/separator
Props:
  - orientation ("horizontal" | "vertical") default: "horizontal" — Direction of the divider
  - decorative (boolean) default: true — When true, the separator is ignored by assistive technologies. Set false when the divider carries semantic grouping (renders role="separator").
  - class (string) — Applied to the root <div>

### Overview
```vue
<div style="display: flex; max-width: 384px; flex-direction: column; gap: 16px; font-size: var(--font-text-sm-size); line-height: var(--font-text-sm-line-height)">
  <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 2px; line-height: 1.3">
    <div style="font-weight: 500">Aisha Cooper</div>
    <div style="color: var(--color-text-tertiary)">Engineering · Platform team</div>
  </div>
  <Separator />
  <div>Shipped 24 pull requests this week across CI/CD and developer tooling.</div>
</div>
```

### Vertical
Use orientation="vertical" between inline items. Works for meta-strips, breadcrumbs, and dot-less separators.
```vue
<div style="display: flex; height: 20px; align-items: center; gap: 16px; font-size: var(--font-text-sm-size)">
  <div>MIT license</div>
  <Separator orientation="vertical" />
  <div>12k downloads</div>
  <Separator orientation="vertical" />
  <div>48 kB bundle</div>
</div>
```

### Menu
Vertical separators between menu items with a short caption underneath each label.
```vue
<div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 16px; font-size: var(--font-text-sm-size)">
  <template v-for="(plan, i) in [
    { name: 'Starter', meta: 'Free · 1 project' },
    { name: 'Pro', meta: '$19/mo · Unlimited' },
    { name: 'Enterprise', meta: 'Custom · Priority SLA' },
  ]" :key="plan.name">
    <Separator v-if="i > 0" orientation="vertical" />
    <div style="display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 2px; line-height: 1.2">
      <span style="font-weight: 500">{{ plan.name }}</span>
      <span style="font-size: var(--font-text-xs-size); color: var(--color-text-tertiary)">{{ plan.meta }}</span>
    </div>
  </template>
</div>
```

### List
Horizontal separators between rows in a definition list.
```vue
<div style="display: flex; width: 100%; max-width: 384px; flex-direction: column; gap: 8px; font-size: var(--font-text-sm-size); line-height: var(--font-text-sm-line-height)">
  <template v-for="(row, i) in [
    ['Version', '0.9.0'],
    ['Published', '2 days ago'],
    ['Bundle', '48 kB'],
    ['License', 'MIT'],
  ]" :key="row[0]">
    <Separator v-if="i > 0" />
    <dl style="display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; margin: 0">
      <dt>{{ row[0] }}</dt>
      <dd style="margin: 0; color: var(--color-text-tertiary)">{{ row[1] }}</dd>
    </dl>
  </template>
</div>
```

## Shimmer Text
Text with a shimmer animation for loading and in-progress states, always on or toggled with a graceful stop.
Import: import { ShimmerText, ShimmerableText } from "@plexui/vue/components/ShimmerText"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/shimmer-text
Props:
  - as ("p" | "span" | "h1" | "h2" | "h3" | "h4" | "h5" | "h6" | "div") default: "div" — HTML element to render
  - default slot (VNode[]) — Text content to shimmer
  - class (string) — Additional CSS classes
  ShimmerableText:
    - as ("p" | "span" | "h1" | "h2" | "h3" | "h4" | "h5" | "h6" | "div") default: "div" — HTML element to render
    - shimmer (boolean) default: false — Whether the shimmer animation is active
    - default slot (VNode[]) — Text content to shimmer
    - class (string) — Additional CSS classes

### Overview
ShimmerText applies a continuous shimmer animation to text content. Useful for loading states, AI-generated content indicators, or visual emphasis.
```vue
<ShimmerText class="text-lg font-semibold">
  Thinking...
</ShimmerText>
```

### Semantic elements
Use the as prop to render as any text element: p, span, h1 to h6, or div.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-4">
  <ShimmerText as="h2" class="text-2xl font-bold">Heading shimmer</ShimmerText>
  <ShimmerText as="p" class="text-base">Paragraph shimmer effect</ShimmerText>
  <ShimmerText as="span" class="text-sm text-secondary">Inline shimmer</ShimmerText>
</div>
```

### Controllable shimmer
ShimmerableText allows toggling the shimmer on and off. When shimmer is set to false, the animation finishes its current iteration and then pauses gracefully, with no abrupt cut. Use this variant when text transitions between a loading and idle state.
```vue
<ShimmerableText :shimmer="shimmer" class="text-lg font-semibold">
  Generating response...
</ShimmerableText>
```

## Sidebar
Composable navigation sidebar for app shells with collapsible sections, search and filter, tree views with hover actions, and a mobile menu
Import: import { SidebarProvider, SidebarLayout, Sidebar, SidebarHeader, SidebarContent, SidebarFooter, SidebarGroup, SidebarGroupLabel, SidebarMenu, SidebarMenuItem, SidebarMenuButton, SidebarInset, SidebarTrigger } from "@plexui/vue/components/Sidebar"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/sidebar
Props:
  - variant ("sidebar" | "docs") default: "sidebar" — Visual variant
  - side ("left" | "right") default: "left" — Which side the sidebar is on
  SidebarProvider:
    - collapsible ("offcanvas" | "icon" | "none") default: "offcanvas" — Collapse behavior: offcanvas (drawer on mobile, full collapse on desktop), icon (icon rail), or none
    - variant ("sidebar" | "app") default: "sidebar" — Look of the whole shell. "app" is the sidebar of a product: 260px, pill rows on a grey ground, a page with no inset, and on a phone a menu under the page
    - default-open (boolean) default: true — Initial open state (uncontrolled)
    - open (boolean) — Controlled open state. Bind with v-model:open
    - @update:open ((open: boolean) => void) — Emitted when the open state changes; powers v-model:open. Without open the provider keeps its own state and still emits
    - default-open-mobile (boolean) default: false — Initial open state of the phone drawer (uncontrolled)
    - open-mobile (boolean) — Controlled open state of the phone drawer. Bind with v-model:open-mobile
    - @update:open-mobile ((open: boolean) => void) — Emitted when the phone drawer opens or closes: its menu button, its backdrop, Escape or a swipe; powers v-model:open-mobile. Without open-mobile the provider keeps its own state and still emits
    - class (string) — Class of the provider's wrapper
    - style (StyleValue) — Style of the provider's wrapper, e.g. --sidebar-width
    - default slot ({ state, open, setOpen, openMobile, setOpenMobile, isMobile, collapsible, toggleSidebar }) — Receives the sidebar context, the object useSidebar() returns
  SidebarGroup:
    - default-open (boolean) default: true — Whether the group is open initially. A closed group hides its SidebarGroupContent
    - open (boolean) — Controlled open state, also exposed as data-state. The group has no toggle of its own: change open from your own control
  SidebarGroupLabel:
    - size ("sm" | "lg") default: "lg" — sm: compact, tertiary. lg: larger, primary, more spacing
    - as-child (boolean) default: false — Render the single child element as the label
  SidebarMenuItem:
    - expanded (boolean) — Marks the item expanded, which turns its SidebarMenuChevron. Pair it with open on its SidebarMenuSub
  SidebarMenuButton:
    - is-active (boolean) default: false — Highlight the current page (aria-current="page")
    - tooltip (string) — Label shown on hover when collapsed (icon mode). Hidden on mobile. The tooltip slot takes rich content
    - as-child (boolean) default: false — Render the single child element (e.g. a RouterLink) as the button. The button attributes go on the child and its content is wrapped like the button's
  SidebarMenuSub:
    - open (boolean) default: true — Whether submenu is expanded. A closed submenu is inert, so its links and buttons leave the tab order and the accessibility tree
    - has-icons (boolean) default: false — Align child items with parent text that has icons
  SidebarMenuSubButton:
    - is-active (boolean) default: false — Highlight the current page (aria-current="page")
    - indent (0 | 1 | 2 | 3) default: 0 — Nesting level; each level adds 12px of left margin
    - as-child (boolean) default: false — Render the single child element (e.g. a RouterLink) as the button
  SidebarMobile:
    - contained (boolean) default: false — Positions the drawer and backdrop absolute inside a frame instead of fixed to the window
    - disclosure (boolean) default: true — Rows that lead to another page end with a disclosure chevron, as in an iOS list. false leaves it out
  SidebarCard:
    - variant ("default" | "elevated") default: "default" — Visual variant
    - dismissible (boolean) default: false — Shows dismiss button
    - @dismiss ((event: MouseEvent) => void) — Emitted when the dismiss button is clicked

### App Shell
A multi-section app shell with a workspace selector, expandable parent sections with sub-items, and a footer with a documentation link plus a user menu. Inbox carries a quiet count Badge, as the inbox of a platform does. When collapsed (icon mode), hovering a parent icon opens a Popover flyout listing the section&apos;s items so users keep one-click access without expanding the rail.
```vue
<component :is="ResponsiveSidebarProvider"
  v-model:open="open"
  v-slot="sidebar"
  :class="['h-full console-demo', { 'demo-sidebar-flat': !bordered }]"
  collapsible="icon"
>
  <SidebarLayout class="h-full">
    <component :is="ResponsiveSidebar">
      <SidebarHeader v-if="!sidebar.isMobile">
        <span class="demo-sidebar-wordmark">Console</span>
        <Tooltip v-if="sidebar.collapsible !== 'none'" compact>
          <SidebarTrigger />
          <template #content>
            <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
              {{ sidebar.state === 'collapsed' ? 'Expand' : 'Collapse' }}
              <kbd class="console-trigger-kbd">⌘B</kbd>
            </span>
          </template>
        </Tooltip>
      </SidebarHeader>

      <SidebarContent class="console-content">
        <!-- Workspace selector -->
        <SidebarGroup class="console-group">
          <SidebarGroupContent>
            <SidebarMenu>
              <SidebarMenuItem>
                <Menu>
                  <MenuTrigger>
                    <SidebarMenuButton class="console-menu-button" tooltip="Default">
                      <SidebarMenuButtonIcon><Cube /></SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                      <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>Default</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                      <ChevronUpDown class="ml-auto console-workspace-chevron" style="width: 14px; height: 14px" />
                    </SidebarMenuButton>
                  </MenuTrigger>
                  <MenuContent :min-width="200" align="start">
                    <MenuItem @select="() => {}">Default</MenuItem>
                    <MenuItem @select="() => {}">Production</MenuItem>
                    <MenuItem @select="() => {}">Staging</MenuItem>
                    <MenuSeparator />
                    <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><Plus /> New workspace</MenuItem>
                  </MenuContent>
                </Menu>
              </SidebarMenuItem>
            </SidebarMenu>
          </SidebarGroupContent>
        </SidebarGroup>

        <SidebarGroup class="console-group">
          <SidebarGroupContent>
            <SidebarMenu>
              <template v-for="section in consoleSections" :key="section.id">
                <!-- Collapsed rail: hovering a parent opens a flyout with its items -->
                <SidebarMenuItem v-if="section.items && sidebar.state === 'collapsed' && !sidebar.isMobile">
                  <Popover show-on-hover :hover-open-delay="80">
                    <PopoverTrigger>
                      <SidebarMenuButton class="console-menu-button">
                        <SidebarMenuButtonIcon><component :is="section.icon" /></SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                        <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>{{ section.label }}</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                        <SidebarMenuBadge v-if="section.badge">
                          <Badge size="sm" color="info">{{ section.badge }}</Badge>
                        </SidebarMenuBadge>
                        <SidebarMenuChevron class="ml-auto transition-transform" />
                      </SidebarMenuButton>
                    </PopoverTrigger>
                    <PopoverContent side="right" :side-offset="8" :min-width="220" align="start" :auto-focus="false">
                      <div class="p-1">
                        <div class="px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-tertiary">{{ section.label }}</div>
                        <button
                          v-for="item in section.items"
                          :key="item.id"
                          type="button"
                          :data-active="active === item.id ? 'true' : undefined"
                          class="flex items-center w-full text-left rounded-md px-3 py-1.5 text-sm text-primary cursor-pointer bg-transparent border-0 appearance-none select-none transition-colors hover:bg-[var(--sidebar-accent)] data-[active=true]:bg-[var(--sidebar-accent)] data-[active=true]:text-[var(--sidebar-menu-text-active)] focus:outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-[var(--color-ring)]"
                          @click="active = item.id"
                        >{{ item.label }}</button>
                      </div>
                    </PopoverContent>
                  </Popover>
                </SidebarMenuItem>

                <!-- Expanded: the parent toggles its sub items; a row without items opens its page -->
                <SidebarMenuItem v-else :expanded="!!expanded[section.id]">
                  <SidebarMenuButton
                    class="console-menu-button"
                    :is-active="!section.items && active === section.id"
                    :tooltip="section.items ? undefined : section.label"
                    @click="section.items ? (expanded[section.id] = !expanded[section.id]) : (active = section.id)"
                  >
                    <SidebarMenuButtonIcon><component :is="section.icon" /></SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                    <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>{{ section.label }}</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                    <SidebarMenuBadge v-if="section.badge">
                      <Badge size="sm" color="info">{{ section.badge }}</Badge>
                    </SidebarMenuBadge>
                    <SidebarMenuBadge v-if="section.count">
                      <Badge size="sm" color="secondary">{{ section.count }}</Badge>
                    </SidebarMenuBadge>
                    <SidebarMenuChevron
                      v-if="section.items"
                      class="ml-auto transition-transform"
                      :style="{ transform: expanded[section.id] ? 'rotate(90deg)' : undefined }"
                    />
                  </SidebarMenuButton>
                  <SidebarMenuSub v-if="section.items" :open="!!expanded[section.id]" has-icons>
                    <SidebarMenuSubItem v-for="item in section.items" :key="item.id">
                      <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="1" :is-active="active === item.id" @click="active = item.id">{{ item.label }}</SidebarMenuSubButton>
                    </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                  </SidebarMenuSub>
                </SidebarMenuItem>
              </template>
            </SidebarMenu>
          </SidebarGroupContent>
        </SidebarGroup>
      </SidebarContent>

      <SidebarFooter class="console-footer">
        <SidebarMenu>
          <SidebarMenuItem>
            <SidebarMenuButton class="console-menu-button" tooltip="Documentation">
              <SidebarMenuButtonIcon><Book /></SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
              <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>Documentation</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
            </SidebarMenuButton>
          </SidebarMenuItem>
          <SidebarSeparator class="console-footer-separator" />
          <SidebarMenuItem>
            <Menu>
              <MenuTrigger>
                <SidebarMenuButton class="console-menu-button console-account-button" tooltip="Alex · Acme Inc">
                  <SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                    <Avatar name="Alex" :size="24" color="secondary" variant="solid" />
                  </SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                  <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                    <span class="flex flex-col leading-tight">
                      <span class="font-medium text-primary text-sm">Alex</span>
                      <span class="text-tertiary text-xs">Admin</span>
                    </span>
                  </SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                  <ChevronDownMd class="ml-auto" style="width: 14px; height: 14px" />
                </SidebarMenuButton>
              </MenuTrigger>
              <MenuContent :min-width="260" align="start" side="top">
                <MenuItem disabled>
                  <span class="text-tertiary text-sm">alex@example.com</span>
                </MenuItem>
                <MenuItem @select="() => {}">
                  <BuildingWorkspace /> Acme Inc
                  <span class="ml-auto text-xs text-tertiary">API plan</span>
                </MenuItem>
                <MenuSeparator />
                <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><SettingsCog /> Organization settings</MenuItem>
                <MenuSeparator />
                <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><Chat /> Feedback</MenuItem>
                <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><Help /> Get help</MenuItem>
                <MenuItem @select="() => {}">
                  <InfoCircle /> Legal center
                  <ChevronRightMd class="ml-auto" />
                </MenuItem>
                <MenuSeparator />
                <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><Logout /> Log out</MenuItem>
              </MenuContent>
            </Menu>
          </SidebarMenuItem>
        </SidebarMenu>
      </SidebarFooter>
    </component>

    <component :is="SidebarDemoPage" :title="consoleSections.flatMap((s) => [s, ...(s.items ?? [])]).find((i) => i.id === active)?.label ?? 'Workbench'">
        <p class="text-secondary">
          Hover the icons when collapsed to reveal a flyout with the section's items.
        </p>
      </component>
  </SidebarLayout>
</component>
```

### App
The sidebar of a product: 260px, pill rows on a grey ground and a page with no inset. Rows keep their place as it folds, the labels fade, and along the rail the next tooltip opens at once. A Badge in SidebarMenuBadge sits as far from the row's edge as from its top and bottom, and its digits end where plain counts and the chevron end; in the folded rail it becomes a dot of its color above the icon, while plain text such as a count fades. A group lists its items in a Menu beside the icon when folded. The person row in the footer is 40px tall, so the round end of its highlight is concentric with the avatar on the icons' axis. In these examples, the phone toolbar opens the same navigation tree in a full-screen drawer. Set --sidebar-menu-sub-max-height to a group's real height for an even fold.
```vue
<component :is="ResponsiveSidebarProvider" v-model:open="open" v-slot="sidebar" class="h-full" collapsible="icon" variant="app">
  <SidebarLayout class="h-full">
    <component :is="ResponsiveSidebar">
      <SidebarHeader v-if="!sidebar.isMobile">
        <span class="demo-sidebar-wordmark">App</span>
        <Tooltip v-if="sidebar.collapsible !== 'none'" compact>
          <SidebarTrigger />
          <template #content>
            <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
              {{ sidebar.state === 'collapsed' ? 'Expand' : 'Collapse' }}
              <kbd class="console-trigger-kbd">⌘B</kbd>
            </span>
          </template>
        </Tooltip>
      </SidebarHeader>
      <SidebarContent>
        <SidebarMenu>
          <SidebarMenuItem v-for="row in appTopRows" :key="row.id">
            <SidebarMenuButton :is-active="active === row.id" :tooltip="row.label" @click="active = row.id">
              <SidebarMenuButtonIcon><component :is="row.icon" /></SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
              <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>{{ row.label }}</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
              <SidebarMenuBadge v-if="row.alert">
                <Badge color="danger" variant="solid" pill>{{ row.alert }}</Badge>
              </SidebarMenuBadge>
              <SidebarMenuBadge v-if="row.count">{{ row.count }}</SidebarMenuBadge>
            </SidebarMenuButton>
          </SidebarMenuItem>

          <!-- Collapsed rail: the group's items in a menu beside its icon -->
          <SidebarMenuItem v-if="sidebar.state === 'collapsed' && !sidebar.isMobile">
            <Menu>
              <MenuTrigger>
                <SidebarMenuButton :is-active="appDocumentItems.some((item) => item.id === active)" aria-label="Documents">
                  <SidebarMenuButtonIcon><FileDocument /></SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                  <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>Documents</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                </SidebarMenuButton>
              </MenuTrigger>
              <MenuContent side="right" align="start" :min-width="220">
                <MenuCheckboxItem
                  v-for="item in appDocumentItems"
                  :key="item.id"
                  :checked="item.id === active"
                  indicator-position="start"
                  indicator-variant="ghost"
                  @select="active = item.id"
                >
                  {{ item.label }}
                  <span class="ml-auto text-xs text-secondary tabular-nums">{{ item.count }}</span>
                </MenuCheckboxItem>
              </MenuContent>
            </Menu>
          </SidebarMenuItem>

          <!-- Expanded: the group opens in place, each item with its count -->
          <SidebarMenuItem v-else :expanded="documentsOpen">
            <SidebarMenuButton
              :is-active="!documentsOpen && appDocumentItems.some((item) => item.id === active)"
              :aria-expanded="documentsOpen"
              @click="documentsOpen = !documentsOpen"
            >
              <SidebarMenuButtonIcon><FileDocument /></SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
              <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>Documents</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
              <SidebarMenuChevron />
            </SidebarMenuButton>
            <SidebarMenuSub :open="documentsOpen" has-icons>
              <SidebarMenuSubItem v-for="item in appDocumentItems" :key="item.id">
                <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="1" :is-active="active === item.id" @click="active = item.id">
                  {{ item.label }}
                  <SidebarMenuBadge>{{ item.count }}</SidebarMenuBadge>
                </SidebarMenuSubButton>
              </SidebarMenuSubItem>
            </SidebarMenuSub>
          </SidebarMenuItem>

          <SidebarMenuItem v-for="row in appBottomRows" :key="row.id">
            <SidebarMenuButton :is-active="active === row.id" :tooltip="row.label" @click="active = row.id">
              <SidebarMenuButtonIcon><component :is="row.icon" /></SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
              <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>{{ row.label }}</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
              <SidebarMenuBadge v-if="row.alert">
                <Badge color="danger" variant="solid" pill>{{ row.alert }}</Badge>
              </SidebarMenuBadge>
              <SidebarMenuBadge v-if="row.count">{{ row.count }}</SidebarMenuBadge>
            </SidebarMenuButton>
          </SidebarMenuItem>
        </SidebarMenu>
      </SidebarContent>

      <SidebarFooter>
        <SidebarMenu>
          <SidebarMenuItem>
            <Menu>
              <MenuTrigger>
                <SidebarMenuButton tooltip="Sam Lee">
                  <SidebarMenuButtonIcon><Avatar name="Sam Lee" :size="24" color="secondary" variant="solid" /></SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                  <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>Sam Lee</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                </SidebarMenuButton>
              </MenuTrigger>
              <MenuContent side="top" align="start" :min-width="220">
                <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><Members /> Account</MenuItem>
                <MenuSeparator />
                <MenuItem @select="() => {}"><Logout /> Sign out</MenuItem>
              </MenuContent>
            </Menu>
          </SidebarMenuItem>
        </SidebarMenu>
      </SidebarFooter>
    </component>

    <component :is="SidebarDemoPage" :title="appLabels.find((item) => item.id === active)?.label">
          <p class="text-secondary">Fold the sidebar: the Notifications badge turns into a dot above its icon, the counts fade.</p>
        </component>
  </SidebarLayout>
</component>
```

### Basic
```vue
<component :is="ResponsiveSidebarProvider"
  :key="collapsible + '-' + icons"
  v-slot="sidebar"
  :class="['h-full', { 'demo-sidebar-flat': !bordered }]"
  :collapsible="icons ? collapsible : 'none'"
>
  <SidebarLayout class="h-full">
    <component :is="ResponsiveSidebar">
      <SidebarHeader v-if="!sidebar.isMobile">
        <span class="demo-sidebar-wordmark">App</span>
        <Tooltip v-if="sidebar.collapsible !== 'none'" compact>
          <SidebarTrigger />

            <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
              {{ sidebar.state === 'collapsed' ? 'Expand' : 'Collapse' }}

            </span>
          </template>
        </Tooltip>
      </SidebarHeader>
      <SidebarContent>

          <SidebarGroupLabel :size="labelSize">{{ group.label }}</SidebarGroupLabel>

                  :is-active="activeItem === item.label"
```

### Collapsible Sections
Build collapsible nested menus with chevron toggles for documentation and API reference sidebars. A parent is a SidebarMenuItem whose SidebarMenuSub holds the next level; expanded on the item turns its SidebarMenuChevron and open on the submenu shows it. Each level of the tree is one v-for.
```vue
<component :is="ResponsiveSidebarProvider" :key="icons ? 'icons' : 'no-icons'" class="h-full" collapsible="none">
  <SidebarLayout class="h-full">
    <component :is="ResponsiveSidebar" :variant="icons ? undefined : 'docs'">
      <SidebarContent>
        <SidebarGroup v-if="icons">
          <SidebarGroupContent>
            <SidebarMenu>
              <SidebarMenuItem v-for="section in iconSections" :key="section.id" :expanded="!!expanded[section.id]">
                <SidebarMenuButton @click="expanded[section.id] = !expanded[section.id]">
                  <SidebarMenuButtonIcon><component :is="section.icon" /></SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                  <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>{{ section.label }}</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                  <SidebarMenuChevron />
                </SidebarMenuButton>
                <SidebarMenuSub :open="!!expanded[section.id]" has-icons>
                  <SidebarMenuSubItem v-for="child in section.items" :key="child.id">
                    <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="1" :is-active="appPage.id === child.id" @click="appPage = child">{{ child.label }}</SidebarMenuSubButton>
                  </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                </SidebarMenuSub>
              </SidebarMenuItem>
            </SidebarMenu>
          </SidebarGroupContent>
        </SidebarGroup>
        <template v-else>
          <SidebarGroup v-for="section in docsSections" :key="section.id">
            <SidebarGroupLabel>{{ section.label }}</SidebarGroupLabel>
            <SidebarGroupContent>
              <SidebarMenu>
                <template v-for="a in section.items" :key="a.id">
                  <SidebarMenuItem v-if="a.items" :expanded="!!expanded[a.id]">
                    <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="0" @click="expanded[a.id] = !expanded[a.id]">
                      {{ a.label }}<SidebarMenuChevron />
                    </SidebarMenuSubButton>
                    <SidebarMenuSub :open="!!expanded[a.id]">
                      <template v-for="b in a.items" :key="b.id">
                        <SidebarMenuItem v-if="b.items" :expanded="!!expanded[b.id]">
                          <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="1" @click="expanded[b.id] = !expanded[b.id]">
                            {{ b.label }}<SidebarMenuChevron />
                          </SidebarMenuSubButton>
                          <SidebarMenuSub :open="!!expanded[b.id]">
                            <template v-for="c in b.items" :key="c.id">
                              <SidebarMenuItem v-if="c.items" :expanded="!!expanded[c.id]">
                                <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="2" @click="expanded[c.id] = !expanded[c.id]">
                                  {{ c.label }}<SidebarMenuChevron />
                                </SidebarMenuSubButton>
                                <SidebarMenuSub :open="!!expanded[c.id]">
                                  <SidebarMenuSubItem v-for="d in c.items" :key="d.id">
                                    <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="3" :is-active="docsPage.id === d.id" @click="docsPage = d">{{ d.label }}</SidebarMenuSubButton>
                                  </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                                </SidebarMenuSub>
                              </SidebarMenuItem>
                              <SidebarMenuSubItem v-else>
                                <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="2" :is-active="docsPage.id === c.id" @click="docsPage = c">{{ c.label }}</SidebarMenuSubButton>
                              </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                            </template>
                          </SidebarMenuSub>
                        </SidebarMenuItem>
                        <SidebarMenuSubItem v-else>
                          <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="1" :is-active="docsPage.id === b.id" @click="docsPage = b">{{ b.label }}</SidebarMenuSubButton>
                        </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                      </template>
                    </SidebarMenuSub>
                  </SidebarMenuItem>
                  <SidebarMenuSubItem v-else>
                    <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="0" :is-active="docsPage.id === a.id" @click="docsPage = a">{{ a.label }}</SidebarMenuSubButton>
                  </SidebarMenuSubItem>
```
