```vue
                </template>
              </SidebarMenu>
            </SidebarGroupContent>
          </SidebarGroup>
        </template>
      </SidebarContent>
    </component>

    <component :is="SidebarDemoPage" :title="(icons ? appPage : docsPage).label">
        <p class="text-secondary">
          Documentation page for {{ (icons ? appPage : docsPage).label }}. This is where the content would be displayed.
        </p>
      </component>
  </SidebarLayout>
</component>
```

### Search and Filter
Combine search and type-based filtering with nested tree navigation. Each item has an icon based on its type. A Menu with checkbox items filters items by type; filterWorkflowTree and flattenByType are plain functions in the setup scope. Create a unique treeId with Vue useId() to connect each disclosure to its sublist.
```vue
<component :is="ResponsiveSidebarProvider" :class="['h-full', { 'demo-sidebar-flat': !bordered }]" collapsible="none">
  <SidebarLayout class="h-full">
    <component :is="ResponsiveSidebar">
      <SidebarHeader>
        <div class="flex items-center gap-2 w-full [&>div:first-child>div]:!p-0" style="padding: 16px 0 8px">
          <div class="flex-1">
            <SidebarInput
              v-model:value="search"
              size="md"
              :pill="false"
              variant="soft"
              placeholder="Search"
              @clear="search = ''"
            />
          </div>
          <Menu>
            <MenuTrigger>
              <Button variant="soft" size="md" color="secondary" :pill="false" aria-label="Filter by type" uniform>
                <FilterBadge v-if="typeFilters.length > 0" />
                <Filter v-else />
              </Button>
            </MenuTrigger>
            <MenuContent min-width="auto" align="end">
              <MenuCheckboxItem
                v-for="option in stepFilterOptions"
                :key="option.value"
                :checked="typeFilters.includes(option.value)"
                indicator-position="end"
                indicator-variant="ghost"
                @update:checked="(checked) => (typeFilters = checked ? [...typeFilters, option.value] : typeFilters.filter((v) => v !== option.value))"
                @select="(event) => event.preventDefault()"
              >{{ option.label }}</MenuCheckboxItem>
            </MenuContent>
          </Menu>
        </div>
      </SidebarHeader>
      <SidebarContent>
        <SidebarGroup>
          <SidebarGroupContent>
            <!-- Filtered by type: a flat list -->
            <SidebarMenu v-if="typeFilters.length > 0">
              <SidebarMenuItem v-for="step in flattenByType(workflowSteps, typeFilters, search)" :key="step.id">
                <SidebarMenuButton :is-active="activeStep === step.id" @click="activeStep = step.id">
                  <SidebarMenuButtonIcon style="--sidebar-icon-size: 16px">
                    <component :is="stepTypes[step.type].icon" />
                  </SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                  <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>{{ step.label }}</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                </SidebarMenuButton>
              </SidebarMenuItem>
            </SidebarMenu>

            <!-- Otherwise the tree, narrowed by the search -->
            <SidebarMenu v-else>
              <template v-for="step in filterWorkflowTree(workflowSteps, search)" :key="step.id">
                <SidebarMenuItem v-if="step.children" :expanded="!!expanded[step.id]">
                  <div class="flex items-center">
                  <SidebarMenuButton
                    class="!w-auto min-w-0 flex-1"
                    :aria-expanded="expand === 'row' ? !!expanded[step.id] : undefined"
                    :aria-controls="expand === 'row' ? treeId + '-' + step.id : undefined"
                    :is-active="activeStep === step.id"
                    @click="expand === 'chevron' ? (activeStep = step.id) : (expanded[step.id] = !expanded[step.id])"
                  >
                    <SidebarMenuButtonIcon style="--sidebar-icon-size: 16px">
                      <component :is="stepTypes[step.type].icon" />
                    </SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                    <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>{{ step.label }}</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                    <SidebarMenuChevron v-if="expand === 'row'" aria-hidden="true" />
                  </SidebarMenuButton>
                  <Button v-if="expand === 'chevron'" variant="ghost" color="secondary" size="xs" uniform
                    class="!size-[26px] max-md:!size-11 [&_svg]:!size-4"
                    :aria-label="(expanded[step.id] ? 'Collapse ' : 'Expand ') + step.label"
                    :aria-expanded="!!expanded[step.id]" :aria-controls="treeId + '-' + step.id"
                    @click="expanded[step.id] = !expanded[step.id]">
                    <ChevronDownMd aria-hidden="true" :style="{ transform: expanded[step.id] ? 'rotate(0deg)' : 'rotate(-90deg)' }" />
                  </Button>
                  </div>
                  <SidebarMenuSub :id="treeId + '-' + step.id" :open="!!expanded[step.id]" has-icons>
                    <SidebarMenuSubItem v-for="child in step.children" :key="child.id">
                      <SidebarMenuSubButton
                        class="gap-2"
                        :indent="1"
                        :is-active="activeStep === child.id"
                        @click="activeStep = child.id"
                      >
                        <span
                          class="inline-flex items-center justify-center [&>svg]:w-full [&>svg]:h-full"
                          style="width: 16px; height: 16px; flex-shrink: 0"
                        >
                          <component :is="stepTypes[child.type].icon" />
                        </span>{{ child.label }}
                      </SidebarMenuSubButton>
                    </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                  </SidebarMenuSub>
                </SidebarMenuItem>
                <SidebarMenuItem v-else>
                  <SidebarMenuButton :is-active="activeStep === step.id" @click="activeStep = step.id">
                    <SidebarMenuButtonIcon style="--sidebar-icon-size: 16px">
                      <component :is="stepTypes[step.type].icon" />
                    </SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                    <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>{{ step.label }}</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                  </SidebarMenuButton>
                </SidebarMenuItem>
              </template>
            </SidebarMenu>
          </SidebarGroupContent>
        </SidebarGroup>
      </SidebarContent>
    </component>

    <component :is="SidebarDemoPage" :title="findStepLabel(activeStep) || 'Start'">
        <p class="text-secondary">
          {{ typeFilters.length > 0 ? 'Filtered results shown as a flat list.' : 'Use the search and filter controls to narrow the navigation tree.' }}
        </p>
      </component>
  </SidebarLayout>
</component>
```

### Tree with Hover Actions
Tree navigation with chevrons on the left and actions on the right. Clicking a row selects the item and its chevron toggles expand/collapse. On hover, a row shows a + menu (top-level rows and nested groups) and nested rows show a drag handle for reordering. The actions sit next to the row button, not inside it, so no button is nested in another.
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
// Local responsive helpers from the Responsive composition example below.
import { ResponsiveSidebarProvider, ResponsiveSidebar, SidebarDemoPage } from "./responsive-sidebar"
import { computed, ref, useId, watch, type Component } from "vue"
import { Button } from "@plexui/vue/components/Button"
import { Menu, MenuCheckboxItem, MenuContent, MenuItem, MenuSeparator, MenuTrigger } from "@plexui/vue/components/Menu"
import {
  Sidebar,
  SidebarContent,
  SidebarGroup,
  SidebarGroupContent,
  SidebarHeader,
  SidebarInput,
  SidebarInset,
  SidebarLayout,
  SidebarMenu,
  SidebarMenuButton,
  SidebarMenuButtonIcon,
  SidebarMenuButtonLabel,
  SidebarMenuItem,
  SidebarMenuSub,
  SidebarMenuSubButton,
  SidebarMenuSubItem,
  SidebarProvider,
} from "@plexui/vue/components/Sidebar"
import {
  // Row types
  Abc, AllProductsExplore, Bolt, ButtonMousePointer, Calendar, CheckboxChecked, CheckCheck, CheckMd,
  Clip, Commit, Desktop, Globe, GroupCheck, GroupCheckCircle, ImageSquare, LinkExternal, MapPin,
  Number123, PanelBottom, Phone, SpacingVertical, SquareAsterisk, SquareDashed, TextAlignStart,
  TextInitial, TextSelect,
  // Header and row actions
  ChevronDownMd, Filter, FilterBadge, GripVertical, ListChevronsDownUp, Plus,
} from "@plexui/vue/components/Icon"

type NodeType =
  | "action"
  | "condition"
  | "module"
  | "screen"
  | "container"
  | "footer"
  | "button"
  | "image"
  | "text"
  | "title"
  | "spacer"
  | "address-input"
  | "checkbox"
  | "checkbox-group"
  | "country-select"
  | "date"
  | "file-upload"
  | "masked-text"
  | "multi-select"
  | "number-input"
  | "phone-number"
  | "radio-group"
  | "select"
  | "text-area"
  | "text-input"
  | "url-input"

type ActionNode = { id: string; label: string; type: NodeType; children?: ActionNode[] }

const nodeTypes: Record<NodeType, { icon: Component; label: string }> = {
  action: { icon: Bolt, label: "Action" },
  condition: { icon: Commit, label: "Condition" },
  module: { icon: AllProductsExplore, label: "Module" },
  screen: { icon: Desktop, label: "Screen" },
  container: { icon: SquareDashed, label: "Container" },
  footer: { icon: PanelBottom, label: "Footer" },
  button: { icon: ButtonMousePointer, label: "Button" },
  image: { icon: ImageSquare, label: "Image" },
  text: { icon: TextAlignStart, label: "Text" },
  title: { icon: TextInitial, label: "Title" },
  spacer: { icon: SpacingVertical, label: "Spacer" },
  "address-input": { icon: MapPin, label: "Address input" },
  checkbox: { icon: CheckboxChecked, label: "Checkbox" },
  "checkbox-group": { icon: GroupCheck, label: "Checkbox group" },
  "country-select": { icon: Globe, label: "Country select" },
  date: { icon: Calendar, label: "Date" },
  "file-upload": { icon: Clip, label: "File upload" },
  "masked-text": { icon: SquareAsterisk, label: "Masked text" },
  "multi-select": { icon: CheckCheck, label: "Multi select" },
  "number-input": { icon: Number123, label: "Number input" },
  "phone-number": { icon: Phone, label: "Phone number" },
  "radio-group": { icon: GroupCheckCircle, label: "Radio group" },
  select: { icon: CheckMd, label: "Select" },
  "text-area": { icon: TextSelect, label: "Text area" },
  "text-input": { icon: Abc, label: "Text input" },
  "url-input": { icon: LinkExternal, label: "URL input" },
}

const filterOptions = (["action", "condition", "module", "screen", "container", "footer"] as NodeType[]).map((type) => ({
  value: type,
  label: nodeTypes[type].label,
}))

const elementTypes: NodeType[] = ["button", "image", "text", "title", "spacer"]
const formTypes: NodeType[] = [
  "address-input", "checkbox", "checkbox-group", "country-select", "date",
  "file-upload", "masked-text", "multi-select", "number-input", "phone-number",
  "radio-group", "select", "text-area", "text-input", "url-input",
]

/** The + menu: a screen adds a container or a footer, any other row adds elements */
const addMenu = (node: ActionNode): NodeType[][] =>
  node.type === "screen" ? [["container", "footer"]] : [elementTypes, formTypes]

const treeId = useId()

// Demo controls
const action = ref<"collapse" | "filter">("collapse")
const bordered = ref(true)

const steps = ref<ActionNode[]>([
  { id: "action", label: "Action", type: "action" },
  { id: "condition", label: "Condition", type: "condition" },
  {
    id: "screen", label: "Screen", type: "screen",
    children: [
      {
        id: "container-elements", label: "Container 1", type: "container",
        children: [
          { id: "el-button", label: "Button", type: "button" },
          { id: "el-image", label: "Image", type: "image" },
          { id: "el-text", label: "Text", type: "text" },
          { id: "el-title", label: "Title", type: "title" },
          { id: "el-spacer", label: "Spacer", type: "spacer" },
        ],
      },
      {
        id: "container-forms", label: "Container 2", type: "container",
        children: [
          { id: "fm-address", label: "Address input", type: "address-input" },
          { id: "fm-checkbox", label: "Checkbox", type: "checkbox" },
          { id: "fm-checkgroup", label: "Checkbox group", type: "checkbox-group" },
          { id: "fm-country", label: "Country select", type: "country-select" },
          { id: "fm-date", label: "Date", type: "date" },
          { id: "fm-file", label: "File upload", type: "file-upload" },
          { id: "fm-masked", label: "Masked text", type: "masked-text" },
          { id: "fm-multi", label: "Multi select", type: "multi-select" },
          { id: "fm-number", label: "Number input", type: "number-input" },
          { id: "fm-phone", label: "Phone number", type: "phone-number" },
          { id: "fm-radio", label: "Radio group", type: "radio-group" },
          { id: "fm-select", label: "Select", type: "select" },
          { id: "fm-textarea", label: "Text area", type: "text-area" },
          { id: "fm-textinput", label: "Text input", type: "text-input" },
          { id: "fm-url", label: "URL input", type: "url-input" },
        ],
      },
      {
        id: "footer", label: "Footer", type: "footer",
        children: [
          { id: "ft-text", label: "Text", type: "text" },
          { id: "ft-button", label: "Button", type: "button" },
        ],
      },
    ],
  },
  {
    id: "module", label: "Module", type: "module",
    children: [
      { id: "mod-screen-1", label: "Screen 1", type: "screen" },
      { id: "mod-screen-2", label: "Screen 2", type: "screen" },
      { id: "mod-screen-3", label: "Screen 3", type: "screen" },
    ],
  },
])

const search = ref("")
const typeFilters = ref<NodeType[]>([])
const activeItem = ref("screen")

const groupIds = ["screen", "container-elements", "container-forms", "footer", "module"]
const expandAll = (): Record<string, boolean> => Object.fromEntries(groupIds.map((id) => [id, true]))
const expanded = ref<Record<string, boolean>>(expandAll())

function toggle(id: string) {
  expanded.value[id] = !expanded.value[id]
}

// Collapse mode: one button folds every group, or opens them all again
const hasAnyExpanded = computed(() => groupIds.some((id) => expanded.value[id]))
function toggleCollapseAll() {
  expanded.value = hasAnyExpanded.value ? {} : expandAll()
}

// Filter mode: a search or a type filter lists the matching rows flat
const isFlattening = computed(
  () => action.value === "filter" && (typeFilters.value.length > 0 || search.value.length > 0),
)
const visibleSteps = computed(() =>
  isFlattening.value
    ? flattenNodes(steps.value, typeFilters.value, search.value)
    : filterTree(steps.value, search.value),
)
const activeLabel = computed(() => findLabel(steps.value, activeItem.value) || "Screen")

// Switching modes clears the search and the type filters
watch(action, (mode) => {
  typeFilters.value = []
  search.value = ""
  if (mode === "collapse") expanded.value = expandAll()
})

/** The tree, keeping the parents of matching rows */
function filterTree(items: ActionNode[], query: string): ActionNode[] {
  if (!query) return items
  return items.reduce<ActionNode[]>((acc, item) => {
    const match = item.label.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase())
    const children = item.children ? filterTree(item.children, query) : undefined
    const childMatch = !!children && children.length > 0
    if (match || childMatch) acc.push(childMatch ? { ...item, children } : item)
    return acc
  }, [])
}

/** Every row of the checked types (all types when none is) matching the search, as a flat list */
function flattenNodes(items: ActionNode[], types: NodeType[], query: string): ActionNode[] {
  const result: ActionNode[] = []
  const collect = (list: ActionNode[]) => {
    for (const item of list) {
      const matchType = types.length === 0 || types.includes(item.type)
      const matchSearch = !query || item.label.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase())
      if (matchType && matchSearch) result.push(item)
      if (item.children) collect(item.children)
    }
  }
  collect(items)
  return result
}

/** The label of a row anywhere in the tree */
function findLabel(items: ActionNode[], id: string): string {
  for (const item of items) {
    if (item.id === id) return item.label
    if (item.children) {
      const found = findLabel(item.children, id)
      if (found) return found
    }
  }
  return ""
}

// Drag and drop (native HTML5): pressing a row's grip makes the row
// draggable; it drops on the rows of its own list and takes their place.

/** Containers in a screen (not its Footer) and elements reorder; a module's screens do not */
const isSortable = (parent: ActionNode, node: ActionNode) => parent.type !== "module" && node.type !== "footer"

const grabbed = ref<string | null>(null) // the row whose grip is pressed
const dragId = ref<string | null>(null)
const overId = ref<string | null>(null) // the drop target, drawn hovered
let dropIds: string[] = []

/** Moves `activeId` to the place of `targetId` in the list holding both; the Footer never moves */
function reorderTree(items: ActionNode[], activeId: string, targetId: string): ActionNode[] {
  return items.map((item) => {
    if (!item.children) return item
    const from = item.children.findIndex((child) => child.id === activeId)
    const to = item.children.findIndex((child) => child.id === targetId)
    if (from !== -1 && to !== -1) {
      if (item.children[from].type === "footer" || item.children[to].type === "footer") return item
      const children = [...item.children]
      children.splice(to, 0, ...children.splice(from, 1))
      return { ...item, children }
    }
    return { ...item, children: reorderTree(item.children, activeId, targetId) }
  })
}

function startDrag(event: DragEvent, parent: ActionNode, node: ActionNode) {
  dragId.value = node.id
  dropIds = (parent.children ?? []).filter((sibling) => isSortable(parent, sibling)).map((sibling) => sibling.id)
  if (event.dataTransfer) {
    event.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = "move"
    event.dataTransfer.setData("text/plain", node.id)
  }
}

function dragOver(event: DragEvent, id: string) {
  if (!dragId.value || !dropIds.includes(id)) return
  event.preventDefault() // accepts the drop
  overId.value = id === dragId.value ? null : id
}

/** Anywhere else in the sidebar there is no drop target */
function clearDropTarget(event: DragEvent) {
  if (!event.defaultPrevented) overId.value = null
}

function endDrag() {
  grabbed.value = null
  dragId.value = null
  overId.value = null
  dropIds = []
}

function dropOn(event: DragEvent, id: string) {
  if (!dragId.value || !dropIds.includes(id)) return
  event.preventDefault()
  if (id !== dragId.value) steps.value = reorderTree(steps.value, dragId.value, id)
  endDrag()
}

/** Indent each level; leaf rows reserve the disclosure column without adding a control. */
const rowStyle = (depth: number, node: ActionNode) => ({
  paddingLeft: "calc(" + (4 + depth * 24) + "px + " + (node.children ? "0px" : "var(--disclosure-width)") + ")",
})
</script>

<template>
  <component :is="ResponsiveSidebarProvider" :class="['h-full', { 'demo-sidebar-flat': !bordered }]" collapsible="none">
    <SidebarLayout class="h-full">
      <component :is="ResponsiveSidebar" class="!py-4">
        <SidebarHeader>
          <div class="flex items-center gap-2 w-full [&>div:first-child>div]:!p-0">
            <div class="flex-1">
              <SidebarInput
                v-model:value="search"
                size="md"
                :pill="false"
                variant="soft"
                placeholder="Search..."
                @clear="search = ''"
              />
            </div>
            <!-- collapse: folds every group, or opens them all again -->
            <Button
              v-if="action === 'collapse'"
              variant="soft"
              size="md"
              color="secondary"
              :pill="false"
              uniform
              aria-label="Collapse all"
              :aria-pressed="!hasAnyExpanded"
              @click="toggleCollapseAll"
            >
              <ListChevronsDownUp />
            </Button>
            <!-- filter: lists the rows of the checked types -->
            <Menu v-else>
              <MenuTrigger>
                <Button variant="soft" size="md" color="secondary" :pill="false" aria-label="Filter by type" uniform>
                  <FilterBadge v-if="typeFilters.length > 0" />
                  <Filter v-else />
                </Button>
              </MenuTrigger>
              <MenuContent min-width="auto" align="end">
                <MenuCheckboxItem
                  v-for="option in filterOptions"
                  :key="option.value"
                  :checked="typeFilters.includes(option.value)"
                  indicator-position="end"
                  indicator-variant="ghost"
                  @update:checked="(checked) => (typeFilters = checked ? [...typeFilters, option.value] : typeFilters.filter((v) => v !== option.value))"
                  @select="(event) => event.preventDefault()"
                >{{ option.label }}</MenuCheckboxItem>
              </MenuContent>
            </Menu>
          </div>
        </SidebarHeader>
        <SidebarContent @dragover="clearDropTarget">
          <SidebarGroup>
            <SidebarGroupContent>
              <!-- Filter mode, searching or filtering by type: a flat list -->
              <SidebarMenu v-if="isFlattening">
                <SidebarMenuItem v-for="item in visibleSteps" :key="item.id">
                  <SidebarMenuButton :is-active="activeItem === item.id" @click="activeItem = item.id">
                    <SidebarMenuButtonIcon style="--sidebar-icon-size: 16px">
                      <component :is="nodeTypes[item.type].icon" />
                    </SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                    <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>{{ item.label }}</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                  </SidebarMenuButton>
                </SidebarMenuItem>
              </SidebarMenu>

              <!-- Otherwise the tree, narrowed by the search -->
              <SidebarMenu v-else>
                <SidebarMenuItem v-for="item in visibleSteps" :key="item.id" :expanded="!!expanded[item.id]">
                  <div class="group/atp relative flex items-center pl-1">
                    <Button
                      v-if="item.children"
                      variant="ghost" color="secondary" size="xs" uniform
                      class="!size-6 max-md:!size-11 [&_svg]:!size-4"
                      :aria-label="(expanded[item.id] ? 'Collapse ' : 'Expand ') + item.label"
                      :aria-expanded="!!expanded[item.id]"
                      :aria-controls="treeId + '-' + item.id"
                      @click="toggle(item.id)"
                    >
                      <ChevronDownMd aria-hidden="true" :style="{ transform: expanded[item.id] ? 'rotate(0deg)' : 'rotate(-90deg)' }" />
                    </Button>
                    <span v-else class="w-6 max-md:w-11 shrink-0" />
                    <SidebarMenuButton
                      :is-active="activeItem === item.id"
                      class="!w-auto min-w-0 flex-1 group-hover/atp:text-[var(--sidebar-menu-text-active)] group-hover/atp:[&::before]:!opacity-50 group-hover/atp:[&::before]:![transform:scale(1)]"
                      @click="activeItem = item.id"
                    >
                      <span class="flex items-center shrink-0" style="margin-left: 4px">
                        <span
                          class="inline-flex items-center justify-center [&>svg]:w-4 [&>svg]:h-4 relative z-[1]"
                          style="width: 24px; height: 24px; flex-shrink: 0; border-radius: 6px"
                        >
                          <component :is="nodeTypes[item.type].icon" />
                        </span>
                      </span>
                      <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>{{ item.label }}</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                    </SidebarMenuButton>
                    <span
                      class="item-actions flex shrink-0 items-center gap-0.5 opacity-0 pointer-events-none group-hover/atp:opacity-100 group-hover/atp:pointer-events-auto group-focus-within/atp:opacity-100 group-focus-within/atp:pointer-events-auto max-md:opacity-100 max-md:pointer-events-auto"
                    >
                      <Menu>
                        <MenuTrigger>
                          <Button variant="ghost" color="secondary" size="xs" uniform class="!size-6 max-md:!size-11" :aria-label="'Add to ' + item.label"><Plus /></Button>
                        </MenuTrigger>
                        <MenuContent min-width="auto" align="end">
                          <template v-for="(types, index) in addMenu(item)" :key="index">
                            <MenuSeparator v-if="index > 0" />
                            <MenuItem v-for="type in types" :key="type" @select="() => {}">
                              <span class="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-4 h-4 shrink-0 [&>svg]:w-full [&>svg]:h-full"><component :is="nodeTypes[type].icon" /></span> {{ nodeTypes[type].label }}
                            </MenuItem>
                          </template>
                        </MenuContent>
                      </Menu>
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <SidebarMenuSub :id="treeId + '-' + item.id" v-if="item.children" :open="!!expanded[item.id]" has-icons>
                    <SidebarMenuSubItem
                      v-for="child in item.children"
                      :key="child.id"
                      :style="dragId === child.id ? 'opacity: 0.7' : undefined"
                    >
                      <div
                        class="group/ati relative flex items-center [--disclosure-width:24px] max-md:[--disclosure-width:44px]"
                        :style="rowStyle(1, child)"
                        :draggable="grabbed === child.id"
                        @dragstart="startDrag($event, item, child)"
                        @dragover="dragOver($event, child.id)"
                        @drop="dropOn($event, child.id)"
                        @dragend="endDrag"
                      >
                        <Button
                          v-if="child.children"
                          variant="ghost" color="secondary" size="xs" uniform
                          class="!size-6 max-md:!size-11 [&_svg]:!size-4"
                          :aria-label="(expanded[child.id] ? 'Collapse ' : 'Expand ') + child.label"
                          :aria-expanded="!!expanded[child.id]"
                          :aria-controls="treeId + '-' + child.id"
                          @click="toggle(child.id)"
                        >
                          <ChevronDownMd aria-hidden="true" :style="{ transform: expanded[child.id] ? 'rotate(0deg)' : 'rotate(-90deg)' }" />
                        </Button>
                        <SidebarMenuSubButton
                          :indent="1"
                          :is-active="activeItem === child.id"
                          :class="[
                            'gap-0.5 !w-auto min-w-0 flex-1 !h-8 !px-1 !py-0 max-md:!h-11',
                            dragId === child.id || overId === child.id
                              ? 'text-[var(--sidebar-menu-text-active)] [&::before]:!opacity-50 [&::before]:![transform:scale(1)]'
                              : 'group-hover/ati:text-[var(--sidebar-menu-text-active)] group-hover/ati:[&::before]:!opacity-50 group-hover/ati:[&::before]:![transform:scale(1)]',
                          ]"
                          @click="activeItem = child.id"
                        >
                          <span class="flex items-center shrink-0">
                            <span
                              class="inline-flex items-center justify-center [&>svg]:w-4 [&>svg]:h-4 relative z-[1]"
                              style="width: 24px; height: 24px; flex-shrink: 0; border-radius: 6px"
                            >
                              <component :is="nodeTypes[child.type].icon" />
                            </span>
                          </span>
                          <span class="flex-1 min-w-0 truncate text-left relative z-[1]">{{ child.label }}</span>
                        </SidebarMenuSubButton>
                        <!-- Hover actions: + on parents, the drag grip on rows that reorder -->
                        <span
                          v-if="child.children || isSortable(item, child)"
                          class="item-actions flex shrink-0 items-center gap-0.5"
                          :class="
                            dragId === child.id
                              ? 'opacity-100 pointer-events-auto'
                              : 'opacity-0 pointer-events-none group-hover/ati:opacity-100 group-hover/ati:pointer-events-auto group-focus-within/ati:opacity-100 group-focus-within/ati:pointer-events-auto max-md:opacity-100 max-md:pointer-events-auto'
                          "
                        >
                          <Menu v-if="child.children">
                            <MenuTrigger>
                              <Button variant="ghost" color="secondary" size="xs" uniform class="!size-6 max-md:!size-11" :aria-label="'Add to ' + child.label"><Plus /></Button>
                            </MenuTrigger>
                            <MenuContent min-width="auto" align="end">
                              <template v-for="(types, index) in addMenu(child)" :key="index">
                                <MenuSeparator v-if="index > 0" />
                                <MenuItem v-for="type in types" :key="type" @select="() => {}">
                                  <span class="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-4 h-4 shrink-0 [&>svg]:w-full [&>svg]:h-full"><component :is="nodeTypes[type].icon" /></span> {{ nodeTypes[type].label }}
                                </MenuItem>
                              </template>
                            </MenuContent>
                          </Menu>
                          <span
                            v-if="isSortable(item, child)"
                            class="inline-flex size-6 max-md:size-11 items-center justify-center shrink-0 [&>svg]:block [&>svg]:w-4 [&>svg]:h-4 relative z-[1] pointer-events-auto text-[var(--color-text-tertiary)]"
                            style="border-radius: 6px; touch-action: none"
                            :style="{ cursor: dragId === child.id ? 'grabbing' : 'grab' }"
                            @pointerdown="grabbed = child.id"
                            @pointerup="grabbed = null"
                          >
                            <GripVertical />
                          </span>
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <SidebarMenuSub :id="treeId + '-' + child.id" v-if="child.children && dragId !== child.id" :open="!!expanded[child.id]" has-icons>
                        <SidebarMenuSubItem
                          v-for="grandchild in child.children"
                          :key="grandchild.id"
                          :style="dragId === grandchild.id ? 'opacity: 0.7' : undefined"
                        >
                          <div
                            class="group/ati relative flex items-center [--disclosure-width:24px] max-md:[--disclosure-width:44px]"
                            :style="rowStyle(2, grandchild)"
                            :draggable="grabbed === grandchild.id"
                            @dragstart="startDrag($event, child, grandchild)"
                            @dragover="dragOver($event, grandchild.id)"
                            @drop="dropOn($event, grandchild.id)"
                            @dragend="endDrag"
                          >
                            <Button
                              v-if="grandchild.children"
                              variant="ghost" color="secondary" size="xs" uniform
                              class="!size-6 max-md:!size-11 [&_svg]:!size-4"
                              :aria-label="(expanded[grandchild.id] ? 'Collapse ' : 'Expand ') + grandchild.label"
                              :aria-expanded="!!expanded[grandchild.id]"
                              :aria-controls="treeId + '-' + grandchild.id"
                              @click="toggle(grandchild.id)"
                            >
                              <ChevronDownMd aria-hidden="true" :style="{ transform: expanded[grandchild.id] ? 'rotate(0deg)' : 'rotate(-90deg)' }" />
                            </Button>
                            <SidebarMenuSubButton
                              :indent="1"
                              :is-active="activeItem === grandchild.id"
                              :class="[
                                'gap-0.5 !w-auto min-w-0 flex-1 !h-8 !px-1 !py-0 max-md:!h-11',
                                dragId === grandchild.id || overId === grandchild.id
                                  ? 'text-[var(--sidebar-menu-text-active)] [&::before]:!opacity-50 [&::before]:![transform:scale(1)]'
                                  : 'group-hover/ati:text-[var(--sidebar-menu-text-active)] group-hover/ati:[&::before]:!opacity-50 group-hover/ati:[&::before]:![transform:scale(1)]',
                              ]"
                              @click="activeItem = grandchild.id"
                            >
                              <span class="flex items-center shrink-0">
                                <span
                                  class="inline-flex items-center justify-center [&>svg]:w-4 [&>svg]:h-4 relative z-[1]"
                                  style="width: 24px; height: 24px; flex-shrink: 0; border-radius: 6px"
                                >
                                  <component :is="nodeTypes[grandchild.type].icon" />
                                </span>
                              </span>
                              <span class="flex-1 min-w-0 truncate text-left relative z-[1]">{{ grandchild.label }}</span>
                            </SidebarMenuSubButton>
                            <!-- Hover actions: + on parents, the drag grip on rows that reorder -->
                            <span
                              v-if="grandchild.children || isSortable(child, grandchild)"
                              class="item-actions flex shrink-0 items-center gap-0.5"
                              :class="
                                dragId === grandchild.id
                                  ? 'opacity-100 pointer-events-auto'
                                  : 'opacity-0 pointer-events-none group-hover/ati:opacity-100 group-hover/ati:pointer-events-auto group-focus-within/ati:opacity-100 group-focus-within/ati:pointer-events-auto max-md:opacity-100 max-md:pointer-events-auto'
                              "
                            >
                              <Menu v-if="grandchild.children">
                                <MenuTrigger>
                                  <Button variant="ghost" color="secondary" size="xs" uniform class="!size-6 max-md:!size-11" :aria-label="'Add to ' + grandchild.label"><Plus /></Button>
                                </MenuTrigger>
                                <MenuContent min-width="auto" align="end">
                                  <template v-for="(types, index) in addMenu(grandchild)" :key="index">
                                    <MenuSeparator v-if="index > 0" />
                                    <MenuItem v-for="type in types" :key="type" @select="() => {}">
                                      <span class="inline-flex items-center justify-center w-4 h-4 shrink-0 [&>svg]:w-full [&>svg]:h-full"><component :is="nodeTypes[type].icon" /></span> {{ nodeTypes[type].label }}
                                    </MenuItem>
                                  </template>
                                </MenuContent>
                              </Menu>
                              <span
                                v-if="isSortable(child, grandchild)"
                                class="inline-flex size-6 max-md:size-11 items-center justify-center shrink-0 [&>svg]:block [&>svg]:w-4 [&>svg]:h-4 relative z-[1] pointer-events-auto text-[var(--color-text-tertiary)]"
                                style="border-radius: 6px; touch-action: none"
                                :style="{ cursor: dragId === grandchild.id ? 'grabbing' : 'grab' }"
                                @pointerdown="grabbed = grandchild.id"
                                @pointerup="grabbed = null"
                              >
                                <GripVertical />
                              </span>
                            </span>
                          </div>
                        </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                      </SidebarMenuSub>
                    </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                  </SidebarMenuSub>
                </SidebarMenuItem>
              </SidebarMenu>
            </SidebarGroupContent>
          </SidebarGroup>
        </SidebarContent>
      </component>

      <component :is="SidebarDemoPage" :title="activeLabel">
          <p class="text-secondary">
            {{ isFlattening ? 'Filtered results shown as a flat list.' : 'Screen → Container / Footer → Elements. Containers and elements are drag-reorderable; Footer stays pinned.' }}
          </p>
        </component>
    </SidebarLayout>
  </component>
</template>
```

### Mobile Menu
Use Phone preview, then open the menu in the page toolbar. The same navigation tree renders in a desktop rail or a full-screen mobile drawer. Selecting a leaf closes the drawer; expanding a group keeps it open.
```vue
<component :is="ResponsiveSidebarProvider"
  v-slot="sidebar"
  :class="['h-full', { 'demo-sidebar-flat': !bordered }]"
  :collapsible="icons ? 'icon' : 'none'"
>
  <SidebarLayout class="h-full">
    <component :is="ResponsiveSidebar" v-if="!nested">
      <SidebarHeader v-if="!sidebar.isMobile">
        <span class="demo-sidebar-wordmark">App</span>
        <Tooltip v-if="sidebar.collapsible !== 'none'" compact>
          <SidebarTrigger />
          <template #content>
            <span class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
              {{ sidebar.state === 'collapsed' ? 'Expand' : 'Collapse' }}
              <kbd class="console-trigger-kbd">⌘B</kbd>
            </span>
          </template>
        </Tooltip>
      </SidebarHeader>
      <SidebarContent>
        <SidebarGroup v-for="group in navGroups" :key="group.label">
          <SidebarGroupLabel size="sm">{{ group.label }}</SidebarGroupLabel>
          <SidebarGroupContent>
            <SidebarMenu>
              <SidebarMenuItem v-for="item in group.items" :key="item.label">
                <SidebarMenuButton
                  :is-active="active.label === item.label"
                  :tooltip="item.label"
                  @click="active = { id: item.label.toLowerCase(), label: item.label }; sidebar.setOpenMobile(false)"
                >
                  <SidebarMenuButtonIcon v-if="icons"><component :is="item.icon" /></SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                  <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>{{ item.label }}</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                </SidebarMenuButton>
              </SidebarMenuItem>
            </SidebarMenu>
          </SidebarGroupContent>
        </SidebarGroup>
      </SidebarContent>
    </component>
    <component :is="ResponsiveSidebar" v-else-if="icons" style="width: 280px">
      <SidebarContent>
        <SidebarGroup>
          <SidebarGroupContent>
            <SidebarMenu>
              <SidebarMenuItem v-for="section in iconSections" :key="section.id" :expanded="!!expanded[section.id]">
                <SidebarMenuButton @click="expanded[section.id] = !expanded[section.id]">
                  <SidebarMenuButtonIcon><component :is="section.icon" /></SidebarMenuButtonIcon>
                  <SidebarMenuButtonLabel>{{ section.label }}</SidebarMenuButtonLabel>
                  <SidebarMenuChevron />
                </SidebarMenuButton>
                <SidebarMenuSub :open="!!expanded[section.id]" has-icons>
                  <SidebarMenuSubItem v-for="child in section.items" :key="child.id">
                    <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="1" :is-active="active.id === child.id" @click="active = child; sidebar.setOpenMobile(false)">{{ child.label }}</SidebarMenuSubButton>
                  </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                </SidebarMenuSub>
              </SidebarMenuItem>
            </SidebarMenu>
          </SidebarGroupContent>
        </SidebarGroup>
      </SidebarContent>
    </component>
    <component :is="ResponsiveSidebar" v-else variant="docs" style="width: 280px">
      <SidebarContent>
        <SidebarGroup v-for="section in docsSections" :key="section.id">
          <SidebarGroupLabel>{{ section.label }}</SidebarGroupLabel>
          <SidebarGroupContent>
            <SidebarMenu>
              <template v-for="a in section.items" :key="a.id">
                <SidebarMenuItem v-if="a.items" :expanded="!!expanded[a.id]">
                  <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="0" @click="expanded[a.id] = !expanded[a.id]">
                    {{ a.label }}<SidebarMenuChevron />
                  </SidebarMenuSubButton>
                  <SidebarMenuSub :open="!!expanded[a.id]">
                    <template v-for="b in a.items" :key="b.id">
                      <SidebarMenuItem v-if="b.items" :expanded="!!expanded[b.id]">
                        <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="1" @click="expanded[b.id] = !expanded[b.id]">
                          {{ b.label }}<SidebarMenuChevron />
                        </SidebarMenuSubButton>
                        <SidebarMenuSub :open="!!expanded[b.id]">
                          <template v-for="c in b.items" :key="c.id">
                            <SidebarMenuItem v-if="c.items" :expanded="!!expanded[c.id]">
                              <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="2" @click="expanded[c.id] = !expanded[c.id]">
                                {{ c.label }}<SidebarMenuChevron />
                              </SidebarMenuSubButton>
                              <SidebarMenuSub :open="!!expanded[c.id]">
                                <SidebarMenuSubItem v-for="d in c.items" :key="d.id">
                                  <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="3" :is-active="active.id === d.id" @click="active = d; sidebar.setOpenMobile(false)">{{ d.label }}</SidebarMenuSubButton>
                                </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                              </SidebarMenuSub>
                            </SidebarMenuItem>
                            <SidebarMenuSubItem v-else>
                              <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="2" :is-active="active.id === c.id" @click="active = c; sidebar.setOpenMobile(false)">{{ c.label }}</SidebarMenuSubButton>
                            </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                          </template>
                        </SidebarMenuSub>
                      </SidebarMenuItem>
                      <SidebarMenuSubItem v-else>
                        <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="1" :is-active="active.id === b.id" @click="active = b; sidebar.setOpenMobile(false)">{{ b.label }}</SidebarMenuSubButton>
                      </SidebarMenuSubItem>
                    </template>
                  </SidebarMenuSub>
                </SidebarMenuItem>
                <SidebarMenuSubItem v-else>
                  <SidebarMenuSubButton :indent="0" :is-active="active.id === a.id" @click="active = a; sidebar.setOpenMobile(false)">{{ a.label }}</SidebarMenuSubButton>
                </SidebarMenuSubItem>
              </template>
            </SidebarMenu>
          </SidebarGroupContent>
        </SidebarGroup>
      </SidebarContent>
    </component>
    <component :is="SidebarDemoPage" :title="active.label">
      <p class="text-secondary">Open the menu to choose a different page.</p>
    </component>
  </SidebarLayout>
</component>
```

### Search Input
The sidebar provides a dedicated SidebarInput component for search functionality. It forwards everything to Input, so bind it with v-model:value and clear it with @clear.
```vue
<component :is="ResponsiveSidebarProvider" class="h-full" collapsible="none">
  <SidebarLayout class="h-full">
    <component :is="ResponsiveSidebar" variant="docs">
      <SidebarHeader>
        <div style="padding: 12px 0 8px; width: 100%">
          <SidebarInput v-model:value="searchValue" variant="soft" placeholder="Search" @clear="searchValue = ''" />
        </div>
      </SidebarHeader>
      <SidebarContent>
        <SidebarGroup v-for="section in searchSections" :key="section.label">
          <SidebarGroupLabel>{{ section.label }}</SidebarGroupLabel>
          <SidebarGroupContent>
            <SidebarMenu>
              <SidebarMenuItem v-for="item in section.items" :key="section.label + '-' + item">
                <SidebarMenuSubButton
                  :indent="0"
                  :is-active="activeItem === section.label + '-' + item"
                  @click="activeItem = section.label + '-' + item"
                >{{ item }}</SidebarMenuSubButton>
              </SidebarMenuItem>
            </SidebarMenu>
          </SidebarGroupContent>
        </SidebarGroup>
      </SidebarContent>
    </component>
    <component :is="SidebarDemoPage" :title="activeItem">
        <p class="text-secondary">
          Search input stays fixed at the top while sidebar content scrolls.
        </p>
      </component>
  </SidebarLayout>
</component>
```

## Skeleton
Loading placeholders that match the shape of the content they stand in for, from avatars and text to cards, forms, and tables.
Import: import { Skeleton } from "@plexui/vue/components/Skeleton"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/skeleton
Props:
  - circle (boolean) default: false — Render a circular skeleton
  - class (string) — Set dimensions and custom styling

### Overview
Use a circular skeleton to represent loading avatars, combined with text placeholders. Without circle a skeleton keeps its own radius, as for a square picture. Set its size and shape with class.
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref } from 'vue';

const circle = ref(true);
</script>

<template>
<div class="flex items-center gap-4">
  <Skeleton :circle="circle" class="size-12" />
  <div class="flex flex-col gap-2">
    <Skeleton class="h-4 w-[200px]" />
    <Skeleton class="h-4 w-[150px]" />
  </div>
</div>
</template>
```

### Card
Compose multiple skeletons to represent loading cards with images and text content.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-3 w-[300px] p-4 border border-default rounded-xl">
  <Skeleton class="h-[150px] w-full rounded-lg" />
  <Skeleton class="h-4 w-3/4" />
  <Skeleton class="h-4 w-1/2" />
</div>
```

### Text
Multiple text lines with varying widths create natural-looking paragraph placeholders.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2 w-[300px]">
  <Skeleton class="h-4 w-full" />
  <Skeleton class="h-4 w-full" />
  <Skeleton class="h-4 w-4/5" />
</div>
```

### Form
Form skeletons use Plex UI control sizing (32px height for inputs and buttons).
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-4 w-[300px]">
  <div class="flex flex-col gap-2">
    <Skeleton class="h-4 w-[80px]" />
    <Skeleton class="h-8 w-full rounded-lg" />
  </div>
  <div class="flex flex-col gap-2">
    <Skeleton class="h-4 w-[100px]" />
    <Skeleton class="h-8 w-full rounded-lg" />
  </div>
  <Skeleton class="h-8 w-[120px] rounded-lg" />
</div>
```

### Table
Table skeletons show header and row structure while data loads.
```vue
<div class="flex flex-col gap-2 w-full min-w-0 max-w-[400px]">
  <div class="flex gap-4 pb-2 border-b border-default">
    <Skeleton class="h-4 w-[100px]" />
    <Skeleton class="h-4 w-[150px]" />
    <Skeleton class="h-4 w-[80px]" />
  </div>
  <div v-for="i in 4" :key="i" class="flex gap-4 py-2">
    <Skeleton class="h-4 w-[100px]" />
    <Skeleton class="h-4 w-[150px]" />
    <Skeleton class="h-4 w-[80px]" />
  </div>
</div>
```

## Slider
Slider for fine-tuning numeric values with single or range thumbs, marks, an editable value input, and vertical orientation.
Import: import { Slider } from "@plexui/vue/components/Slider"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/slider
Props:
  - value (number | number[]) — Required. Current value; an array when range is set. Bind with v-model:value
  - min (number) — Required. Minimum value
  - max (number) — Required. Maximum value
  - step (number) — Required. Step increment
  - range (boolean) default: false — Enable multi-thumb range mode
  - orientation ("horizontal" | "vertical") default: "horizontal" — Layout direction of the slider
  - reset-value (number) — Value for the reset option (single mode only)
  - reset-tooltip (string) default: "Reset to default" — Tooltip and accessible name of the reset button (single mode only)
  - unit (string) — Unit displayed after the value (e.g. ms, px)
  - prefix-unit (string) — Unit displayed before the value (e.g. $)
  - label (string) — Label text. Also names the slider thumbs for assistive technology
  - label slot (VNode[]) — Rich label content (e.g. a tooltip); takes precedence over the label prop
  - aria-label (string) — Accessible name when there is no visible label. Range thumbs add Minimum / Maximum (or Value n of m)
  - aria-labelledby (string) — Id of an element that names the slider; takes precedence over label
  - marks (Array<{ value: number, label: string }>) — Marks below the track (horizontal only)
  - track-color (string) — Color of the slider track
  - range-color (string) — Color of the filled part of the track
  - min-steps-between-thumbs (number) — Minimum steps between thumbs (range mode only)
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Disable the slider
  - class (string) — Extra classes on the wrapper element
  - @update:value ((value: number | number[]) => void) — Emitted when the value changes; an array when range is set; powers v-model:value
  - @change ((value: number | number[]) => void) — Emitted with update:value, named after the React onChange prop
  - @focus ((event: FocusEvent) => void) — Emitted when the value input or a thumb gains focus
  - @blur ((event: FocusEvent) => void) — Emitted when the value input or a thumb loses focus

### Overview
Set range and bind an array of two numbers with v-model:value for a range, or three or more for several thumbs. min-steps-between-thumbs keeps the thumbs that many steps apart. reset-value, with one thumb, adds a button to the label that brings the value back; a disabled slider hides it. unit follows the value and prefix-unit comes before it. marks label points of the scale under the track, horizontal only. track-color and range-color color the track and its fill. The label slot can carry a tooltip, and orientation="vertical" stands the slider up. On a phone the slider is horizontal, as a list of iOS settings has no vertical slider. A tooltip opens on hover, which a phone does not have, and the cell has no label to hold a reset button, so the Phone preview leaves out those controls. A slider with a label of its own keeps the reset on a phone, as the small round gray button of the iOS 27 kit 8px before the value: a 28px circle that takes touches 44px around it. Marks take the place of the value, so with marks the phone shows no unit either.
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref } from 'vue';
import { FieldGroup } from '@plexui/vue/components/FieldGroup';
import { Slider } from '@plexui/vue/components/Slider';
import { Tooltip, TooltipTriggerDecorator } from '@plexui/vue/components/Tooltip';

const scaleMarks = [0, 25, 50, 75, 100].map((value) => ({ value, label: String(value) }));
const thumbs = ref<"one" | "two" | "three">("one");
const values = ref<{ one: number; two: number[]; three: number[] }>({ one: 50, two: [25, 75], three: [10, 40, 70] });
const minStepsBetweenThumbs = ref(5); // with two or three thumbs
const resetValue = ref(true); // with one thumb, while enabled
const unit = ref<"none" | "unit" | "prefixUnit">("unit");
const marks = ref(false); // horizontal only
const colors = ref(false);
const tooltip = ref(false);
const orientation = ref<"horizontal" | "vertical">("horizontal");
const disabled = ref(false);
</script>

<template>
<!-- Desktop -->
<div :style="orientation === 'horizontal' ? { width: '300px' } : undefined">
  <Slider
    :range="thumbs !== 'one'"
    label="Example field"
    :min="0"
    :max="100"
    :step="1"
    v-model:value="values[thumbs]"
    :min-steps-between-thumbs="thumbs === 'one' ? undefined : minStepsBetweenThumbs"
    :reset-value="thumbs === 'one' && resetValue ? 50 : undefined"
    :unit="unit === 'unit' ? '%' : undefined"
    :prefix-unit="unit === 'prefixUnit' ? '$' : undefined"
    :marks="marks ? scaleMarks : undefined"
    :track-color="colors ? '#61C454' : undefined"
    :range-color="colors ? '#EF4146' : undefined"
    :orientation="orientation"
    :disabled="disabled"
  >
    <template v-if="tooltip" #label>
      <Tooltip content="More details about this field">
        <TooltipTriggerDecorator>Example field</TooltipTriggerDecorator>
      </Tooltip>
    </template>
  </Slider>
</div>

<!-- Phone: the group's title names the slider; one value ends the cell, a range goes under the track, and marks show the scale in its place. The phone slider is horizontal, with no reset button or tooltip -->
<FieldGroup title="Example field">
  <div v-if="thumbs === 'one' && !marks" data-row class="flex min-h-[var(--field-row-min-height)] items-center gap-3 px-[var(--field-row-padding-inline)]">
    <Slider
      aria-label="Example field"
      :min="0"
      :max="100"
      :step="1"
      v-model:value="values.one"
      :track-color="colors ? '#61C454' : undefined"
      :range-color="colors ? '#EF4146' : undefined"
      :disabled="disabled"
      class="min-w-0 flex-1"
    />
    <span :class="['min-w-[4ch] shrink-0 text-end text-[length:var(--field-row-label-font-size)] leading-[var(--field-row-label-line-height)] tabular-nums', disabled ? 'text-[var(--mobile-label-tertiary,var(--color-text-disabled))]' : 'text-secondary']">{{ (unit === 'prefixUnit' ? '$' : '') + values.one + (unit === 'unit' ? '%' : '') }}</span>
  </div>
  <div v-else data-row class="flex flex-col px-[var(--field-row-padding-inline)] pb-1 [--slider-control-height:44px]">
    <Slider
      :range="thumbs !== 'one'"
      aria-label="Example field"
      :min="0"
      :max="100"
      :step="1"
      v-model:value="values[thumbs]"
      :min-steps-between-thumbs="thumbs === 'one' ? undefined : minStepsBetweenThumbs"
      :marks="marks ? scaleMarks : undefined"
      :track-color="colors ? '#61C454' : undefined"
      :range-color="colors ? '#EF4146' : undefined"
      :disabled="disabled"
    />
    <span v-if="!marks" :class="['text-center text-[length:var(--field-row-description-font-size)] leading-[var(--field-row-description-line-height)] tabular-nums', disabled ? 'text-[var(--mobile-label-tertiary,var(--color-text-disabled))]' : '']">{{ [values[thumbs]].flat().map((value) => (unit === 'prefixUnit' ? '$' : '') + value + (unit === 'unit' ? '%' : '')).join(' – ') }}</span>
  </div>
</FieldGroup>
</template>
```

## Sortable
"Reorders a list by dragging: rows of a group, table rows, images, tags and cards. The order changes on the drop."
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/sortable
Props:
  - value (unknown[]) — The list, in the order of the children. Bind with v-model:value
  - layout ('list' | 'grid') default: list — list for rows one under another, grid for items that stand side by side
  - as (string | Component) default: div — Element to render, such as TableBody for table rows
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Turns dragging off
  - handles (boolean) default: true in a table, otherwise false — Shows the grips of SortableHandle. A hidden grip still takes the keyboard, and the item drags from anywhere but a control
  - group (string) — Lists of one group trade items, and a SortableTarget of that group takes them. Give each list a name
  - name (string) — Names the list in move
  - aria-label (string) default: Reorder — Name of the handle for assistive technology

### Overview
Every direct child is an item, in the order of value. A row drags from anywhere but a control, so the switch keeps working. Its grip is hidden and still takes the keyboard: Tab to the row and press the arrow keys. On a touch screen a row lifts after a short hold, and a swipe still scrolls the page. Esc cancels a drag. A wrapper with data-row keeps the lines of the group, and rows that touch hold still: the line alone marks the gap. On a phone the rows sit on the grouped background of an iOS settings screen. A row that is mostly a field, such as an Input, needs a grip: handles shows it, and then only the grip drags the row. On a phone with touch.css the grip ends the row, after its value, as three flat lines 21px wide in the tertiary label color, where and as iOS 27.2 draws its reorder control; it stays first in the reading order. A grip drags at once, so on a phone show it while the list is being edited, as iOS shows its reorder controls only in Edit mode. disabled turns dragging off.
```vue
<script setup lang="ts">
import { ref } from 'vue';

const rows = ref(sections);
const handles = ref(false);
const disabled = ref(false);
</script>

<template>
<div class="w-[480px] max-w-full">
  <FieldGroup title="Home screen">
    <Sortable v-model:value="rows" :handles="handles" :disabled="disabled">
      <div v-for="row in rows" :key="row.id" data-row>
        <SortableHandle :aria-label="'Reorder ' + row.label" />
        <Field row :label="row.label" :description="row.description">
          <Switch default-checked />
        </Field>
      </div>
    </Sortable>
  </FieldGroup>
</div>
</template>
```

### Stacked rows
A long value under its label sorts the same way.
```vue
<div class="w-[480px] max-w-full">
  <FieldGroup title="Questions">
    <Sortable v-model:value="rows">
      <div v-for="row in rows" :key="row.id" data-row>
        <SortableHandle :aria-label="'Reorder ' + row.question" />
        <Field row orientation="vertical" :label="row.question">
          <Textarea variant="ghost" :rows="1" auto-resize :default-value="row.answer" />
        </Field>
      </div>
    </Sortable>
  </FieldGroup>
</div>
```

### Different controls
The kind of control does not matter: each row keeps its state when it moves.
```vue
<div class="w-[480px] max-w-full">
  <FieldGroup title="Preferences">
    <Sortable v-model:value="rows">
      <div v-for="row in rows" :key="row.id" data-row>
        <SortableHandle :aria-label="'Reorder ' + row.label" />
        <Field row :label="row.label">
          <Select
            v-if="row.id === 'language'"
            v-model:value="language"
            :options="languages"
            variant="soft"
            :block="false"
            align="end"
          />
          <SegmentedControl v-else-if="row.id === 'theme'" v-model:value="theme" aria-label="Theme">
            <SegmentedControlOption value="light">Light</SegmentedControlOption>
            <SegmentedControlOption value="dark">Dark</SegmentedControlOption>
          </SegmentedControl>
          <DatePicker
            v-else-if="row.id === 'start'"
            v-model:value="start"
            trigger-date-format="MMM d, yyyy"
            placeholder="Select date"
            variant="soft"
          />
          <Switch v-else-if="row.id === 'summary'" default-checked />
          <Input v-else variant="ghost" default-value="Weekly report" />
        </Field>
      </div>
    </Sortable>
  </FieldGroup>
</div>
```

### Table
Pass :as="TableBody" and the rows sort inside the table. Columns keep their width. Grips show here without handles.
```vue
<div class="w-[640px] max-w-full">
  <Table>
    <TableHeader>
      <TableRow>
        <TableHead class="w-10" aria-label="Order" />
        <TableHead>Product</TableHead>
        <TableHead>SKU</TableHead>
        <TableHead>Stock</TableHead>
        <TableHead class="text-end">Price</TableHead>
      </TableRow>
    </TableHeader>
    <Sortable :as="TableBody" v-model:value="rows">
      <TableRow v-for="row in rows" :key="row.id">
        <TableCell><SortableHandle :aria-label="'Reorder ' + row.name" /></TableCell>
        <TableCell>{{ row.name }}</TableCell>
        <TableCell>{{ row.sku }}</TableCell>
        <TableCell>{{ row.stock }}</TableCell>
        <TableCell class="text-end">{{ row.price }}</TableCell>
      </TableRow>
    </Sortable>
  </Table>
</div>
```

### Images
layout="grid" for items side by side: the line stands between two items, and the chip shows the picture. With tabindex an item takes the arrow keys itself.
```vue
<Sortable v-model:value="pictures" layout="grid" class="grid w-[420px] max-w-full grid-cols-3 gap-3">
  <div
    v-for="picture in pictures"
    :key="picture.id"
    tabindex="0"
    class="aspect-square overflow-hidden rounded-xl"
  >
    <img :src="picture.src" :alt="picture.alt" class="block size-full object-cover" />
  </div>
</Sortable>
```

### Tags
The grid layout also fits a line that wraps: the end of one line and the start of the next are separate places to land.
```vue
<Sortable v-model:value="tags" layout="grid" class="flex w-[420px] max-w-full flex-wrap gap-2">
  <div v-for="tag in tags" :key="tag" tabindex="0" class="rounded-full">
    <Badge color="secondary" size="lg" pill>{{ tag }}</Badge>
  </div>
</Sortable>
```

### Cards
Any element can be an item, such as a StatsCard.
```vue
<Sortable v-model:value="cards" layout="grid" class="grid w-[480px] max-w-full grid-cols-2 gap-3">
  <div v-for="card in cards" :key="card.label" tabindex="0" class="rounded-xl">
    <StatsCard :label="card.label" :value="card.value" />
  </div>
</Sortable>
```

### Between lists
Lists of one group trade items, and an empty list takes a drop. Each list has a name. move fires for every move, also inside one list, and the handler updates both lists. Past the end of a list the arrow keys go on to the next one. A data-sortable-static child, such as a head or a message, stays out of the items.
```vue
<div class="grid w-[640px] max-w-full gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
  <div v-for="(tasks, name) in lists" :key="name">
    <p class="mb-2 text-sm font-medium text-primary">{{ name }}</p>
    <Sortable group="tasks" :name="name" :value="tasks" class="rounded-xl border border-default" @move="move">
      <div v-for="task in tasks" :key="task" class="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-primary">{{ task }}</div>
      <p v-if="!tasks.length" class="px-4 py-3 text-sm text-secondary" data-sortable-static>Nothing here</p>
    </Sortable>
  </div>
</div>
```

## Stats Card
Stats card for dashboards displaying KPIs with trend deltas, an info tooltip, an icon, a progress bar, and an accent bar.
Import: import { StatsCard } from "@plexui/vue/components/StatsCard"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/stats-card
Props:
  - label (string) — Required. Label describing the metric
  - value (string | number) — The primary metric value
  - value slot (VNode[]) — Rich value content; takes precedence over the value prop
  - description (string) — Subtitle below the value
  - trend ({ value: number; label?: string }) — Trend indicator with percentage
  - invert-trend (boolean) default: false — Invert trend colors (positive = bad)
  - trend-position ("value" | "header") default: "value" — Where the trend indicator is rendered
  - trend-variant ("text" | "badge") default: "text" — Render trend as text or badge
  - size ("default" | "sm") default: "default" — Card size; sm for compact analytics layouts
  - icon slot (VNode[]) — Icon in a small soft tile at the end of the header
  - sparkline slot (VNode[]) — Chart rendered at the bottom
  - progress (number) — Progress bar value (0-100)
  - progress-label (string) — Label after the progress bar
  - tooltip (string) — Shows an info icon next to the label with tooltip text
  - variant ("default" | "accent") default: "default" — Visual variant
  - accent-color ("primary" | "secondary" | "danger" | "info" | "discovery" | "success" | "caution" | "warning") default: "primary" — Color of the accent bar (accent variant)
  - class (string) — Extra classes on the card element

### Overview
Dashboard-ready stats cards with trends, icons, and progress bars. Use size="sm" for compact analytics rows with denser spacing and smaller typography; it pairs well with tooltip, a string that adds an info icon next to the label. The trend sits under the value with its label, or right-aligned in the header row (trend-position), as plain text or in a badge (trend-variant). Use invert-trend for metrics where a rise is bad, such as error rate or response time. An icon in the icon slot stands in a small soft tile at the end of the header, and progress draws a bar of completion or usage across the card. The accent variant adds a colored bar before the content in one of eight semantic colors (accent-color).
```vue
<div style="max-width: 320px; width: 100%" class="mx-auto">
  <StatsCard
    label="Total Revenue"
    value="$45,231"
    :size="size"
    :variant="variant"
    :accent-color="accentColor"
    :trend-position="trendPosition"
    :trend-variant="trendVariant"
    :trend="trend ? { value: 12.5, label: 'vs last month' } : undefined"
    :invert-trend="invertTrend"
    :tooltip="tooltip ? 'Sum of all revenue streams' : undefined"
    :progress="progress ? 72 : undefined"
    :progress-label="progress ? '72%' : undefined"
    :description="progress ? 'of $62,800 goal' : undefined"
  >
    <template v-if="icon" #icon><DollarCircle /></template>
  </StatsCard>
</div>
```

## Stepper
Two segments that decrease and increase a number in steps, within a range or wrapping around, as the iOS 26 stepper does.
Import: import { Stepper } from "@plexui/vue/components/Stepper"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/stepper
Props:
  - value (number) — Controlled value; bind with v-model:value
  - default-value (number) default: min — Initial value (uncontrolled)
  - min (number) default: 0 — Lowest value
  - max (number) default: 100 — Highest value
  - step (number) default: 1 — Amount each step adds or removes
  - wrap (boolean) default: false — A step past max continues from min, and one below min from max
  - auto-repeat (boolean) default: true — Holding a segment keeps stepping: after 400ms, every 100ms
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Prevent interaction and focus
  - pill (boolean) default: true — Fully rounded ends
  - decrement-label (string) default: "Decrease" — Accessible name of the minus segment
  - increment-label (string) default: "Increase" — Accessible name of the plus segment
  - name (string) — Submits the value with the owning form through a hidden input
  - aria-label (string) — Name of the value, when no visible label names it
  - aria-labelledby (string) — The id of the visible label of the value
  - aria-describedby (string) — The id of an element that describes the stepper
  - id (string) — The id of the stepper

### Overview
Put a stepper in a Field row and name its value in the row's label, such as 3 copies: the stepper shows no value of its own. At the lowest value the minus turns tertiary and stops stepping, at the highest the plus, as 1 copy shows; the segment keeps its fill, as in iOS. Holding a segment keeps stepping, as a native stepper does. A disabled stepper takes no focus and shows both symbols tertiary. On a desktop set :pill="false" for square ends, as other controls in a dense layout have; on a phone the stepper stays the capsule of iOS, so the Phone preview does not offer it. On a phone the rows sit on the grouped background of an iOS settings screen.
```vue
<FieldGroup class="w-full max-w-[400px]">
  <template #footer>Up to 20 copies and 14 nights.</template>
  <Field row :label="copies === 1 ? '1 copy' : copies + ' copies'">
    <Stepper v-model:value="copies" :min="1" :max="20" :disabled="disabled" :pill="pill" />
  </Field>
  <Field row :label="nights === 1 ? '1 night' : nights + ' nights'">
    <Stepper v-model:value="nights" :min="1" :max="14" :disabled="disabled" :pill="pill" />
  </Field>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Range, step and wrap
Decimal steps add without rounding drift. With wrap a step past max continues from min, so the hour goes from 23 to 0, and neither segment stops; turn it off and the plus stops at 23.
```vue
<div class="flex w-full flex-col items-center gap-6">
  <FieldGroup class="w-full max-w-[400px]">
    <template #footer>Steps of 10% from 0% to 100%.</template>
    <Field row :label="'Opacity: ' + Math.round(opacity * 100) + '%'">
      <Stepper v-model:value="opacity" :min="0" :max="1" :step="0.1" />
    </Field>
  </FieldGroup>
  <FieldGroup class="w-full max-w-[400px]">
    <template #footer>{{ wrap ? 'Past 23:00 the hour goes on from 00:00, and neither segment stops.' : 'The hour stops at 00:00 and 23:00, where a segment turns tertiary.' }}</template>
    <Field row :label="'Hour: ' + String(hour).padStart(2, '0') + ':00'">
      <Stepper v-model:value="hour" :min="0" :max="23" :wrap="wrap" />
    </Field>
  </FieldGroup>
</div>
```

## Swipe Actions
"Actions behind a row of a list, as the swipe actions of an iOS list: a swipe across the row reveals them, a full swipe runs the first one, and each is a button for the keys."
Import: import { SwipeActions } from "@plexui/vue/components/SwipeActions"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/swipe-actions
Props:
  - leading (SwipeAction[]) default: [] — Actions revealed by a swipe toward the trailing edge, the first one at the leading edge
  - trailing (SwipeAction[]) default: [] — Actions revealed by a swipe toward the leading edge, the first one at the trailing edge
  - label-placement ("hidden" | "inside" | "below") default: "hidden" — Where the actions show their labels: symbols only, inside the capsule, or below it (for rows of 60px and taller, as iOS sets them)
  - full-swipe (boolean) default: true — A swipe past the middle between the open actions and the far edge runs the first action of that side
  SwipeAction:
    - id (string) — Unique ID of the action within its side
    - label (string) — Name of the action: shown inside or below the capsule (and on an action without an icon), and its accessible name always
    - Icon (Component) — Icon of the action, a component (mark it raw in reactive state)
    - color ("primary" | "secondary" | "danger" | "success" | "info" | "discovery" | "caution" | "warning") default: "secondary" — Solid color of the action
    - onSelect (() => void) — Called when the action is pressed, or by a full swipe when it is the first action of its side

### Overview
Wrap the content of a row of a FieldGroup: the swipe row is the data-row of the group, so its separators and corners follow the card. The actions are solid capsules behind the content, 10px apart, 10px from the edge and 4px inside the top and bottom of the row, as iOS 27.2 sets them; the first action of a side stands at its edge. A symbol alone is a capsule of 60px that grows with the row and is a 50px circle from a row of 58px. Swiped aside, the content rounds its corners by 26px and, on a phone, turns soft gray on the background of the list, as iOS fills it. A swipe past half of the actions opens them, a press on an action runs it and closes the row, and a press on the content or anywhere outside closes it too; one row is open at a time. A swipe past the middle between the open actions and the far edge stretches the first action across the row, and letting go runs it.
```vue
<FieldGroup>
  <SwipeActions
    v-for="list in lists"
    :key="list.name"
    :leading="[{ id: 'pin', label: 'Pin', Icon: Pin, color: 'warning', onSelect: () => pin(list) }]"
    :trailing="[
      { id: 'delete', label: 'Delete', Icon: RemoveTrash, color: 'danger', onSelect: () => remove(list) },
      { id: 'archive', label: 'Archive', Icon: Archive, color: 'info', onSelect: () => archive(list) },
    ]"
  >
    <div class="row">{{ list.name }}</div>
  </SwipeActions>
</FieldGroup>
```

### Labels inside
With label-placement=&quot;inside&quot; each capsule holds a smaller symbol and its label, and every action of a side takes the width of the widest, as iOS 27.2 sets them. An action without an icon shows its label alone, in a rounded rectangle: beside labels inside it takes the width of the widest too, and elsewhere it is as wide as its text.
```vue
<SwipeActions
  label-placement="inside"
  :leading="[{ id: 'pin', label: 'Pin', Icon: Pin, color: 'warning', onSelect: () => pin(list) }]"
  :trailing="[
    { id: 'delete', label: 'Delete', Icon: RemoveTrash, color: 'danger', onSelect: () => remove(list) },
    { id: 'archive', label: 'Archive', Icon: Archive, color: 'info', onSelect: () => archive(list) },
  ]"
>
  <div class="row">{{ list.name }}</div>
</SwipeActions>
```

### Labels below
With label-placement=&quot;below&quot; the label stands under the capsule, which takes the rest of the row: iOS 27.2 sets its labels this way from a row of 60px, such as a row of two lines, and draws a 50px circle from a row of 78px.
```vue
<SwipeActions
  label-placement="below"
  :leading="[{ id: 'flag', label: 'Flag', Icon: Flag, color: 'warning', onSelect: () => flag(message) }]"
  :trailing="[
    { id: 'delete', label: 'Delete', Icon: RemoveTrash, color: 'danger', onSelect: () => remove(message) },
    { id: 'archive', label: 'Archive', Icon: Archive, color: 'info', onSelect: () => archive(message) },
  ]"
>
  <div class="row">
    <div>{{ message.from }}</div>
    <div>{{ message.subject }}</div>
  </div>
</SwipeActions>
```

## Switch
Toggle switch for turning settings on or off, with an optional label, a supporting description linked for screen readers, and a configurable label position.
Import: import { Switch } from "@plexui/vue/components/Switch"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/switch
Props:
  - checked / v-model:checked (boolean) — Controlled state
  - default-checked (boolean) — Initial state (uncontrolled)
  - label (string) — Accessible label rendered next to the switch
  - label slot (VNode[]) — Rich label content; takes precedence over the label prop
  - description (string) — Secondary text below the label, linked via aria-describedby
  - description slot (VNode[]) — Rich description content; takes precedence over the description prop
  - label-position ("start" | "end") default: "end" — Label on the left (start) or right (end)
  - color ("primary" | "accent") default: "primary" — Checked track color. Accent reads --switch-accent-color, with platform green as its fallback. Disabled checked controls retain a muted accent.
  - disabled (boolean) default: false — Prevent interaction
  - required (boolean) — Required for form submission
  - name (string) — Form field name
  - value (string) default: "on" — Value when submitted
  - class (string) — CSS classes applied to the wrapper node
  - id (string) — The id of the switch element (generated when omitted)
  - @update:checked ((next: boolean) => void) — Emitted with the next state when toggled
  - @focus ((event: FocusEvent) => void) — Emitted when the switch gains focus
  - @blur ((event: FocusEvent) => void) — Emitted when the switch loses focus

### Overview
One setting on and one off. Click either the label or the control. Choose accent for the configured application accent (platform green by default), or primary for the default Switch style. A description is linked with aria-describedby. On a desktop label-position sets the label before or after the switch; on a phone the label stays at the start of its row and the switch trails it, so the Phone preview does not offer it. disabled keeps a switch as it is: on retains a muted accent, off uses the neutral track. Use default-checked for an initial value and name for form submission.
```vue
<!-- Desktop -->
<div class="flex w-full max-w-sm flex-col gap-6">
  <Switch :color="color" name="notifications" label="Notifications" :description="description ? 'Messages and mentions.' : undefined" :label-position="labelPosition" default-checked :disabled="disabled" />
  <Switch :color="color" name="summary" label="Weekly summary" :description="description ? 'Sent on Mondays.' : undefined" :label-position="labelPosition" :disabled="disabled" />
</div>

<!-- Phone: each switch is the trailing control of a row; Field associates its label and description with it -->
<div data-phone-rows class="w-full grow bg-[var(--mobile-surface-grouped-primary)] px-4 py-6 [--field-group-background:var(--mobile-surface-grouped-secondary)]">
  <FieldGroup>
    <Field row label="Notifications" :description="description ? 'Messages and mentions.' : undefined">
      <Switch :color="color" name="notifications" default-checked :disabled="disabled" />
    </Field>
    <Field row label="Weekly summary" :description="description ? 'Sent on Mondays.' : undefined">
      <Switch :color="color" name="summary" :disabled="disabled" />
    </Field>
  </FieldGroup>
</div>
```

### Controlled state
Change checked above or toggle the setting. Both update the same state. Use v-model:checked when your application owns the value.
```vue
<script setup>
import { ref } from 'vue'
const checked = ref(false)
</script>

<!-- Desktop -->
<Switch color="accent" label="Airplane mode" v-model:checked="checked" />

<!-- Phone -->
<div data-phone-rows class="w-full grow bg-[var(--mobile-surface-grouped-primary)] px-4 py-6 [--field-group-background:var(--mobile-surface-grouped-secondary)]">
  <FieldGroup><Field row label="Airplane mode"><Switch color="accent" v-model:checked="checked" /></Field></FieldGroup>
</div>
```

### Grouped settings
Group related settings with FieldGroup. Use a row description to explain a locked setting and a footer for help that applies to the group. Phone shows the same groups in a full Page with navigation.
```vue
<!-- Place these groups inside PageContent on Phone. -->
<div class="flex w-full max-w-lg flex-col gap-6"><FieldGroup title="Notifications">
  <Field row label="Push notifications"><Switch color="accent" default-checked /></Field>
  <Field row label="Product updates" description="A few emails a month."><Switch color="accent" /></Field>
</FieldGroup>
<FieldGroup title="Privacy">
  <Field row label="Share usage data"><Switch color="accent" /></Field>
  <Field row label="Security alerts" description="Required for your account."><Switch color="accent" default-checked disabled /></Field>
  <template #footer>You can change your data sharing preference at any time.</template>
</FieldGroup></div>
```

## Tab Bar
"The tab bar of a phone screen: two to five sections at the bottom in a floating glass capsule, an optional search or prominent tab, minimizing while the content scrolls. Desktop layouts navigate with a Sidebar or Tabs, so a tab bar never renders from 768px up."
Import: import { TabBar, TabBarItem } from "@plexui/vue/components/TabBar"
Docs: https://plexui.com/vue/components/tab-bar
Props:
  - value (string) — Controlled value of the selected tab; bind with v-model:value
  - default-value (string) — Selected tab (uncontrolled)
  - search ({ value: string; label?: string }) — A search tab in a glass circle of its own at the trailing end; label defaults to Search
